| NEW tests/p5/integration/test_search_elasticsearch.py, test_search_publication.py | Explicit isolated ES namespace and M2 PG fixtures, real combined pipeline/outage/cleanup/restart | No fallback application DB/index; existing M1 fixtures untouched |
| NEW tests/p5/benchmarks/test_search_capacity.py | Reproducible projection/index/readback/PIT/cancellation/retention costs and parent/process memory attribution | Measurements, not invented defaults or frozen threshold changes |
| MODIFY PROJECT_CONTEXT.md | Detailed plan, gates and actual execution/approval ledger only | Planning status stays unimplemented until execution evidence exists |

No standalone plan/ADR, embedding.py, worker task/queue, M1 lifecycle file, P1–P4 domain file, frozen contract, Express/frontend or CI modification is authorized by this proposal. If selected ES sealing needs different files or a frozen interface change, return for change control rather than silently expanding inventory.

### 7.10 Three-batch implementation sequence and gates

**Batch 1 — C1 foundation, deterministic projection and compatibility/capacity/sealing spike.** Start only after explicit B1 authorization, including an approved disposable experiment environment; none is authorized by this document. Create model/contracts/projection and focused tests; prototype only M2 adapter/mapping/seal operations in that isolated environment. Repository dependency/compose/settings/.env changes must wait for owner approval of the tested exact pair/deployment configuration. Inspect current official supported ES/client versions, Python 3.14/Linux install/TLS/auth/PIT/bulk/cancellation/license/maintenance, then report exact candidate pair and immutable image/client pins for owner approval. Do not call a literature recommendation a passed compatibility test. Compare shared-index versus per-generation exclusion/cost and execute CREATE → INDEX → VERIFY → DRAIN/SEAL → INDEPENDENT READBACK → PREPARE proof without production acceptance. Measure empty/representative/canonical-boundary records (up to 25k entities/50k relationships/75k evidence/10k unresolved/5k conflicts and 128MiB canonical source), noisy metadata/Unicode/long identity, concurrent projects and staged generations. Canonical source limits stay frozen; expanded ES record/payload capacities require new measurements. Record CPU/current RSS/workload peak/shard and disk overhead, bulk errors, readback speed, cancellation completion and pin/PIT costs. Proposed trial grid (test-only, not defaults): bulk 100/500/1000 docs × 1/5/10MiB, concurrency 1/2/4, pages 25/50/100 and whole-operation trials 1/2/5s; abort within explicit test-environment budget. ES heap/CPU/disk/container policy must be explicit for each run. Use §7.12.1's compatibility/measurement matrix and §7.12.3's M3/M6 fixture review; no M3/M6 implementation.

B1 exit report: deterministic complete mappings/hashes with no secret/source bytes; C1 golden producer/consumer fixtures reviewed by M3/M6; real seal/drain/late-write/credential proof, measured resource envelope and supported package/deployment recommendation. An absent/failed seal proof is a BLOCKED exit report, not B1 technical validation or B2 entry. One consolidated owner checkpoint approves physical-layout amendment/protocol, tested pair/deployment, C1 freeze and measured affected limits, plus initial semantic acceptance. No silent production defaults or production registration. **B1 DoD:** exact experiment/environment/results/limitations and approval matrix recorded. B2 remains blocked until both actual proof and owner approval exist; signing a design cannot substitute for proof.

**Batch 2 — Durable candidate indexing, verification and atomic M1 publication.** Hard dependency: B1 C1/package/deployment/layout/capacity approvals AND actual physical exclusion/readback proof. Implement M2 migration/resource/generation/pin metadata, approved physical generation design, bounded writer/verifier, SearchWorkHandler and guarded publication/readiness; activate no candidate based on an unproven seal. Wire only approved shared registry composition. Test M1 claim → M2 index/provisional verify/drain+seal/independent readback/prepare → M1 atomic accept, crash/expiry/rollback before/after fence, invalid prepared/accepted copies, historical readiness, foreign attempts, duplicate messages, partial bulk/unknown outcomes, index disappearance/recreation and stale publisher after cleanup marking. Demonstrate old accepted generation remains readable throughout failed candidate work. Completed-work integration depends separately on owner-authorized, independently reviewed M1 extension (§7.12.2); do not edit frozen M1 as ordinary B2 work. Without that extension, use legitimate M1 fixtures for producer-publication proof, report completed-rebuild unavailable and retain its acceptance blocker.

B2 acceptance/DoD: no missing/extra/tampered/late-written record can publish; physical seal proof and all SQL receipt/pointer/completion changes atomic; zero external I/O inside publication; real PG+ES+C0 conformance including accepted-reference-only reads; no duplicate lifecycle or M1 private access; migration isolated and rollback verified. Production dispatch remains off absent separate fleet/policy acceptance.

**Batch 3 — Bounded lexical reads, freshness, safe cleanup and downstream handoff.** Depends on accepted B2 and frozen query/rank/cursor/capability/status/read-pin contracts plus approved affected limits. Completed-work rebuild/storage-loss repair requires its own approved AND implemented/independently verified M1 public extension, not an assumed B3 entitlement; profile-change reprojection remains explicitly outside that narrow amendment. Implement internal SearchReader, scoped authorization/filtering, generation-bound HMAC/PIT pagination, final pointer check, read pins, status, thin FastAPI routes and real outage/reader/cleanup tests. Implement orphan cleanup only after approved grace + proven seal/reader exclusion; published/historical deletion disabled. Expose typed rebuild capability/status as unavailable until approved integration exists; module cannot claim full rebuild/recovery acceptance on that basis. Provide C1 fixtures to M3/M6, no actual M3/Express/UI work. Semantic/hybrid follows the explicit initial-P5 scope decision in §7.12.3; if required, completion is blocked until approved provider/profile and real acceptance exist. Disabled semantic is never a substitute for that decision.

B3 acceptance/DoD: exact identifiers first, relevance fixtures stable, all scopes filtered before scoring, no active-generation continuation drift, bounded full-operation/cancellation behavior, historical STALE readable, rebuild failure preserves prior accepted data, no secrets/raw DSL/snippets, public errors/roles correct and no diagram/report dependence on ES. Run full relevant quality/regression gates and independently review/freeze C1/M2 only with owner acceptance. A blocked mandatory successful-version rebuild or unproven sealing protocol prevents declaring M2 complete; remaining later producer/production deployment gates are separately classified.

### 7.11 Tests, failure classification, observability and final acceptance

Required unit/contract cases: input permutations/golden UTF-8 hashes; preserved canonical statuses/provenance/ranges/optional fact keys; empty models, all record kinds, metadata allowlists and explicit omissions; capacity/no truncation; exact C0 profile/candidate/mismatch/stale version rejection; C1 M3/M6 fixture compatibility; exact-ID ranking, hostile literal queries, bad filters, empty results, unsupported semantic; tampered/expired/wrong-user/project/version/query/index/generation cursors; observed-current unknown versus historical freshness; structured API errors and delegated roles.

Real-service proofs: every bulk item checked; duplicate-create exact equality; missing/extra/mis-scoped/duplicate-reference/payload/scope/schema/digest/direction/endpoint failures; partial shards/timeout/PIT expiry/physical UUID replacement; late bulk after seal, post-seal mutation attempts, disk-full/unavailable/restart/unknown acknowledgement; guarded Session lifecycle poison and outer rollback; expired/stale M1 attempt cannot accept; concurrent publishers/readers/cleanup; read-pin expiry with outstanding query; no accepted/historical deletion; retry/idempotent orphan cleanup and crash after deletion mark; ES outage cannot affect diagrams/report eligibility or canonical publication. M1 pytest fixtures prove SQL coordination only; real ES tests prove actual ES behavior and must report skips as unverified.

Failures: connection/429/recoverable transient backend -> typed C0 DeliveryExecutionFailure(DeliveryFailure(code, retry_class=transient, source_capability=...)), with M1 solely accounting execution retry. Invalid canonical scope/schema/mapping, capacity violation, verification mismatch or uncertain/failed seal -> fail closed, explicit stable nonretryable code for the pinned inputs unless a reviewed classification proves transient. Cooperative stop/deadline/ownership loss propagate through C0 control, never swallowed. Unknown exceptions follow M1 permanent-by-default policy. A failed query does not mutate the accepted work lifecycle. Safe logs/metrics: project/version/bundle/work/attempt/candidate/generation/correlation, pinned schema/profile, stage duration, expected/actual counts, bulk failure-class totals, key/digest mismatch category, build/verify/publish latency, unavailable/stale age, active pins/PITs, cleanup backlog and deadline/cancel completion. Never log SDK request payloads, query text, credentials, physical locators or rejected values; no new public event vocabulary.

Verification commands for implementation (NOT executed during planning): `./venv/bin/python -m pytest tests/p5/knowledge -q`; with explicit isolated M2 PG/ES credentials/namespace, `./venv/bin/python -m pytest tests/p5/integration/test_search_elasticsearch.py tests/p5/integration/test_search_publication.py -q`; `./venv/bin/python -m pytest tests/p5 -q`; `./venv/bin/python -m pytest tests/p4 tests/p3 tests/p2 tests/p1 -q` only for paths actually present (confirm via rg --files before selecting); `./venv/bin/ruff check backend tests`; `./venv/bin/mypy backend`; `./venv/bin/black --check --workers 1 backend tests`; `./venv/bin/pip check`; `git diff --check`. Before final acceptance run actual repository pytest collection/suite with explicit approved infrastructure and disclose skipped/unrun checks. Use fixtures that reject application DB/index and clean only their validated exact test namespace. Migration and compose validation must target explicit isolated resources, never expose rendered secrets; no service start/migration now. Add benchmark command after its real marker/path exists; no invented pytest options.

M2 final acceptance: (1) C1 owner freeze after recorded M3/M6 fixture review, tested exact ES/client/security/deployment and measured finite operational profile; (2) complete deterministic canonical projection, no new semantics/source access; (3) owner-approved physical-layout decision plus real drain/seal/independent match-all readback proof, old credentials/late writes unable to mutate an accepted generation; (4) atomic fenced M1-owned outer acceptance, producer-only metadata, no private lifecycle access; (5) bounded authorized exact-version lexical reads/cursors with current/historical identity explicit and typed capability states; (6) failed candidate/rebuild never destroys prior accepted data; (7) independently reviewed/implemented approved M1 completed-work rebuild/storage-loss interface OR explicit owner acceptance of these unavailable capabilities as a scope deferral; changed-profile rebuild also needs explicit deferral or separately approved/implemented policy evolution, never fake support; (8) safe reader/cleanup/orphan proof, approved grace and retained accepted histories; (9) ES failure isolated from canonical/diagram/report paths; (10) explicit initial-P5 semantic decision: approved deferral with clear disabled capability, or actual approved semantic/hybrid provider and acceptance evidence if required; (11) real infrastructure, regression/quality/resource evidence and owner module acceptance. An unproven seal blocks M2 completion without exception; a missing mandatory rebuild/semantic capability cannot be waved away by reporting unavailable. Implementation remains planned, not authorized, regardless of this checklist's detail.

### 7.12 Owner decisions, risks and approval prerequisites

**Decision-resolution record:** the refined M2 plan is OWNER APPROVED FOR IMPLEMENTATION; execution is explicitly NOT STARTED. The six decision records below retain their individual evidence/change-control prerequisites: no ES option is technically validated, C1 is not frozen, and no unmeasured operational value is approved by plan acceptance. M1/C0 remain frozen; the proposed M1 amendment requires its separate authorization/review. Wait for owner instruction before starting B1. Evidence/approval checkpoints distinguish plan acceptance from a successful experiment, deployed capability and final module acceptance.

| Decision | Recommendation / alternatives and rationale | Required evidence, approval and batch dependency |
|---|---|---|
| M2-D1 ES server/client/deployment | Official self-hosted compatible pair in reproducible approved isolated environment; managed ES viable only with equivalent exact-index/security/seal APIs. No “latest” or assumed Python support | B1 compatibility matrix below; owner approves tested exact image digest/client version/license/security/resources before repository dependency/compose/settings changes or B2 |
| M2-D2 Physical sealing | Prefer generation-specific concrete index + dedicated write-block/drain API, not because it is simpler but because the physical exclusion covers all documents/in-flight writes independently of scope tags. Shared index cheaper but no proven per-generation barrier found | Explicit amendment to P5 shared-index layout, real B1 race/permission/seal proof and resource/retention/admission evidence. B2 hard blocked if any missing. No C0/P1–P4 change |
| M2-D3 Completed-work rebuild | Narrow proposed public M1 same-profile successor request; distinguish failed retry, completed rebuild, integrity repair and changed-profile migration. Defer changed-profile rebuild until separately scoped policy evolution; no per-work profile rewrite | Explicit M1 change-control approval + independent M1 review/regression before rebuild integration. No M1 changes now. If deferred, completed rebuild/accepted-index repair and automated integrity-repair capability remain unavailable; full acceptance needs explicit scope deferral |
| M2-D4 C1 vocabulary/read compatibility | Freeze immutable manifest/envelope, typed records/hash domains, status/cursor/errors/capabilities and exact reads after M3/M6 fixture review, separate from C0 | B1 consumer review then owner C1 freeze before affected B2/B3 integration; compatibility matrix and all versions/unknown fields tested. C0 stays exactly v1.0.0 |
| M2-D5 Operational profile | Approve measured finite budgets with headroom and project/global admission, retaining accepted histories. Trial inputs are never defaults | B1 measurement matrix below; consolidated owner budget decision before B2/B3 affected work. Missing grace disables cleanup; insufficient capacity blocks admission, not history truncation |
| M2-D6 Lexical/semantic scope | Explicit initial lexical-only P5 acceptance with semantic/hybrid deferred pending provider is recommended, not already approved. Product semantics preserved; alternative is required semantic delivery with approved provider/profile | Master product goal + P5 conditional baseline reconciled in §7.2. Owner must choose initial acceptance; mandatory semantics then blocks completion until implemented, not merely reported unavailable |

#### 7.12.1 D1 compatibility and D5 operational evidence — consolidated B1 checkpoint

B1 requires separate explicit implementation/spike authorization and an owner-provided/authorized disposable environment. This task approves neither installing a trial dependency nor starting a service. Before selecting final deployment versions, validate candidate pairs in that authorized isolated environment; this does not authorize editing repository requirements/compose/.env. After testing, submit one consolidated report for exact package/deployment/layout/C1/limits approval, rather than routine per-helper owner pauses. If an isolated test environment is not authorized/provided, report compatibility/sealing BLOCKED; literature review alone cannot advance B2.

Compatibility report must record exact server/client/Python 3.14/Linux versions, official support matrix, distribution/license and maintenance status, package installation/import/type behavior, image digest, TLS CA/hostname/auth and credential separation, persistent storage/health/container heap/CPU/memory/disk configuration. Test strict mapping/settings comparison and same-name new UUID rejection, bulk partial errors/duplicates/unknown outcomes, PIT/search_after renewal/expiry/tie ordering, finite transport/server deadlines and actual cancellation completion, disabled hidden helper/SDK retry, restart recovery and no auto-create. Verify least privileges by negative tests, not role names: provisioner create/schema only as needed; writer exact candidate create_doc without manage/delete; independent verifier/read-only/PIT plus required exact metadata visibility; sealing adapter isolated manage capability; cleanup exact authorized delete without ordinary worker access. Selected privilege grants must be API-tested because broad manage can include unblock/delete. Treat trusted administrative misuse as an audited integrity failure, never assert WORM against a cluster administrator.

| Measurement | Experimental design / evidence required before approval |
|---|---|
| Projection size/serialization | Empty, representative, maximum canonical-boundary and adversarial safe metadata/Unicode inputs; record counts/UTF-8 document, manifest, bulk and total bytes; deterministic-equivalence checks and no hidden truncation |
| Bulk/concurrency | Existing §7.10 trial grid remains test-only; record throughput, item failure/retry/backpressure, submission/drain duration and memory for multiple projects/attempts; derive finite docs+bytes+in-flight limits |
| Application CPU/memory | Fresh process baseline/current RSS and workload peak (not lifetime pytest ru_maxrss), CPU/time and retained allocations after GC; distinguish client/writer memory from ES JVM and containers |
| ES cost | Heap/GC/native RSS/CPU, disk/segments/file handles, primary/replica count, cluster-state/startup/restart/recovery latency for shared versus per-generation indexes. Model accepted historical generations + in-progress replacements + retries/orphans at realistic project counts |
| Verification/seal | Independent full-key/payload readback time/memory, refresh and drain/block acknowledgement, unknown result and delayed-request races; boundary counts must fit finite attempt budget without weakening completeness |
| Reads/deadlines | p50/p95/p99 and worst observed latency for scoped exact/name/text queries and concurrent projects; SQL pin + ES iteration + mapping + final pointer check + cleanup included; actual cancel/timeout server completion recorded separately |
| PIT/pins/grace | Measured PIT resource/segment retention, renewal/exhaustion and reader-crash behavior; max session/request lifetime and orphan deletion safe point must justify grace, not just elapsed lease. No cleanup before outstanding work is known stopped |
| Admission/retention | Bounded active builds/index count/shards/disk/heap/orphan backlog globally and per project; startup/recovery envelope and fairness; throttle rather than delete accepted history. Record deployment size assumptions and required operational headroom |

Report recommended finite values, measured sample count/repeatability/environment, headroom and failure mode at each boundary. Exact numbers remain unapproved until owner sign-off; missing values fail closed, not default to P4 or SDK unbounded behavior. Record limits that gate production activation separately from verified functional fixtures. Initial retention remains no automatic deletion of accepted/historical generations; orphan grace/cleanup still requires explicit numerical approval.

#### 7.12.2 D3 proposed M1 public extension — change control, not M2 orchestration

Four distinct operations:

1. Eligible exhausted-transient FAILED run: existing M1 retry/RetryRequest, identical pinned input/profile. No extension or completed-generation claim.
2. Explicit rebuild of a COMPLETED search run: proposed new public M1 operation below creates a new PENDING logical run, not an execution retry of the completed attempt.
3. Confirmed missing/corrupted accepted storage: M2 records an integrity observation, exposes UNAVAILABLE for affected exact reads, and may request the same M1 operation only under approved automatic-repair policy. A transport outage alone never establishes missing/corrupt data or triggers an unbounded rebuild cascade.
4. Projector/mapping/profile change: a different immutable input/policy, not same-profile retry. Frozen WorkIdentity validation binds each profile/input to its immutable bundle DeliveryPolicy. **Recommend reject changed-profile requests in this narrow amendment**, with explicit profile-migration-unavailable capability. Separately authorized M1 policy evolution/new-bundle selection must be designed/reviewed before supporting this operation; changing an old bundle/work identity or silently rerunning all six slots is forbidden. Initial M2 upgrade-rebuild acceptance must be explicitly deferred if this remains outside scope.

Proposed M1-owned port (does not exist): `request_search_reprojection(scope: VersionScope, request: SearchReprojectionRequest, principal: AuthorizedReprojectionPrincipal) -> SearchReprojectionAccepted`. A separately reviewed additive M1 contract, NOT a field/change to C0 RetryRequest/RetryAccepted/WorkIdentity, would define:

- request: idempotency_key, expected_predecessor_work_id, expected_accepted_reference (exact C0 ArtifactReference), reason enum (manual_rebuild/storage_missing/storage_corrupt); internal observation_id required for system integrity repair. Resource/generation/reference locators stay internal; browser body remains idempotency_key only at the proposed M2 route. Trusted server resolves expected predecessor/reference/profile once and persists the request snapshot; retries resolve prior operation BEFORE re-resolving today's pointer. Clients cannot supply profile/fence/epoch/runtime config or counterfeit system principal.
- principal: delegated OWNER/ADMIN for manual request with fresh project authorization; system execution is a narrowly configured trusted repair actor with durable audit/policy admission, not a public role or browser service-token override. Proposed system integration needs explicit owner authorization. M2 supplies an immutable observation through this port, never inserts/updates delivery rows.
- accepted: exact scope, operation_id, predecessor_work_id, new_work_id and immutable accepted state=PENDING, source/profile fingerprint and reason. 202 only after M1's durable transaction; replay returns original accepted result even after successor completion. New contract/version and error taxonomy are reviewed independently of unchanged C0.

Proposed transaction: use M1's project admission → bundle/current search slot lock order, validate eligible exact source and trusted configured runtime/profile/registered handler/fleet, and enforce approved bounded rebuild rate/round policy. Resolve/check the idempotency record under actor/project/action/key with canonical request digest. Same key+body returns original operation; different body conflicts. Expected predecessor/reference mismatch conflicts. For different keys racing the same predecessor, one creates a successor, others return conflict identifying safe operation metadata, not another run. While an active successor is pending/queued/running/retrying, a further request conflicts; no cancellation/reset. Under the same-profile bundle, mark only predecessor is_current=false (existing M1 structural pointer pattern); preserve its COMPLETED state, attempt/receipt/output/identity bytes and timestamps. Insert successor with new work UUID/run_number+1, same scope/binding/bundle/input and normal M1 pending execution counters; initial attempt/fence/dispatch assigned only through normal M1 orchestration. Preserve all diagram/report work and pinned barriers.

Persist the durable idempotency/audit record and successor intent atomically; M1's existing dispatch/recovery sends identifier-only work after commit. Broker failure cannot lose intent. Old M2 active generation/pointer is not cleared: sealed successor prepares outside UoW, M2 stages its own pointer on the borrowed Session, M1 final live-fence CAS/commit controls replacement. Failed rebuild leaves predecessor COMPLETED history and old accepted reference (physically healthy => readable; physically lost => UNAVAILABLE) unchanged; M1 successor ends FAILED/retries under normal rules. Ambiguous enqueue/acceptance is resolved through public operation status/idempotency replay and exact committed reference, not another request or candidate adoption. All pinned historical/report references remain readable if their physical data exists.

Persistence impact: recommend a NEW M1-owned reprojection request table in a separately authorized additive migration, with operation UUID, actor/action/project/key digest uniqueness, canonical request fingerprint, exact predecessor/reference/profile/input snapshot, successor UUID, reason/observation ID and audit timestamp. Reference only M1/P5-owned keys; no cross-backend FK. Do not overload DeliveryRetryRequestModel.action with a new meaning or reuse retry_of_work_id as a rebuild link without review. Existing delivery_work uniqueness/current-slot/run fields can be reused; completed state/receipt schemas and C0 stay unchanged. New policy knobs require explicit approval, not manual-retry defaults copied into rebuild. Operation read/replay belongs to the public M1 extension, not M2 tables. Migration is not claimed unnecessary merely because current fields appear reusable.

Exact proposed M1 change-control inventory: additive public reprojection contracts/provider and operation-read interface; narrow application/coordinator/repository method for request/idempotency/successor intent; new M1-owned reprojection request model/migration/metadata registration; focused authorization/concurrency/redelivery/ambiguous-commit/history regressions. Final filenames/revision allocated only in the separately approved amendment, not M2 source inventory. Existing contracts.py C0, retry semantics, attempts/fencing/leases/publication UoW, recovery algorithms/task payload/queues, source adapter, P1–P4, diagram/report behavior are outside that amendment. An independent M1 review must verify any necessary call-site additions rather than infer approval from M2 planning. **No such change is authorized or implemented here.** If not approved, completed rebuild, accepted-storage repair and profile upgrades remain explicitly unavailable; M2 must not claim full rebuild/recovery DoD without an owner-approved scope deferral. Temporary unavailability is not a new successful projection.

#### 7.12.3 D4 C1 freeze and D6 capability acceptance

C1 is separately versioned proposed 1.0.0, not C0 1.0.0 reused under a different name. B1 must supply producer and M3/M6 consumer JSON/golden fixtures covering every record kind, immutable manifest bytes/hash domains, exact ArtifactReference, empty/partial canonical inputs, uncertainty/provenance, SearchRequest/Hit/Page/Status/capabilities, cursor continuation/error envelope and active/historical reference reads. M3/M6 reviewers must review fixtures before C1 freeze; this does not begin their implementation. Owner records accepted fields/version/errors and compatibility matrix. Strict unknown required fields/versions fail closed; additive fields need explicit producer/consumer compatibility review; incompatible representations require new C1/profile/index generation, never an in-place old manifest rewrite or C0 extension.

SearchManifest is the exact immutable primary artifact bytes stored durably by M2; C0 content digest and size describe these bytes. It binds concrete index name+UUID, generation/candidate/artifact, full scope/profile/input, key/count/payload/semantic digests and seal/verifier versions. M3 consumes an accepted ArtifactReference through M2's public reader; M6 receives safe identity/status/hits, not manifest locators or internal PIT credentials. C1 must reconcile semantic hash exclusions versus stored-envelope exactness, all optional/required provenance, rank ordering and score meaning, observed-current timestamp/unknown semantics, cursor authorization/generation/index/profile binding and HTTP 409/410/422/503/504 mappings before downstream integration. All active-generation paginated operations reject pointer drift; historical explicit reads stay tied to their accepted generation, never latest. Representation/mismatch fixtures are contract proof, not live ES durability proof.

Capability proposal: SearchCapabilities has separately typed lexical/semantic/hybrid entries with state=`available|disabled|unavailable|unsupported`, safe reason_code and applicable pinned profile/version. available = configured compatible healthy path; disabled = deployment/owner policy intentionally off; unavailable = configured/required capability cannot currently serve (e.g. missing approved provider/config or outage); unsupported = selected implementation/profile has no such method. Before any semantic provider approval, advertise semantic/hybrid unavailable with reason=provider_not_approved and no model/dimensions claim; after an explicit initial-scope deferral, advertise disabled with reason=owner_deferred. Neither state means semantic passed acceptance. Recognized but disabled/unavailable/unsupported mode returns typed 422 search_capability_unavailable with mode/state/reason; unknown mode is typed input 422; a transient configured-provider outage returns typed 503. Never silently fallback to lexical or label keyword scores semantic.

Future semantic support requires a provider-neutral EmbeddingProvider port and separate immutable embedding profile: provider/model revision, tokenizer/text-policy digest, dimensions/distance metric, normalization and deterministic tolerance/version; hybrid fusion/tie policy pinned as well. Privacy/allowed canonical text, offline versus external network, credentials/retention, model/redistribution license, cost/rate/resource budgets, Python 3.14 install, reproducibility and scope-before-ranking tests require approval before implementing vector fields/provider calls. This future design is not AI architectural interpretation or M3/P6 implementation. If initial semantics is required, expand the approved module work within these same three batches only after a scoped revision, and block final acceptance until real embedding/hybrid tests pass. Global phase approval alone does not settle the baseline's explicit D4 acceptance question.

Primary risks: shared-index sealing contradiction, missing M1 successful-rebuild API, schema/shard inflation, ES eventual refresh versus actual completeness, wrong-process memory measures, indefinite PIT/readers, hidden client retry/partial results, stale current-version hints, sensitive canonical metadata and accidental M1 lifecycle duplication. Mitigate with independent readback, exact UUID/profile/cursor pinning, least privileges, bounded memory and real failure tests; do not claim these are solved by schema declarations.

Technical references inspected for this plan: [Elastic create_doc privilege](https://www.elastic.co/docs/reference/elasticsearch/security-privileges), [dedicated add-block API](https://www.elastic.co/docs/api/doc/elasticsearch/operation/operation-indices-add-block), [write-block and in-flight drain semantics](https://www.elastic.co/docs/reference/elasticsearch/index-settings/index-block), [Python client timeout/retry configuration](https://www.elastic.co/docs/reference/elasticsearch/clients/python/configuration), [PIT/search_after semantics](https://www.elastic.co/docs/reference/elasticsearch/rest-apis/paginate-search-results), [official bulk helper behavior](https://elasticsearch-py.readthedocs.io/en/stable/api_helpers.html). Elastic documents a drain guarantee for successfully completed dedicated write-block calls; this supports option B's design, not proof of StackSense's selected deployment. No selected pair, real-service sealing/cancellation/resource experiment, parser spike or new dependency was run.

**Architecture consistency check:** canonical PostgreSQL model remains authoritative; ES projection is disposable; platform/Mongo ownership and ADR-0001 remain unchanged; no graph traversal/lifecycle, source analysis, context/diagram/report/frontend/AI enters M2; no P1–P4/C0 change is made. M1 owns all durable executions and acceptance commit; M2 owns actual ES generation proof/readers and enlisted local metadata. The plan is owner-approved for implementation; the separately scoped M1 amendment and evidence-dependent physical-layout/contract/operational checkpoints remain explicit gates, not hidden exceptions. No implementation has begun.

No C0/C1 schema, M1 lifecycle, publication/persistence ownership, index mapping or P1–P4 contract changes. This authorization is not a general M2 reopening or approval of C3/operating limits.

### 7A.1 Authority, inspection evidence and discrepancies

Planning inspection: `feature_mannan`, 2026-11-18, with the existing uncommitted M2 remediation/infrastructure changes and user-deleted review/summary files preserved. No branch switch, application/test/configuration edit, dependency installation, service operation or migration is part of this planning pass. Relevant Python code uses 3.14, absolute imports, SQLAlchemy, FastAPI and strict Pydantic contracts. No `AGENTS.md` was found in this repository outside excluded dependency directories.

**Authority:** master Sections 0, 12–14, 18 and 25.16–25.17; accepted ADR-0001 and Architecture Model v1; frozen C0/C1 and P4 public interfaces; owner decisions/current execution ledger; retained P5 baseline now consolidated in §9, especially §§7.3–7.4, 15.3 and 17. This plan changes only this context file. The phase baseline was read in full. **Inspection extent is explicit:** master is 64,735 lines /164,583 words; its whole-file heading/reference inventory and M3-relevant normative sections were inspected, not an exhaustive line-by-line reading of every unrelated section. Do not represent this as the requested full-master audit. Relevant implementation and authoritative contracts, rather than historical planning names, ground the design below; an exhaustive master read remains an inspection limitation for owner review.

**Targeted final consistency audit — 2026-11-18:** rechecked normative master §§0.8/0.17 (project isolation/superseding scope), 12 (canonical authority/immutable versions), 13.34–13.40, 13.54–13.56 and 13.65–13.70 (bounded evidence/provenance, authorization and explicit degradation), 14.18–14.22, 14.37–14.51, 14.75–14.79 and 14.87 (ambiguity/ranking, context budgets/uncertainty, strategy versioning/testing and reproducibility only as versions permit), 18.40–18.43 and 18.51–18.56 (public interfaces/data ownership), and 25.16–25.17 (deterministic retrieval versus AI). Cross-checked ADR-0001, Architecture Model v1, frozen C0 normative handoff and actual C1/P4 public read signatures/final-check behavior. **No relevant M3 contradiction was identified.** This is a targeted normative/contract audit, not a claim to have exhaustively audited the entire master or runtime behavior. Full-master inspection limitation remains accurately disclosed; it is not grounds for speculative redesign. Existing master-wide product wording/history outside M3 is not reopened.

Audit interpretation: master retrieval ranking is relevance, never architecture truth; default/current-version convenience is resolved outside this exact-version C3 contract; frozen lexical-only C1 does not become semantic/vector search because M3 also uses graph context; metadata-only initial evidence does not claim source-snippet support. P6 prompt/provider-specific context consumes C3 rather than moving AI into M3. None changes the frozen architecture or the accepted phase scope.

No historical-generation selector, explicit cursor-close method, absolute deadline parameter or status deadline argument. |
