**Reconciliations:** the retained baseline's shared-index and unfrozen-header statements predate owner-approved per-generation M2/C1. Consume the actual frozen implementation, not that superseded sketch. Its proposed extension of `knowledge/contracts.py`, `dependencies.py`, `__init__.py` and `api/v1/knowledge.py` is no longer an ordinary M3 edit after M2 freeze: use new M3-owned files plus additive root composition instead. This preserves purpose/batches, not a redesign of M2. The residual master description calling StackSense a PostgreSQL workload product is not copied into this plan; no frozen master edit is authorized. M2's owner freeze does not turn missing production budgets, disabled deployment or its unrun final combined regression into passed evidence.

### 7A.2 Objective, ownership and non-goals

Deliver **authorized exact-version structured retrieval -> canonical resolution -> bounded evidence selection -> immutable C3 ContextBundle**, usable without AI and suitable for M6/future P6. M3 owns planning/selection, query-time disambiguation, context validation, derivative query cursors and safe response construction. These are not P3/P4 architectural identity resolution or new findings.

In scope: the seven baseline operations `lookup`, `relationships`, `dependencies`, `dependents`, `containment`, `paths`, `evidence`; lexical candidate discovery through C1; explicit optional graph enrichment through P4; canonical evidence/uncertainty/conflict resolution; deterministic reasons, omissions, budgets and typed failures; request-time APIs and C3 consumer fixtures.

Out of scope: ES/Neo4j clients or raw DSL/Cypher in M3; indexing/sealing/rebuild/retention; private M1/M2/P3/P4 tables/repositories; new durable work, queues, lifecycle states or public events; parsing/source execution/reanalysis; canonical entity merging or new architectural kinds; LLMs, embeddings/vector search, natural-language intent inference, answer/prompt generation, documentation ingestion, architecture comparison; diagram/report generation; React/Express implementation. **Recommend metadata-only evidence for initial C3; excerpts stay unavailable**, not partially implemented behind a pretend safe-reader port.

M1 alone retains durable dispatch/attempts/fencing/recovery/publication. M2 alone owns index generation, PIT/pins and search execution. P4 alone owns graph traversal/lifecycle. PostgreSQL's canonical Architecture Model remains the fact authority; M3 never updates it. No new C0 WorkKind or PreparedOutput is needed for a synchronous retrieval result.

### 7A.3 Proposed architecture and public interfaces

Data flow: trusted delegation -> validate request/policy -> exact published header -> immutable authorized context -> retrieval plan -> lazy C1/P4 adapters -> identity checks -> paged canonical rehydration -> evidence/conflict planning -> deterministic bounded assembly -> final participating-source checks -> validated serialization -> response. One request owns one operation budget, not a fresh budget per step.

Proposed public C3 interfaces in **new** `knowledge/retrieval_contracts.py`:

- `KnowledgeQueryPort.query(request, *, principal) -> KnowledgeQueryResponse` performs one controlled operation and returns canonical views, resolution state, source observations and optional signed continuation.
- `ContextReadPort.build_context(request, *, principal) -> ContextBundle` composes a bounded selection atomically. It is non-paginated: a later call is a new composition, not a mutable extension of an earlier bundle.
- `EvidenceReadPort.read_evidence(request, *, principal) -> EvidencePage` pages safe canonical metadata from a fixed selector set. No source file read, URL fetch or object-store locator.
- M3-local `CanonicalReadPort` exposes exact header/entity reads and bounded typed collection pages; `GraphReadPort` accepts only the existing P4 request types; C1 ports remain imported unchanged. These adapters establish testable cross-module boundaries, not alternative repositories or query engines.

`RetrievalPrincipal(actor_id, project_id, role, correlation_id?)` is constructed only after existing trusted delegation and project validation. Public Python consumers must supply that authorized context; a UUID alone is not authorization. In-process trusted composition is not a malicious-Python sandbox. M6 rechecks live project membership in Express before delegation; M3 does not query MongoDB or invent a membership service.

### 7A.4 Request contracts and controlled semantics

All C3 models: proposed `c3_version="1.0.0"`, strict closed fields/enums, finite numbers, recursive immutability and revalidation at ingress, adapter returns, assembly and wire serialization; reject unsupported versions and model-copy/construct bypasses. C3 is independent of C0/C1 versioning. Preserve their values/types, never overload their version fields.

`KnowledgeQueryRequest`: project UUID, exact architecture_version UUID, optional expected repository/analysis/snapshot UUIDs (mismatches rejected), operation, typed subject/target, literal `q?`, closed filters, graph parameters, requested limits, optional C3 cursor. No `latest`, arbitrary index, user actor, raw query language, arbitrary sort, requested producer IDs or resource paths.

`SubjectSelector` is a tagged union, not loosely interpreted text: `{record_kind, record_id:UUID}` OR `{record_kind, logical_id:str}` OR entity `{name:str, namespace:tuple[str,...]?}`. A logical ID is preserved byte-for-byte; never percent-decode, casefold or NFC-rewrite it. Name comparison may trim/NFC-normalize the query under the versioned strategy; stored identity remains unchanged. Case-insensitive lexical suggestions do not become identity matches. Qualified matching uses the actual `namespace` tuple plus name; no invented `qualified_name`/display-name column or heuristic namespace parser. `paths` requires two unambiguously resolved entity selectors.

`ContextRequest`: exact scope inputs, bounded ordered subject selectors, optional literal query hint, at most one lexical stream and an allowlisted bounded list of structural/evidence operations, explicit required/optional source declarations, filters and requested selection budgets. `question_hint` is retrieval data only; M3 does not interpret “why”, assume a database exists or call an LLM. Unknown operations, conflicting modes and unsupported natural-language/semantic requests fail validation. Query length must fit frozen C1's **500-character** maximum; a separate UTF-8 transport/identifier bound is policy, not a silent substitution of the older 512-byte sketch.

| Operation | Source / behavior | Limits and unavailable behavior |
| --- | --- | --- |
| lookup | Exact canonical UUID first; typed logical ID or name/namespace through bounded canonical pages; literal q through C1 for candidates | Search is not required for explicit UUID reads. A lexical top hit never proves unique identity. |
| relationships | Bounded canonical incident-relationship collection filtering for fixed subject, direction and canonical kinds | No graph outage dependency; one-hop facts only, not a relational reimplementation of traversal. |
| dependencies / dependents | P4 `get_dependencies` / `get_dependents`; outgoing/incoming preserve canonical edge direction | All twelve approved dependency kinds remain available; `contains` is rejected here. No runtime/causal impact claim. |
| containment | P4 `get_containment`; parents/children/both | Hierarchy only; never blended into dependency traversal. |
| paths | P4 `get_bounded_paths`; two canonical entity UUIDs | Existing depth/path caps; no arbitrary path language. P4 path results are bounded, not cursor-paginated. |
| evidence | Canonical evidence metadata associated with explicit entity/relationship/evidence selectors | Bounded scans and pagination; source snippets remain unavailable. |

P4 ceilings remain depth 5, 500 nodes, 1,000 edges, 25 paths, page 100 and maximum 5 seconds per graph operation. These are ceilings, not M3 operating defaults. C3 request limits must also fit the approved M3 total-operation/source-call budget. Supported filters initially match actual fields: C1 document/entity kinds and static statuses, canonical kind/status, explicit subject scope and P4 relationship kinds/directions. Do not claim efficient language/framework/application/path filtering not exposed by C1. Optional post-canonical predicates must be explicitly budgeted, disclosed as bounded-window filtering, and separately approved before adding them.

### 7A.5 Canonical resolution, missing interfaces and ambiguity

Recommend **paged canonical adapters**, not full-document loads per request. Use `ArchitectureQueryService.get_version` once to establish `RetrievalScope`: project/repository/snapshot/analysis/version UUIDs, sequence, canonical digest, architecture/IR schema versions and publication time. This is a C3 published-header view, **not a fabricated full C0 VersionScope**. When search is used, retain its real full `VersionScope` in the source observation and compare every header-exposed identity/digest/schema/sequence/time field. Analyzer/configuration values not exposed by the canonical header are copied as C1 provenance, not claimed independently reverified. Canonical-only retrieval does not invent those missing fields. Unsupported schema/digest/ownership disagreement fails closed.

Exact entity UUID resolves through `get_entity`; all other record kinds/typed logical IDs use their existing paged collection services. Correlate returned UUID/logical ID pairs, never copy `architecture_resource_id` hashing logic into M3 or import a private persistence helper. Scan once per needed collection with bounded pages/rows and retain only required matches/references; batch selected targets in memory, not N full scans per evidence ID. `list_evidence(entity_id=...)` can narrow an entity's evidence. There is no promised arbitrary-ID bulk method, direct relationship/evidence/conflict lookup, cross-table SQL, or unapproved indexed lookup. Large-scope latency is a real cost; if measurements require a new upstream public read port, stop and request explicit owner change control rather than editing P3/P4.

**Canonical-resolution measurement policy (owner-amended at freeze, 2026-11-19):** No fixed five-second M3 end-to-end latency acceptance requirement remains. The owner accepts the completed 25,000-entity measurements in §7A.0. For future operating-policy evaluation, before measurements, identify the required-success use cases and candidate finite policy envelope (deadline including final checks/serialization, rows/pages/calls, response bytes, retained/current/peak memory and concurrent requests). Trial envelopes are experimental, not production approval. Measure cold/warm repeated lookups with early/middle/late/missing/ambiguous IDs/names, high-fanout evidence and conflicts, representative and near-canonical-ceiling models, and record the actual public method calls, rows/pages, durations and process measurements. A disclosed `incomplete` result is valid for a deliberately bounded exploratory scan; it is **not** a passing result for a use case whose agreed acceptance requires exact resolution/complete minimum evidence.

**Future operating-policy stop-and-escalate rule (not a withdrawn five-second freeze gate):** if a required-success case cannot resolve and assemble its minimum evidence within that finite envelope, if scan/retained-memory or finalization costs exceed its bounds, or if the proposed result requires claiming absence/uniqueness without exhausting the necessary scope. The affected path cannot pass B1/production readiness merely by returning incomplete; report reproducible commands/environment, input sizes/positions, repeated measurements, failed bound and user-visible effect. Submit a scoped options/change proposal for owner review (accept an explicitly reduced bounded capability, revise a measured operating policy, or separately authorize an upstream public read-interface improvement). Do not compensate through private P3/P4 persistence, direct/cross-table SQL, copied identity logic, an unapproved cache/full-model materialization or a frozen contract edit. Do not silently raise budgets. Unaffected pure fixtures/builders may progress only within separately authorized work, not as evidence that this gate passed.

Resolution states: `resolved`, `ambiguous`, `not_found`, `incomplete`. Apply an explicitly supplied namespace as a hard constraint, not an optional ranking hint; within the valid selector scope exact record identity outranks name/text discovery. Preserve master's deterministic priorities without inventing unavailable qualified/display-name fields. Multiple equally applicable matches remain ambiguous, ordered by canonical logical ID and UUID; return candidates with reasons for caller selection. Lower-ranked lexical suggestions are not silently selected. A name/logical-ID scan cannot establish global uniqueness or absence until its relevant canonical collection is exhausted. Hitting a scan budget yields `incomplete`, even if only one candidate has been seen; do not relabel it resolved/not_found. An explicit identity supplied by a user can remain resolved independently of incomplete discovery of other names.

Missing **user-selected** item may yield query `not_found` / safe evidence-detail 404 within an authorized version. Missing referenced canonical evidence/endpoints after exhaustive resolution is integrity failure, not ordinary no-results. A budget-stopped lookup is unknown/incomplete, not a missing-reference defect. Duplicate same-kind canonical identity with different payloads fails integrity; identical duplicates dedupe and preserve their retrieval origins. User query disambiguation never merges canonical entities or changes existing conflict decisions.

### 7A.6 Search and graph binding, cursors and final consistency

For every C1 page verify supported C1, complete page/hit scope equality, requested exact version and canonical header agreement; retain `(generation_id, index_uuid)` from the first page. All participating continuation pages, independent searches and any required final probe must match this pair. Graph results must match project/version plus one P4 generation/schema/projector binding. **Search and graph generation UUIDs are independently owned and need not equal each other.** They must refer to the same canonical Architecture Version. Rehydrate graph UUID/logical-ID pairs and relationships/endpoints against canonical records; projected metadata/scores are never final evidence.

Treat C1/P4 cursors as opaque, preserve request query/filter/page-size semantics and actor binding, never call private decoders or reinterpret PIT tokens. No automatic cross-generation restart; any source drift rejects the entire composition with a typed 409. Caller can explicitly retry a fresh whole request. Search generation/index loss, expiry and integrity failures retain their distinct typed meaning. M3 cannot select a historical search generation of the same version; historical **Architecture Versions** remain supported exactly as frozen C1/P4 allow.

Proposed **C3 cursor** is only a bounded stateless continuation value, not a generic retrieval-session framework. Its minimum signed envelope contains contract/token version and signing-key identifier; actor UUID; exact scope/canonical digest; operation and request/selection/policy fingerprints; only the policy-bounded resolved subject identifiers required to continue; participating search generation/index and/or graph generation bindings; required opaque upstream cursor or canonical scan position; original issued-at/absolute expiry; and authenticated continuation-page/work counters needed to enforce the finite chain budget. Unused source bindings are null/absent under one frozen wire convention. Do not embed candidate/result/evidence bodies, a ContextBundle, raw query, credentials, arbitrary client state or physical locators. Caller repeats the original request, whose fingerprint must match. No server-side session table, cache, renewal service or background job is introduced.

Reauthorize membership/delegated actor and exact scope on **every** page: cursor possession is never authorization. Before data-port work enforce the encoded-byte cap, bounded decoding/JSON depth/field count and closed shape, valid HMAC/key ID and actor/request/scope/policy binding; reject duplicate/unknown fields and malformed/forged/mismatched tokens safely with 422. After authentication, an expired valid token is 410; changed participating generation is 409. Validate integer positions/counters and require forward scan/page progress; a token cannot reset work counters or authorize an arbitrary offset. Keep the original chain expiry and cumulative page/work ceiling across continuations, not a sliding renewal; exhausted chain budget ends the bounded view with explicit coverage/no cursor, never hidden work. A fresh user request starts a new separately bounded operation. Numerical TTL/counter/size ceilings require measurement/approval, not invented defaults.

Every paginated operation binds **all and only its participating sources**, including canonical evidence pages whose fixed subjects came from search/graph selection. Canonical-only paging has no fabricated projection generation. Preserve opaque upstream tokens without decoding them; the C3 expiry does not extend a C1/P4 expiry, and the upstream may return a typed earlier expiry. Reject an upstream token/selector envelope that cannot fit the C3 size budget rather than externalize it into an unapproved session/cache or silently drop its binding. Bound signature/decoding costs and aggregate calls per page and chain; replay of an otherwise valid token is not a privilege escalation, but remains subject to current authorization and deployment admission/rate controls.

M3 continuation is a **bounded view**, not an unlimited global re-sort: do not reorder pages by newly discovered evidence strength after already emitting them. Canonical views use logical-ID order; search views preserve C1 ordering and use deterministic in-window selection with explicit window provenance; graph views preserve P4 continuation semantics. Bind page size/order/filters and selection plan. Query-level ambiguity cannot be bypassed by a cursor. Context assembly is non-paginated and may apply its full bounded-set ordering before emission.

Filtered canonical evidence/relationship scan windows may legitimately contain zero selected items while more canonical rows remain. C3 must distinguish that state from exhausted empty results: advance a strictly increasing signed scan position, disclose `scan_incomplete`/known rows examined, and return continuation only within the validated per-request work budget. Do not reuse C1's nonempty-page validator for this different C3 case. Lookup candidate pages never claim global uniqueness by accumulating untrusted client candidates. `paths` is bounded/non-paginated because the frozen P4 path result has no continuation; disclose its `truncated` flag, never fabricate a path cursor.

**Final checks using only existing ports:** finish canonical rehydration/assembly, re-read the exact canonical header/digest; **only if graph participated**, use public `GraphGenerationReader.verify_generation_still_active` with the remaining budget. The conditional search rule below applies to a successful C1-dependent response, not every M3 request. Final serialization/checkpoint must finish within the remaining budget. These are final point-in-time observations, not a transaction spanning PG/ES/Neo4j or a guarantee that pointers cannot change after response. No new C1 method or private M2 access is implied.

**Minimum final C1 probe rule:** a response is search-dependent when it retains or relies on a successfully read C1 generation for candidate/subject selection, search-derived absence/no-evidence, ranking/selection provenance, or a carried C1 binding/continuation. This includes a lexical lookup, a lexical ContextBundle, graph traversal anchored by a search-resolved subject, and later query/evidence pages continuing a search-derived selection even if that page obtains no new lexical hits. If those inputs were used before subsequent canonical resolution/context work, the successful response requires one final C1 validation after selection is fixed. A read returning zero hits still establishes a C1-dependent observation. Dependency is determined by provenance/plan, not the endpoint name or whether the selected entity happens to have an explicit UUID later.

No probe for canonical-only lookup/relationships/evidence/context; no probe for graph-only retrieval anchored through canonical IDs; no probe solely because advisory search status was observed. If an optional C1 call fails and the allowed degraded response discards **all** C1-dependent selections/absence claims/bindings, record that limitation but do not probe an unavailable source to validate a canonical/graph-only response. A rejected/error response never performs a success probe. Do not call or initialize ES on these non-search paths.

For the current at-most-one lexical stream, the final validation is one public `SearchReadPort.search` call using the same authorized actor, exact project/version, literal query, mode, filters and page limit as that stream, with `cursor=None` and only timeout reduced to the remaining budget. Compare its full C1 scope/generation/index UUID with the retained binding, rely on C1's existing physical integrity/final pointer checks, and discard probe hits/cursor as selection input. Do not probe per subject, evidence item, page previously read or graph source. If a normal final C1 read is deliberately positioned after all selection-changing work and establishes that same final check, it may serve this role once—never add a duplicate probe. A continuation with only a prior search binding must still perform the validation. Drift or physical/expiry failures remain typed whole-composition failures; no silent switch/retry.

This proves only that the **same exact search generation/index was readable and active at the final C1 observation**; it does not prove canonical evidence completeness, CURRENT freshness, runtime truth, or cross-source atomicity. Account for it separately as an additional search call, result mapping/readback, PG pin finalization, response-finalization time and up to one newly opened PIT/read-pin for that successful response (unless an existing final read serves the role). Reserve these costs before retrieval; include the probe in request/chain call ceilings and concurrent outstanding-PIT measurements. It uses C1's ordinary page/PIT semantics; a `has_more` probe can leave one expiry-bound pin, while an exhausted probe is cleaned by C1. Do not drain it. If policy cannot admit the required validation, reject admission/capacity before work; if the remaining deadline expires, return deadline failure rather than omit the check. No production budget is selected here.

**Pin limitation:** M3 cannot explicitly close a successfully abandoned intermediate C1 cursor or final probe with `has_more` through the frozen public ports. Let M2's configured PIT/pin expiry govern; do not drain the whole index merely to close it. Never decode, delete or update M2 pins. Benchmark concurrent short compositions/probe overhead and bound search streams/pages/admission accordingly. If explicit cancellation/release becomes necessary, a separate reviewed C1 amendment is required; it is not included here.

**Freshness/status decision (owner-approved plan direction):** C1 `SearchStatusPort.get_status` accepts no deadline and may load the full canonical document/perform freshness scans/backend checks. Consume it as the existing separate advisory status/readiness observation for M6 or trusted orchestration, **not synchronously on the strict bounded query/context path**. Context source metadata reports freshness `UNKNOWN`, `is_current_version=null`, reason `not_observed_in_bounded_retrieval`, unless an explicitly identified separate trusted observation is supplied by composition; preserve its observed-at/source scope and mark it advisory, never proof of current availability. SearchPage capabilities and successful reads establish only the capabilities/read identity they actually expose. M3 must not fabricate CURRENT or infer readability from last attempt state. Do not accept client JSON as a trusted status observation. A future additive C1 deadline-aware status port would require separate owner change control before use inside bounded composition; it is not approved here. The approved direction is the advisory split and **no frozen C1 change**; C3 implementation/consumer review and freeze remain B1 gates.

**Retrieval success is independent of freshness classification.** A successful scoped C1 read/final validation establishes that generation's observed read success, not whether its Architecture Version is repository-current. `UNKNOWN` means currentness was not established by the bounded operation, **not** search failure, unreadable data, no evidence or automatically partial context. A context meeting its declared selection/required-source rules may be `complete` with freshness UNKNOWN; a retained historical version may be read successfully with advisory STALE. Keep request outcome, source read observation, capabilities and optional timestamped freshness observation distinct. Advisory failure or an older status timestamp must not overwrite a successful exact-generation read or force an otherwise unnecessary search probe. Actual C1 unavailability/deadline/integrity/identity failures still follow the required/optional source rules, never masquerade as UNKNOWN success.

### 7A.7 Evidence planning, provenance and conflict handling

`EvidencePlanner` takes canonical selected subjects/relationships plus their actual evidence references and a bounded scan plan. Resolve evidence logical IDs to actual records/UUIDs, dedupe on `(version, record kind, logical ID)` while preserving producer, source record type, artifact UUID, path, optional fact key, range, polarity and strength. Evidence does not become an entity; relationship endpoints remain canonical entity identities. Architecture summary hits are discovery/header hints, never invented authoritative summary text. Unresolved hits resolve to canonical unresolved records and preserve target descriptors/reasons/candidate references, not synthetic resolved edges. Conflict hits resolve to canonical conflict records; attach only with a verified subject/reference association, never string-similarity guesses.

Source coordinates are copied in their **actual persisted shape**. Canonical semantics remain UTF-8 bytes, zero-based/end-exclusive byte offsets/columns, one-based lines, BOM excluded and original LF/CRLF preserved. Persisted DTOs may contain `start_col`/`end_col` and only available range fields; do not rename them to F2 field names or invent missing offsets/encoding. Original Python decoding metadata is not promised by the canonical evidence DTO. Unicode/CRLF/BOM/non-UTF-8-derived fixture tests verify lossless metadata propagation, not an unsupported source excerpt operation. No file/network source access or parser recovery is allowed.

Ordering of evidence strength: direct_source > framework_structural > configuration > dependency > documentation, using frozen canonical enum ranks. Preserve supporting **and conflicting** polarity. Relevance determines usefulness, not architectural confidence; do not change confirmed/supported/inferred/unresolved/conflicting status, invent severity, create runtime truth or add a percentage confidence. Retain actual optional producer_id/version and source_fact_key as null when absent; no synthetic analyzer identifiers.

Selection is deterministic: required explicitly identified subjects first, their direct relationships, directly attached strong evidence, then optional nearby context/configuration/documentation. Within a class use exact-match priority, source operation/known path distance where actually available, evidence-strength rank, canonical logical ID/UUID. P4 subgraphs do not expose exact per-node distance: leave it null unless established by a returned path; do not invent shortest distances or implement another graph traversal engine for ranking. Keep C1 score/rank as retrieval provenance only; avoid fusing floating scores from unrelated sources as fact confidence.

Evidence/conflict records are never silently discarded. C3 is a bounded projection: selected evidence records and selected links carry exact observed totals/omissions where known, with completeness flags; original canonical status is retained even when not all supporting references fit. Preserve known opposing/conflict summaries and enough references to distinguish competing evidence before low-relevance optional material. If a required subject's minimum provenance/conflict disclosure cannot fit, fail capacity; do not return a falsely conflict-free fact. Conflicts not exhaustively scanned -> `conflict_coverage=incomplete`, not “no conflicts”. Do not manufacture a total omitted count for unscanned records. One-hop canonical relationship listing is allowed; multi-hop assistance remains P4-owned.

Safe content: reuse exported C1 `SourceReferenceDescriptor` and `filter_safe_metadata` for compatible fields; convert results defensively into C3 immutable views. Do not import M2 private helpers or copy its projection implementation. C3 conflict `details`, unresolved descriptors and provenance are explicitly allowlisted/sanitized; never passthrough arbitrary nested JSON or raw validation errors. Exclude unknown detail fields with aggregate omission reasons; rejected secret values/keys themselves never enter warnings/logs. Relative source paths are references only: `.env`, key/credential files remain unopened; metadata is not authority to fetch them. Literal secrets, raw source bodies, DSNs, internal paths/URLs and instruction-like repository strings must not be executed or promoted into instructions. All displayed source-derived labels remain untrusted data; P6 separately owns provider/prompt isolation.

### 7A.8 C3 ContextBundle v1 proposal

No output is an AI answer or authoritative Architecture Model. Proposed nested models and exact responsibilities:

| C3 model / fields | Invariant and meaning |
| --- | --- |
| `RetrievalScope` | Header-derived exact project/repository/analysis/snapshot/version UUIDs, sequence, canonical digest, arch/IR schema and published_at. Compare expected caller scope; never current-version substitution. |
| `RetrievalPlan` | Versioned strategy, normalized request fingerprint, fixed subjects/operation/direction/kinds, required/optional sources, selected policy fingerprint and approved budget snapshot. No executable query/URL/script. |
| `SourceObservation` | canonical/C1/P4 contract version; scope; actual search generation/index UUID/full C0 scope when used; actual graph generation/schema/projector when used; capability/freshness observation and provenance/limitations. Null means unused/unobserved, not synthetic identity. No physical storage locator or credentials. |
| `ResolvedSubject` | Input selector, resolution state, selected canonical reference if unique, bounded candidates with namespace/UUID/logical ID and deterministic reason codes; scan completeness and omitted/unknown counts. |
| `CanonicalFactView` | Record UUID/logical ID, kind/name/namespace/static status and allowlisted metadata, selected evidence references and explicit coverage. Facts are canonical selections, not free-text generated statements. |
| `RelationshipView` | Canonical relationship UUID/logical ID/kind/status, canonical source/target references in original direction, evidence links and coverage. No label implying observed runtime behavior. |
| `EvidenceItem` / `EvidenceBundle` | Canonical evidence UUID/logical ID, unmodified safe source descriptor, polarity/strength, verified subject links and provenance. Metadata only; excerpt capability unavailable. Every selected reference resolves or is explicitly outside the bounded selection, not a dangling falsely complete link. |
| `UnresolvedView` / `ConflictView` | Actual canonical UUID/logical ID/reason/type/subject and sanitized details/candidate/evidence references with coverage. No new canonical uncertainty vocabulary or forced reconciliation. |
| `SelectionRationale` | Closed codes such as explicit_identity, exact_name, lexical_candidate, direct_relationship, graph_context, supporting_evidence, conflicting_evidence; originating operation/hit key and known path hop only. Not natural-language speculation. |
| `Coverage` / `OmissionSummary` | Canonical completeness retained as provenance; per-source considered/selected/scanned counts, count-known flags, exhausted/truncated state and reasons (budget, optional-unavailable, ambiguity, scan-incomplete, safe-field exclusion). Selection completeness is not repository-analysis completeness. |
| `ContextBundle` | c3_version, scope, strategy/policy versions/fingerprints, request fingerprint, source observations, subjects, facts, relationships, evidence, unresolved/conflicts, rationales, coverage, limits/usage, outcome and semantic_digest. Delivery/correlation/timing metadata is separate from semantic content. |

`outcome`: `complete`, `partial`, `ambiguous`, `no_evidence`. `complete` means the **declared bounded selection** completed, not exhaustive repository truth. `no_evidence` requires exhausted planned mandatory discovery, otherwise partial/incomplete. An ambiguity is a typed successful result needing user selection, not a guessed subject. A canonical integrity or mandatory-source failure returns no successful bundle. Query/evidence page responses share these scope/coverage/error semantics; their cursor is not a ContextBundle continuation.

Deterministic serialization: explicit C3 schema/strategy/policy versions; sorted unique set-like references and stable ordered selection arrays; UUID canonical lowercase strings; sorted-key compact UTF-8 JSON with `ensure_ascii=False`, `allow_nan=False`; exact valid Unicode identifiers preserved; integers for counts/ranges; optional fields represented consistently as null. SHA-256 over domain-tagged `{contract:"C3",version:"1.0.0",kind:"context",semantic_payload:...}`. No use of C0's helper with a misleading C0 domain tag for a C3 digest. Define the semantic payload as an explicit allowlisted projection, not an indiscriminate dump of ContextBundle. Include canonical scope/digest, actually used generation/index identities, normalized request/strategy/policy identity, ordered selected canonical views/evidence/provenance, original statuses/polarities/conflicts, deterministic rationales, declared selection limits, outcome and coverage/omissions/count-known semantics. Exclude all correlation/generated-at/durations, execution-only scan/call timing metrics, cursors/PIT IDs, probe request metadata, floating search scores and the **entire advisory freshness/status observation**, not merely its timestamp. Selection counts/coverage remain semantic; operational cost observations do not. Response byte limits still apply to the complete wire bundle including non-semantic observations.

**Reproducibility guarantee:** byte-identical canonical encoding of the **same validated semantic payload** under the same C3 serialization/strategy/policy versions produces the same semantic digest. A replay that obtains the same ordered canonical selections, source bindings, rationales and coverage under those versions therefore matches. Equal query text/snapshot alone, or even equal generation IDs, does not guarantee equal full output: wall-time/budget stopping, dependency availability, observed candidate windows/coverage or selected content may differ. Those meaningful differences produce a different digest; advisory timestamps/correlation/timings alone do not. No byte-identical full HTTP response, permanent replay across rebuilds, cross-shard ranking stability or future-generation equivalence is promised. C1 scores can explain ranking within the observed C1 result only; they never set architectural status/strength, drive unsupported cross-source score fusion or enter the semantic digest as truth. Preserve canonical uncertainty/conflicts and explicit coverage even when this prevents claiming two bounded contexts are equivalent. Revalidate nested values at hashing/serialization; frozen outer Pydantic alone is insufficient.

### 7A.9 Budgets, deadlines, partiality and persistence

Recommend request-scoped stateless M3: **no new database tables, migrations, durable ContextBundle storage, cache, work state, worker payload or queue registration**. ContextBundle is immutable returned data, not a C0 published artifact. No POST idempotency record: query/context POSTs are read operations; repeat requests may observe a new active generation and are not a promise of permanent identical results. Future saved/AI contexts must retain the accepted bundle plus its fingerprint through separately approved consumer storage, not assume old generations can be selected through C1.

`RetrievalPolicy` is explicit versioned deployment configuration, not magic defaults. Validate before enabling M3; absent/malformed policy -> typed 503. Proposed fields: maximum query/identifier UTF-8 bytes, subjects/filters/operations, canonical rows/pages per collection, retained candidates, source calls/search pages/streams/final probes, graph depth/nodes/edges/paths within frozen ceilings, selected facts/relationships/evidence/unresolved/conflicts/reference links, output bytes, cursor bytes/TTL, total deadline, finalization/cancellation reserve and per-process admission. Bound aggregate work across pages/subjects/operations; no multiplicative bypass. Distributed ingress/admission belongs to M6/deployment policy, not a fabricated global semaphore. No production values approved here.

Initial **measurement candidates only**, inherited from phase baseline where applicable: metadata output 256 KiB, selected evidence 100, query/context whole-operation target 5s, canonical page at most the existing 100, C1 pages 25/50/100. Benchmark small and high-fanout entities (including 200+ evidence), 10k and near canonical ceilings, incomplete scans and concurrent requests in fresh subprocesses. Measure canonical scan cost, retained/current/peak RSS, PG/ES/Neo4j time, serialization bytes, final-probe/PIT lifetime cost and cancellation. Recommend finite values/headroom after evidence and obtain owner approval before operational activation. Do not infer them from the Compose JVM/CPU settings or M2 indexing benchmarks. C1's frozen representational ceilings are not proof of a production query SLO.

One monotonic deadline covers policy/authenticated scope setup, connection/pool acquisition, canonical queries/iteration, C1/P4 calls, mapping, final checks, pin transaction finalization and JSON serialization. Reserve finalization time before further work; pass actual remaining positive budgets to C1/P4, capped by their existing maxima. Canonical adapters use M3-owned read-only Sessions through the public canonical provider; set bounded PostgreSQL statement/lock waits per call and bounded connection acquisition, never edit P3 queries or leave a worker thread running after a response timeout. Search bookkeeping remains on its public provider's separate normal transaction/Session; do not mark it READ ONLY or roll it back while advertising a usable cursor. Composition owns safe Session finalization, not M1 lifecycle or M2 tables. B3 must prove cancellation of real DB work and backend bounds; a `wait_for` around a synchronous thread is not proof of termination. Honour the existing ES ambiguous-submit timeout/expiry backstop rather than promising immediate cancellation during network partition.

Capacity handling: invalid/excessive requested budgets -> 422; inability to fit required minimum context/provenance -> typed capacity error, no truncated JSON. Deterministically omit optional low-priority selection units and disclose exact known omissions; never trim raw strings/serialized bytes or hide conflicting evidence. Optional-source availability may yield a partial bundle only when explicitly optional in the validated plan. Required source unavailability, deadline expiry, generation drift or integrity failure abort the whole composition. No best-effort success after total deadline. No automatic M3 retry framework: propagate retryable class/code, allow a new caller-driven request; M1 retries only its own durable work.

### 7A.10 Security, failures, API and observability

Reuse existing service-token verification, actor UUID/project role validation and exact delegated project check; require a non-null actor. All four existing project roles may read; no new mutation permission. Authorization and header ownership validation occur **before** any canonical/search/graph data access, including optional-dependency resolution. Validate claimed repository/analysis/snapshot against the exact published header. Foreign, draft or absent exact versions retain existing safe 404 behavior; delegation failures retain their 401/403 contract. Reauthorize each continuation; signed cursor possession is never membership proof. Do not log the original request, query text, evidence payload, internal tokens/cursors or unsafe upstream exception strings.

Proposed FastAPI **new** `backend/api/v1/retrieval.py`, same existing `/api/v1` prefix; no direct browser boundary is added:

| Proposed endpoint | Request / result |
| --- | --- |
| POST `/architecture-versions/{version_id}/knowledge/query?project_id=...` | Closed controlled-query body without redundant route IDs; build domain request from authorized route scope -> 200 `KnowledgeQueryResponse` with resolution, canonical selected views, observations, coverage and C3 next_cursor if supported. |
| POST `/architecture-versions/{version_id}/knowledge/context?project_id=...` | Closed ContextRequest body, required/optional operations and approved budget overrides -> 200 immutable ContextBundle or typed failure; no async 202/work ID. |
| GET `/architecture-versions/{version_id}/knowledge/evidence?project_id=...` | Repeated typed entity_id/relationship_id/evidence_id selectors, bounded page/cursor and deadline -> 200 EvidencePage. Reject unsupported/mixed selector combinations per C3; no artifact URL/path input. |

No duplicate search/status/rebuild endpoints, new status endpoint, SSE, explanation endpoint or export endpoint. Existing M2 status remains the advisory path. M6 later exposes these paths through the existing Express gateway after C3/C5 review, preserving repeated parameters/typed errors; M3 does not implement that transport or promise the React feature is already accessible.

Proposed `RetrievalError` codes: `retrieval_invalid_request` / `retrieval_cursor_invalid` 422; `retrieval_cursor_expired` 410; `retrieval_generation_changed` 409; `retrieval_not_found` 404 for explicit detail; `retrieval_capacity_exceeded` 422; `retrieval_deadline_exceeded` 504; `retrieval_unavailable` 503; `retrieval_integrity_failed` 500. Dependency identity is a safe source-capability enum, not raw infrastructure detail. Preserve distinguishable C1/P4 codes/statuses in an allowlisted cause field (and existing auth/404 envelopes unchanged), rather than map everything to 502 or empty success. Unrecognized exceptions become sanitized 500. Ambiguous/no-evidence query results are not errors. Scope/integrity failure from an optional source is still fatal; degradation cannot conceal cross-tenant data.

Observability: ephemeral authorize/resolve/plan/search/graph/canonical/evidence/assemble/verify/serialize steps; safe structured durations, source-call counts, candidates considered/selected, ambiguity, scan-incomplete and omitted counts, output bytes, deadline/cancel results and scope/generation/strategy/policy/correlation IDs. These are M3 observations, not new AnalysisDiagnostic records, job lifecycle transitions or public event vocabulary. Avoid raw query/fingerprint metrics labels and high-cardinality sensitive data; detailed identifiers in access-controlled logs only. Existing logging/observer conventions are reused; do not introduce a metrics platform dependency.

REUSE READ ONLY: `analysis/architecture/{service,dto,model}.py`, public canonical provider; `graph/{query_contracts,query_service,query_repository,dependencies,source}.py`; `knowledge/{contracts,model,projection,dependencies}.py` public exports/providers; `delivery/contracts.py` value types; existing API exception handlers and delegation; existing test fixtures. **Do not modify** frozen M1/M2 contracts/model/reader/repositories/DI/APIs/tests, worker files, migrations, dependency locks, Express/React, ADRs, master or phase baseline. No new production dependency is currently justified. Shared composition/config additions are the only existing application-file changes proposed for later authorization.

### 7A.12 Three-batch implementation sequence

**Batch 1 — C3 foundation, exact resolution and controlled source adapters.** Create retrieval_contracts/configuration/adapters and the foundation of retrieval.py; add focused contract/adapter/resolution tests and public M6/P6 consumer fixtures. Implement exact published header and typed selectors, bounded scan states, optional lazy DI design, source identity tracking, seven-operation mapping and cursor wire proposal. Input: frozen public C1/P3/P4 fixtures and authorized context; output: validated C3 query/result/plan and resolved canonical selections, no published artifact. Measure public canonical paging and conditional final-probe/PIT costs under §7A.5's required-success gate in an explicitly authorized isolated environment; any comparison with the existing full-load port is measurement-only, not permission to substitute it for bounded paging. Do not choose production defaults from fixture values. No source excerpts, routes, persistence or queue work. **Exit/DoD:** owner accepts decisions below, C3 producer/consumer fixture review and freeze, passing required-success measurements under finite trial budgets and ownership/seam tests; no upstream contract change/private dependency needed. If the measurement gate fails or advisory status/final-probe/pin tradeoff is rejected, stop the affected path for a scoped owner decision, not a hidden implementation workaround.

### 7A.13 Test matrix, commands and quality gates

Required unit/contract tests: exact IDs versus duplicate names/namespace and scan-incomplete ambiguity; all six C1 document kinds; source scope/VersionScope/header comparisons; index UUID/generation mismatch across pages/independent searches/final probe; opaque cursors, forgery/wrong actor/policy/query/version, expiry and upstream typed errors; canonical-only pages with no fake generation; graph generations independent of search, original edge direction/all twelve dependency kinds/contains hierarchy; unknown hop distance; original five fact statuses, optional source fact/producer fields, duplicate/opposing/conflicting evidence, source-range field preservation; safe allowlist/deep immutability/serialization; deterministic digest and every byte/count boundary; required-minimum rejection versus disclosed optional selection; no full DTO/source dump or instruction execution.

Targeted refinement acceptance matrix (planned tests, not executed): assert zero C1 calls/ES initialization for canonical-only and canonical-anchored graph-only paths, and no probe caused by advisory status alone. Assert one final validation, not per-item probes, for lexical selection, zero-hit search, search-anchored graph and continuations carrying a search-derived selection; a correctly positioned normal final C1 read must not cause a duplicate probe. An allowed degraded response discarding all failed optional C1 dependencies must not probe that unavailable source. Account for reserved final-call/time/PIT costs, capacity rejection, pin expiry and final-check deadline failure. Successful complete retrieval with UNKNOWN advisory freshness must remain distinguishable from actual unavailable/failed reads and from CURRENT. Verify bounded cursor decoding before ports, per-page authorization, original non-renewing expiry, cumulative chain ceilings, upstream earlier expiry and rejection of oversized envelopes without session storage. Digest fixtures vary only scores/advisory status/timestamps/operational timing without changing semantic selections and must match; changed selected source bindings or meaningful coverage must change the semantic payload/digest. Do not assert wall-time-dependent replay equivalence. B1/B3 measurements must demonstrate required-success canonical cases within the declared envelope; returning incomplete does not pass that gate. A failing measurement must produce the scoped escalation evidence from §7A.5, not a private-interface workaround.

Security/API tests must use the real delegation dependency in addition to overridden-unit fixtures: invalid/missing service token, missing/forged actor/project, all four legitimate read roles, foreign version/entity/evidence selectors and returned forged dependency data, reauthorization on continuation. Assert optional factories and data ports are not invoked before authorization, never fetch prohibited source files, and every error excludes submitted secrets, cursor contents and validation payloads. No benchmark/fixture may weaken warning-as-error or frozen parser memory tests.

Real integration requirements: persist a validated canonical model into an isolated PostgreSQL schema; produce/accept real C1 through existing public M1 composition; retrieve through production ports and new M3 API; exercise independent connection generation switch during evidence resolution, final-probe failure, lost/expired/recreated index, actual P4 projection/traversal and canonical rehydration, ES/Neo4j outages independently, historical exact version and failed rebuild preserving canonical access. Prove M2 read pins survive normal provider commit, eventually expire when M3 cannot close them, and remain safe from cleanup. Check real PG statement/pool/lock timeouts and ES/Neo4j cancellation/server residual bounds; inspect outstanding work rather than assert elapsed time alone. Fake port tests validate consumer behavior only. Existing M2 tests are unchanged. Isolated services/credentials are explicit; unavailable infrastructure is BLOCKED, never a skipped test counted as acceptance.

### 7A.14 Proposed decisions, risks and owner approval package

**Decision status — owner approved, 2026-11-18:** following Master Agent acceptance in principle, the owner approved the refined M3 plan and decisions M3-D1–D6 below as the planning baseline. Recommendation/prerequisite wording in the table records the approved direction and continuing gates, not outstanding plan approval. This is not C3 implementation/consumer freeze, numerical-policy approval or authorization to change frozen sources. Existing ADR-0001 and frozen contracts remain authority; a separate ADR/change-control process is necessary only if an actual upstream amendment is later requested and authorized.

| Decision / actual problem | Options and tradeoffs | Recommendation / prerequisite |
| --- | --- | --- |
| M3-D1: durable versus request-time context | Stateless bounded read is smallest/has no recovery obligations; persisted bundles improve saved-result replay but require owned storage/retention/access lifecycle; new C0 work would reopen frozen ownership. | Stateless, no cache/tables/jobs; metadata-only initial C3, excerpts explicitly unavailable. Owner accepts scope at detailed-plan approval. Future saved contexts/excerpts require separate plan/safety approval. |
| M3-D2: canonical resolution without bulk lookup | Existing paged query services are safe/read-only but late matches cost scans; full P4-S1 document load is potentially large; a new upstream indexed/bulk port requires P3/P4 change control. | Owner accepts the existing public bounded-paging strategy and measured completion times at module freeze; no five-second M3 acceptance gate remains. Preserve honest incomplete discovery and fail-closed resource/integrity rules. Future optimization or operating-policy failures require scoped review, not private SQL, copied identity, caching or silent frozen edits. |
| M3-D3: status deadline and final active binding | Frozen status has no bounded timeout; an advisory observation is separate from retrieval validation. A conditional public C1 probe costs a call and possibly a TTL-bound pin, but needs no new frozen port. | Approve §7A.6's provenance-based search-only final validation rule, graph verification only when graph participates, and scope-bound/timestamped advisory freshness outside bounded composition. UNKNOWN is not CURRENT and does not itself make successful retrieval partial/failed. Reserve probe/PIT/finalization costs; no unconditional probe or synchronous unbounded status call. |
| M3-D4: public pin-release absence | Configured expiry preserves C1 but has temporary resource cost; draining all pages is unbounded and rejected; early release would require a separately controlled C1 amendment. | Accept the disclosed expiry-only limitation subject to measured bounded stream/page/chain/admission and outstanding-PIT policy. No production values approved here. If measurements cannot support it, stop/escalate; no private pin/ES access. |
| M3-D5: new-file composition versus modifying frozen M2 files | Extend old M2 files per early baseline (now breaches freeze); new C3 modules/routes plus additive root registration (small, isolates ownership). | Approve §7A.11 scoped new files plus additive router/config/example-env only. No frozen domain/API/C1 edits or new Express work. |
| M3-D6: bounded response policy and numerical limits | Disclosed partial views retain useful optional facts; hidden truncation or absence claims on incomplete scans are invalid. Exact caps affect latency/cost; same request alone cannot guarantee the same bounded selection. | Required minimum must fit; integrity/deadline/drift are fatal. Approve minimal stateless signed cursors with reauthorization, exact original request/source binding and finite non-renewing chain, plus §7A.8's semantic-payload-only digest guarantee. Measure byte/count/call/timeout/cursor/chain/admission limits separately; no score-as-truth or cross-source score fusion. |

**Owner approval record — 2026-11-18:** final plan acceptance closes the design decisions for the unchanged three-batch scope, metadata-only/stateless operation and inventoried additive composition; conditional final validation/advisory status and expiry-only PIT/read-pin handling; bounded canonical discovery with its measurement/stop-and-escalate gate; and minimal cursor/limited digest/coverage semantics. No plan-level design decision remains awaiting approval. This status-only instruction starts no batch; wait for an implementation instruction. C3 review/freeze, measured numerical policies and any genuinely required upstream change control remain separate gates below.

**Separate numerical-policy and implementation gates:** no new numerical operating values are proposed for approval in this pass. After implementation authorization, B1 must document finite experimental envelopes, required-success workloads and reproducible measurements; trial values are not production defaults. C3 producer/M6/future-P6 data-fixture review and owner freeze approval are required before dependent B2 implementation consumes it. Any measured failure triggers §7A.5's scoped escalation; any frozen public-port amendment needs its own change control. Production timeout/count/byte/call/cursor size/TTL/chain/admission and outstanding-PIT policies require measured owner approval before activation. B3 must independently prove real-service deadlines, source binding, expiry and regressions; module completion/freeze remains a later owner decision. None of these gates has passed merely because the design was accepted in principle.

Principal risks: canonical scan latency/memory and incomplete uniqueness; abandoned PIT/probe resource overhead; missing status deadline; fake cross-source atomicity/distance/currentness; deeply mutable canonical DTOs; high-fanout evidence/conflicts overwhelming output; secret-bearing arbitrary details; lazy DI accidentally initializing failed optional services; C1 pin commit or pool time not covered by total deadline; upstream limits mistaken for measured M3 SLOs. Each is paired with an explicit adapter/contract rule and B1/B3 acceptance test above. None is declared solved by a schema sketch. No existing source contract change is inherently necessary for the recommended bounded semantics; stricter status/early-release/fast arbitrary-ID requirements would be genuine upstream change-control decisions.

**Final owner freeze disposition — 2026-11-19:** M3 and its delivered C3 v1.0.0 are accepted/frozen. Earlier plan-time C3 review/freeze and module-acceptance prerequisites are closed by this explicit owner decision, not by claiming unperformed downstream reviews. The five-second canonical-resolution acceptance gate is withdrawn; measured completion is accepted. Production numerical policies/activation and future consumer integration remain separate operational/downstream responsibilities, not a reason to leave M3 marked incomplete. Existing runtime safeguards and frozen C1/P4 query ceilings remain unchanged.

### 7A.15 Acceptance, Definition of Done and implementation handoff

M3 acceptance requires: (1) owner-approved detailed plan/scoped composition, then C3 producer/M6/future-P6-fixture review and freeze; (2) exact authorized published version for every operation and continuation, no private/upstream writes; (3) canonical rehydration of projected facts with correct IDs/endpoints, honest bounded lookup ambiguity and owner-accepted completion measurements under §7A.0/§7A.5, without a five-second M3 latency requirement; (4) same search generation/index across every participating read and graph's independently pinned generation, final public checks only for participating sources under §7A.6 and typed restart conditions; (5) preserved actual provenance/ranges/static uncertainty/opposing evidence, no invented fields/confidence or source reads; (6) immutable validated C3, §7A.8's limited deterministic semantic digest and finite count/byte/call/time limits with approved operating profile; (7) explicit partial/no-evidence/incomplete/capability behavior, independent advisory freshness and no silent source fallback; (8) real cancellation, isolation, pin-expiry and PG/ES/Neo4j integration evidence; (9) existing M1/M2/P1–P4 contracts/source/tests unchanged except separately approved additive composition, with relevant regressions/quality results and carried failures accurately recorded; (10) explicit owner module acceptance, not automatic completion.

Definition of Done: all three batches implemented and reviewed; public-only import/ownership audit; C3 consumer/schema/golden and adversarial tests; production-port real integration, measured policy and safe configuration; exact verification commands/results/skips/failures/environment recorded; no unexpected diff or production activation; owner accepts remaining limitations and authorizes freeze. Future producer/storage/AI work is not counted as M3 completion. Owner acceptance/freezing is recorded above; no new run or independent live M6/P6 review is implied, and deployment-policy approvals remain separate from accepted module completion.

No runtime tests, benchmarks, source/configuration/dependency changes or implementation were performed. The targeted master/contract audit result and full-master inspection limitation are recorded in §7A.1; no relevant conflict was identified.

Implementation handoff: **M3 B1–B3 and delivered C3 v1.0.0 are COMPLETE / FROZEN by owner acceptance, 2026-11-19.** Preserve the existing dirty worktree and verified code; do not reopen frozen contracts without change control. ## 7B. P5 M4 Complete Architecture and Implementation Plan — Diagram Intelligence

### 7B.1 Planning status and scope

**COMPLETE / VERIFIED / OWNER-FROZEN, including C2 v1.0.0 — 2026-11-20 (§7B.24).** The approved §7B plan and D-M4-01–07 remain the design baseline. All four batches and real-browser acceptance are complete in the owner-amended essential scope. B1 evidence is in §7B.20; final combined/browser evidence is in §7B.24. No production activation, deferred-hardening certification or Windows production support is claimed. Implementation agents do not select another architecture/member set/font/protocol/limit/consumer boundary. Actual acquisition hashes and deployment guarantees remain distinct facts.

Scope remains four independent products `system_overview`, `component_architecture`, `dependency_map`, `data_flow`; canonical semantic construction, deterministic C2, isolated headless ELK, physically sealed immutable artifacts, M1-only publication/readiness, internal exact-version reads, safe JSON/SVG and reusable transient React presentation. M5 owns report preparation/PDF; M6 alone owns authenticated Express transport/product pages/routes/polling/retry and later saved workspaces. No new Express work, queues/workflow, persistent workspaces, source parsing, AI, canonical inference, graph/search prerequisites, extra templates, PNG/diagram PDF, cloud store or completed-work rebuild.

Exactly four sequential batches, no mandatory formal review between them; one comprehensive review follows B4. After owner authorization, successful specified experiments are executable acceptance tests, not another design phase or a new approval request for routine implementation. Security failures, failed defined contingencies, frozen-contract conflicts, new dependencies/privileges beyond the exact inventory or materially inadequate capacity require change control. This task itself executes none of those experiments. Historical refinement scope was §7B only; current approval is synchronized with summaries and §8. This section is the owner-approved M4 planning baseline.

### 7B.2 Executive summary and dependency sequence

Use **canonical PostgreSQL public paged reads** to construct a bounded, exact-version diagram input; apply four distinct deterministic view definitions; validate an immutable semantic DiagramSpec; send only its selected layout input to a credential-free, OS-isolated Node/ELK helper; validate LayoutSpec and prepare JSON/SVG primitives; physically seal and independently verify a generation; commit M4 acceptance metadata only through M1's guarded publication transaction. React consumes these prepared outputs, not raw analysis or a new authority. Browser arrival is never required for automatic diagram readiness.

`frozen canonical + C0 → B1 C2/query boundary + compatibility/physical-store experiments → B2 four builders → B3 isolated layout/sealed artifacts/M1 registration → B4 internal API/transient React presentation/export/acceptance → M5/M6 integration`. B1 implements/verifies the exact schemas, deployment and contingency choices specified below before dependent consumers; no pretending a mock seal is a production store. Overview work has priority through M1's existing release rule, not diagram-specific Celery priority. Each of the other three can succeed/fail independently after release. M1 alone applies the four-READY, same-version/digest report barrier.

**Release classification (no hidden optional prerequisite):**

| Classification / feature | Owner, reason and dependency |
| --- | --- |
| **M4 v1 — Mandatory:** four deterministic builders; semantic/layout validation; canonical paging/identity/coverage; headless isolated ELK; sealed spec/layout/SVG generation; committed staging and C0 publication; accepted-only exact/history/member reads; safe JSON/SVG; M5 prepared primitives; injected React adapter/node/edge/group/inspector and bounded focus/filter/expand/collapse/camera/structured alternative | M4, required for the four products, consumer contracts and immutable readiness. Depends on public canonical+C0, proven runtime/store and approved profiles. Four-ready report prerequisite evaluation belongs solely to M1, PDF to M5. |
| **M4 v1 — Optional, only if low-risk and non-blocking:** convenience legend controls and non-persistent manual node positioning | M4 presentation only, after mandatory stored LayoutSpec rendering works. Reuse pinned assets/contracts; no new persistence/security/deployment or consumer prerequisite. Mandatory group collapse/filter/focus/expansion uses stored geometry or the bounded M4 view service; no browser ELK worker is in v1. If changes require unapproved assets/profiles or impair core work, omit/defer. |
| **Deferred / Future:** persistent actor-private workspaces, saved/manual layout preferences, shared collaboration | M6 product planning/owner approval; no M4 workspace table/write DTO/API, VIEWER-save permission, migration or readiness dependency. Transient React state is not persistent storage. |
| **Deferred / Future:** graph/search-assisted enrichment, arbitrary query language, AI interpretation/descriptions, more templates, PNG/standalone diagram PDF, automatic completed-generation rebuild | Later explicitly approved module/phase; optional P4/C3 ports remain frozen and unused in mandatory builders. Rebuild would need separate M1 change control; PDF report is M5, not a diagram-PDF dependency. |

Design choices D-M4-01–07 are **RESOLVED / OWNER APPROVED** (§7B.18); the earlier proposal-awaiting-acceptance wording is superseded by owner approval. Empirical B1/B3 gates have binary pass/fail and specified contingencies, not deferred ordinary choices. The previously proposed browser-worker experiment is removed from the v1 inventory; stored/derived validated server LayoutSpec is the single presentation geometry contract.

### 7B.3 Repository reconnaissance — verified files, reuse and gaps

Actual P3 DTO source_range is an optional bounded integer mapping, not a guaranteed fully populated span. New installed hashes must come from verified upstream bytes during B1, never invented now.

### 7B.4 Frozen boundaries and reconciliation

Canonical model remains immutable authority in PostgreSQL; diagrams are version-bound presentation products, not new architecture. Express owns platform auth/RBAC/MongoDB; trusted service/actor/role/project delegation is reused, not replaced. All project roles read; no public system role. Python owns M4 persistence and computation. No cross-database joins/synchronization, raw source content, source reanalysis, AI interpretation, new canonical kind or stage. P4 graph/search outages cannot block canonical automatic diagrams. M1 alone owns logical runs/attempts/fences/leases/retry/dispatch/accepted-output orchestration/report prerequisites; M4 stages producer metadata, never implements a competing work lifecycle.

**Reconciliations, not master edits:** (1) master §16.5 conceptually includes layout metadata; C2 separates semantic DiagramSpec and geometry LayoutSpec under a single artifact descriptor, preserving the required association. (2) §§16.68/16.73 permit optional persistence/export; approved P5 direction includes immutable artifacts and JSON/SVG export; M5 can consume prepared vector primitives directly, without parsing SVG. The exact packaging/font/M5 producer interface is specified in §§7B.7/14 for approval; it is not a claim that M5 or C4 is implemented. (3) §§16.77/16.97 permit unresolved visualizations; initial product preserves unresolved/conflicts as referenced annotations/panels, never synthetic canonical nodes. (4) master §§17.80/17.90.38 general text-only renderer fallback is preserved; the already approved **P5 automatic product** has a strict four-ready barrier in frozen M1. M4 does not implement either a report fallback or a second barrier. (5) master organization wording is superseded by §0 project-primary scope. (6) baseline's three M4 batch sketch is refined to four complete batches by this brief, without changing module ownership. No actual normative contradiction requiring master/ADR modification was identified; physical isolation/runtime/export choices are finalized in this proposal, pending owner acceptance as a whole.

The current review deliberately **defers** the baseline's workspace-storage sketch; master §§16.69–16.70 treats saved positioning as future presentation preference, not canonical truth. No workspace ownership or write authorization is added. The phase baseline stays untouched; this scoped review record governs the proposed M4 release inventory. Shared GeneratedArtifactStore placement in the baseline was conceptual: implementing its public producer store in the new M4 package avoids modifying frozen `delivery/` internals; M5 consumes the port, not M4 repository classes.

### 7B.5 M4 internal architecture and ownership

Proposed cohesive package `backend/platform/diagrams/` follows existing domain-package style. `contracts.py` owns C2 public immutable schemas/read ports; `model.py` pure selection/identity/state values; `query.py` public canonical adapter and bounded view materialization; `builders.py` four pure builders and a fixed registry; `validation.py` validates canonical references, view semantics and geometry; `layout.py` isolates the ELK helper; `artifacts.py` is the reusable generated-artifact storage port/local implementation first needed here (future M5 consumes its public API); `persistence.py`/`repository.py` own diagram records/acceptance/read pins; `service.py` is the C0 handler/exact reader and transient bounded-view application service; `export.py` pure safe SVG/JSON; `dependencies.py` composes existing providers. Do not create separate modules for every schema/helper or extract a generic workflow/storage platform.

Automatic execution: receive revalidated C0 context → check pinned template/profile and cooperative liveness → read exact header and bounded canonical pages → construct `DiagramInput`/coverage → appropriate pure builder → validate semantic output → layout only selected visible scene → validate routes/geometry → prepare complete immutable members/manifest → seal and independently read back → durable M4 staging transaction **commits/closes** → return exact `PreparedOutput`. Later M1 independently opens its publication UoW → M4 local enlisted acceptance/readiness checks → M1 live-fence CAS and outer commit → only committed exact references become readable/report-eligible. Never keep a producer staging transaction open and assume M1 sees it through another connection.

Read/interaction: reauthorize exact project/version → resolve a committed generation/reference once → return bounded spec/layout/metadata → user focuses/collapses/filters within pinned semantics. Further server views take exact base generation plus validated view request and build a bounded derived view without writing M1 work; reuse stored geometry or use bounded M4 derived-layout execution when necessary; no durable HTTP layout job or auto-regeneration. Deferred complex traversal, only after separate future approval, delegates to frozen P4/C3 and carries its binding/final checks; no private traversal engine. This initial plan does **not** require graph-assisted execution to make any default diagram READY.

Contract ownership is split: `contracts.py` contains separate immutable semantic, layout and artifact descriptor models plus read/view DTOs; internal store/sealer and C0 publication adapters stay behind producer ports. No separate contract module per trivial record is needed. Runtime capability absence is not a new work state. New frontend components receive validated props/injected callbacks only; M6 alone owns authenticated transport, routes, polling/retry and product loading/error state.

### 7B.6 Four independent diagram specifications

Common rules: copy canonical logical IDs/record UUIDs/kinds/status/direction; eligible means established as a canonical record, not truth inferred by the diagram. Every semantic node/edge has actual evidence references (possibly honestly empty), canonical digest/scope and provenance links; no invention of a UUID from a name. No frontend/backend framework favoritism: real application boundaries and containment apply to Python, JS/TS and other recognized canonical content equally. Status `supported` is not silently rewritten as confirmed. Each diagram has its own view rules, template/builder version, profile, generation, validity and coverage. A low-evidence record can be displayed with its status; missing evidence is not magically repaired. Canonical completeness/unresolved/conflict warnings remain visible even when some detail is intentionally hidden.

**A. System Architecture Overview (`system_overview`, highest priority).** Answers “what are the major applications, internal services, important interfaces/stores/messaging and external integrations, and what established connections join them?” Default semantic nodes: application, frontend_application, service, worker, database, message_broker, queue, topic, external_service, external_api; include an endpoint/data_repository/controller/component/module only as an independently exposed boundary (exposes/routes_to/cross-owner interaction) or when a repository has no higher-level owner and it is required to represent an established link. Exclude library/configuration_component/unknown and implementation modules by default, but count exclusions. Prefer canonical applications as grouping roots, then direct canonical major children; never create a fake “backend” application from paths. Layer labels “Interfaces”, “Processing”, “Persistence/Messaging”, “External” are presentation categories based on kinds, not new containment. Important edge allowlist: depends_on, calls, exposes, routes_to, produces, consumes, connects_to, persists_to, uses; imports/extends/implements suppressed at overview level. Contains primarily establishes grouping, not a dependency arrow. No promoted transitive A→C edge just because A→B→C exists. Collapsed cross-boundary edge bundles may represent **only actual direct relationships** with full underlying IDs/kinds/endpoints retained; no new relation is asserted between owner applications. If no unambiguous owner exists, leave node ungrouped with a warning rather than attach it by guess. Defaults prefer architectural boundaries, not every UI component; user may focus a real frontend application to expand its canonical internals. Group/focus/important-interface selection is deterministic and versioned, without external ranking/LLMs. Validate boundary selection, bundle provenance/directions and unknown/multiple containment. Fixtures: Express+FastAPI+React monorepo with Python and JS services/controllers, worker/broker/database/external API; backend-only app; no app detector output but meaningful services; sparse model; wide disconnected multi-app model. Expected: real top-level boundaries/stores, no low-level component flood, no invented cross-app connectivity.

**B. Component Architecture (`component_architecture`).** Answers “which internal modules/controllers/services/workers/components/repositories belong to this application, what interfaces do they expose, and how are they related?” Eligible: applications/frontend_applications as containers; module, component, controller, service, worker, data_repository, endpoint; external dependencies/stores are boundary stubs only when the selected canonical slice actually references them. Default backend-first depth/layer choice is a view policy, not exclusion of Python or hidden frontend facts; frontend internals are a selectable bounded focus. Contains is the sole semantic parent/child source; namespace can supply an explicitly labeled **presentation-only** bucket, not an ownership claim. Multiple parents stay in side metadata; choose a deterministic layout parent by sorted logical ID and record non-selected parent references. Containment cycles are preserved as conflicts/annotations, not a recursive group graph. Internal edges: calls/routes_to/exposes/depends_on/uses/persists_to/connects_to/produces/consumes; imports is opt-in module detail; extends/implements are opt-in structural overlays with exact labels. Unknown “responsibilities” are omitted; labels may show only actual name/kind/allowlisted canonical description, not filename-generated summaries. Lazy parent/child expansion always binds exact base generation/version, preserving other node positions unless explicit layout requested. Fixtures: nested Django modules, FastAPI routers/controllers/services, Express controllers/services, React components, multi-parent containment, isolated module, cyclic containment. Expected: equivalent Python/JS canonical entities included, precise ownership annotations, no fabricated responsibilities or containment edges.

**C. Dependency Map (`dependency_map`).** Answers “what static dependencies exist, which direction do they point, and where are dependency cycles?” Eligible: all canonical non-unknown architectural kinds that participate in selected dependency relations, including module/library/configuration_component when detail permits; unknown canonical records remain visibly unknown if explicitly selected. **All twelve approved non-containment kinds** are available: depends_on/imports/calls/exposes/consumes/produces/connects_to/persists_to/uses/extends/implements/routes_to. Default architectural view can suppress source/module/library detail through explicit granularity, with per-kind counts; `contains` is a separately enabled hierarchy overlay and never traversed as dependency. Direction is always canonical source→target; dependents is an incoming view, not reversed stored edges. Bounded SCC detection runs only over the selected canonical dependency slice, sorted deterministically; SCC groups are presentation objects with exact members/edge refs, never new canonical entities. Label “cycle in selected view”; an incomplete slice cannot claim a repository is acyclic or give global coupling/risk scores. “Incoming/outgoing counts” say selected-view counts unless exhaustive data proves otherwise. Focused neighborhoods/filter sets have finite scan/retained/visible budgets; P4 may later accelerate complex traversal through its public requests without semantic changes. Fixtures: acyclic service/library graph, 2/3-node cycles, self-loop, parallel different-kind edges, containment-only structure, disconnected Python+JS dependencies, cut boundary. Expected: correct directions/kinds, separate containment and no false global impact/runtime claim.

**D. Data Flow Diagram (`data_flow`).** Answers “which statically evidenced interactions, persistence writes and messaging associations are established, and what remains unknown?” Presentation categories: process = canonical application/frontend_application/service/worker/controller/component/endpoint/data_repository as applicable; store = database/queue/topic/message_broker; external = external_service/external_api. These are categories over canonical kinds, not new entities or business processes. Edge rules: persists_to is an evidenced persistence association/write; produces is producer→message/channel according to actual canonical endpoints; consumes remains **consumer→channel** if that is the canonical direction, labeled “consumes from” (do not reverse it merely to draw apparent data movement). Calls/routes_to can be included as **static invocation/routing**, never proof of request payload, response, timing, actual execution or data schema.
