Connects_to is a separately styled connectivity-only overlay, not asserted data transfer. Depends_on/imports/uses/exposes/extends/implements/contains do not become flows. No invented read-from edges, responses, shared database, message names, producer/consumer pair, ordering/sequence or source/sink path. Display a “static interaction view—not observed traffic” legend and keep confirmed/inferred/conflicting separate. Group by real containment; no arbitrary trust boundary or actor (“User”) without a canonical record. When no qualifying edges exist, generate a valid empty/limited spec/layout/SVG with `no_established_data_flow`, actual counts and uncertainty; such an honest product can be READY and is not a failure. Partial qualifying input says “insufficient/partial evidence”, not “no flows exist”. Fixtures: producer/channel/consumer where consumes points toward channel; persistence edge; calls-only model; generic dependencies only; missing/ambiguous targets; conflicting route. Expected: exact static labels/arrows, no fabricated traffic, successful empty output where evidence is absent.

### 7B.7 Complete C2 v1 contract, identity/digest rules and consumer compatibility

**Normative design candidate for owner approval.** C2 v1 names the compatibility set. Separate wire fields `diagram_schema_version`, `layout_schema_version`, `artifact_schema_version` are each literal `"1.0.0"`; no redundant public version per DTO or extra C0 version. Template/builder/selection/identity-rule versions are `"1.0.0"`. Closed records below define **all fields**, required unless explicitly `?` (nullable required field, not omittable). Arrays are immutable tuples internally; no coercion, unknown fields, duplicate keys/IDs, NaN/infinity, unpaired surrogate, arbitrary metadata or null/empty conflation. Empty arrays are present. Definitions below are the source for Pydantic/JSON Schema/OpenAPI and JS guards, not permission to invent extensions in B1.

**Primitives:** `U` = lower-case hyphenated RFC UUID string; `H` = 64 lower-case SHA-256 hex; `N` = JSON integer 0..2^53−1 (bool forbidden); `T[k]` = scalar Unicode text ≤k UTF-8 bytes; `UTC` = UTC RFC3339 with six fractional digits and Z; `Ref` = `{record_type:entity|relationship|evidence|unresolved|conflict,id:U,logical_id:T[8192]}`, each pair copied from a real public canonical DTO. `Kind/Rel/Status` are the frozen Architecture Model v1 enums, including supported and all 13 relationship kinds. `Id` = domain-prefixed SHA-256 visual ID. All array/set lengths and text limits are checked in addition to §7B.15 limits. Canonical logical IDs never come from names/hash reconstruction. Display strings may be escaped/clipped only in LayoutSpec, not silently changed in copied semantic names.

| Closed C2 record | Exact fields and validation |
| --- | --- |
| CanonicalDiagramScope | `project_id,repository_id,analysis_id,snapshot_id,architecture_version_id:U; sequence_number:N>0; canonical_digest:H; architecture_schema_version:"1.0.0"; ir_schema_version:"1.0.0"`. No M1 lifecycle/time/config fabrication; additional source provenance is absent from this semantic scope, full actual C0 scope remains in descriptor. |
| SemanticProfile | `template_version,builder_version,selection_version,identity_version:"1.0.0"; fingerprint:H`. Fingerprint is hash of template rules/default view and semantic selection limits, not runtime/font/storage/time/concurrency. |
| DiagramSpec | `diagram_schema_version:"1.0.0"; scope:CanonicalDiagramScope; template:four frozen template IDs; profile:SemanticProfile; diagram_id:Id; view:ViewDefinition; nodes:[DiagramNode]; edges:[DiagramEdge]; groups:[DiagramGroup]; annotations:[Annotation]; provenance:[EvidenceProvenance]; membership_digest:H; coverage:Coverage; legend:[LegendItem]; semantic_digest:H`. No generation/attempt/storage/coordinates/React/UI state. |
| ViewDefinition | Discriminated by `template`. All include `root_ids:[Ref(entity)],entity_kinds:[Kind],statuses:[Status],relationship_kinds:[Rel],include_contains:bool,depth:N,selected_entity_ids:[Ref(entity)]`. Additional exact template fields: overview `layers:[applications\|interfaces\|processing\|persistence\|external],boundary_ids:[Ref(entity)]`; component `multi_parent:[{child:Ref(entity),parents:[Ref(entity)],layout_parent:Ref(entity)?}],containment_cycles:[[Ref(entity)]]`; dependency `direction:outgoing\|incoming\|both,sccs:[[Ref(entity)]],scc_scope:"selected_view"`; data_flow `semantics:"static_interactions",connectivity_overlay:bool,no_flow_reason:no_established_data_flow\|insufficient_evidence\|null`. Reject incompatible fields/kinds, arbitrary ELK params or path/query DSL. |
| DiagramNode | `id:Id,entity:Ref(entity),kind:Kind,name:T[4096],namespace:[T[1024]],status:Status,evidence:[Ref(evidence)],group_id:Id?,category:application\|interface\|processing\|persistence\|external\|implementation\|unknown`. Actual names/status/namespace preserved; no inferred responsibilities or arbitrary canonical metadata copied. |
| RelationshipMember | `relationship:Ref(relationship),kind:Rel,source:Ref(entity),target:Ref(entity),status:Status,evidence:[Ref(evidence)]`. Every bundle keeps complete real member facts; no direction reversal. |
| DiagramEdge | `id:Id,source_visual_id:Id,target_visual_id:Id,members:[RelationshipMember],label:T[256],bundle:bool`. Visual endpoints refer to existing node/group only, underlying actual endpoints remain in members. Bundle only same canonical kind/direction/status; opposing/conflicting relations stay separate. Label uses canonical kind/static mapping, never runtime claim. |
| DiagramGroup | `id:Id,kind:canonical_container\|namespace_bucket\|selected_scc\|presentation_layer,label:T[4096],container:Ref(entity)?,parent_group_id:Id?,visible_member_ids:[Id],membership_count:N,collapsed_default:bool,rule:T[128]`. Full canonical membership is in sealed catalog; layout-parent hierarchy acyclic, nonchosen canonical parents disclosed in ViewDefinition. |
| MembershipCatalog | `membership_schema_version:"1.0.0",diagram_id:Id,groups:[{group_id:Id,members:[Ref(entity)]}],omitted:[{record:Ref,reason:OmissionReason}]`. Contains only necessary grouping/omission refs, never whole canonical record payloads. Included in semantic digest through membership_digest; no circular digest field. Pagination derives from these exact sealed bytes. |
| EvidenceProvenance | `record:Ref(evidence),source_record_type:T[128],producer_id:T[256]?,producer_version:T[128]?,artifact_id:U?,source_fact_key:T[8192]?,path:T[4096],source_range:Range?,strength:direct_source\|framework_structural\|configuration\|dependency\|documentation,polarity:supporting\|conflicting`. Range is the exact frozen public source_range integer map, ≤16 keys of T[64] and N values; absence/incomplete values preserved, never invented/reinterpreted as a complete span. Paths are safe canonical provenance only; no filesystem/source read. |
| Annotation | `kind:unresolved\|conflict\|omission\|containment_cycle\|multiple_parents\|label_limitation,subject_ids:[Id],records:[Ref],evidence:[Ref(evidence)],reason:T[256],count:N?`. Referenced canonical uncertainty and evidence preserved, not resolved/inferred. `LegendItem={key:T[128],label:T[512],meaning:T[1024]}`; closed generated legends/static wording only. |
| Coverage | `view_state:complete_view\|bounded_view\|partial_view\|empty_view; source_partial:bool; selection_bounded:bool; scan_exhausted:bool; source_completeness:CompletenessSnapshot; counts:[Count]; reasons:[OmissionReason]`. `Count={collection:entities\|relationships\|evidence\|unresolved\|conflicts\|groups,kind:T[128]?,scanned:N,eligible:N?,represented:N,excluded:N?,omitted:N?}`. Unknown totals are null, never zero; CompletenessSnapshot copies exactly the public ArchitectureCompleteness fields: N total_inventory_artifacts/analyzed_artifacts/failed_artifacts/total_syntax_documents/total_syntax_facts/source_entities/source_relationships/source_unresolved_references/source_conflicts/architecture_entities/architecture_relationships/architecture_unresolved_relationships/architecture_conflicts and bool is_partial; unknown fields/schema changes rejected, not invented interpretation. `OmissionReason=granularity\|filter\|depth\|scene_budget\|bundle\|collapsed\|unavailable_source\|unsupported_detail\|label_clipped`. Partial and bounded flags may coexist. |
| LayoutProfile | `id:architecture_compound_v1\|dependency_layered_v1\|static_flow_v1\|simple_layered_v1; version:"1.0.0"; engine:"elkjs"; engine_version:"0.12.1"; runtime_version:"24.22.3"; font_profile:"dejavu_paths_2_37_v1"; fingerprint:H`. No user options; simple profile is the predeclared fallback, not an alternative engine. |
| LayoutSpec | `layout_schema_version:"1.0.0"; diagram_id:Id; semantic_digest:H; profile:LayoutProfile; unit:"milli_css_px"; nodes:[{id:Id,box:Box}]; groups:[{id:Id,box:Box}]; ports:[{id:Id,owner_id:Id,point:Point}]; labels:[LabelGeometry]; routes:[{edge_id:Id,sections:[{start:Point,bends:[Point],end:Point}]}]; glyphs:[Glyph]; font_notice:T[16384]; bounds:Box; fallback:none\|simple_layered; limitations:[T[256]]; layout_digest:H`. Complete exact visible IDs, valid group bounds and no architectural facts. Empty scene has zero box and empty geometry arrays, no fake node. |
| Geometry / text primitives | `Point={x:int,y:int}; Box={x:int,y:int,width:N,height:N}`, abs(x),abs(y),dimensions≤10^9 milli-px; nonempty nodes positive. Coordinates are absolute top-left CSS canvas axes, y down; group relations are semantic-independent. `Glyph={id:Id,font:regular\|bold,commands:[PathCommand]}`; commands are tagged M/L/Q/C/Z with respectively 2/2/4/6/0 signed integer milli-px operands. `LabelGeometry={id:Id,owner_id:Id,box:Box,display_text:T[4096],original_text:T[4096],glyphs:[{glyph_id:Id,origin:Point}],escaped:bool,clipped:bool}`. Glyph outlines are pre-scaled at fixed profile size, no consumer shaping/font lookup. |
| MemberDescriptor | `member:spec\|layout\|members\|svg; file_name:spec.json\|layout.json\|members.json\|scene.svg; media_type:application/json\|image/svg+xml; schema_version:"1.0.0"; size_bytes:N; sha256:H`. Exact mapping enforced, no client paths. |
| DiagramArtifactManifest | `artifact_schema_version:"1.0.0"; scope:C0.VersionScope; binding:C0.HandlerProfile; input_fingerprint:H; diagram_id:Id; generation_id:U; artifact_id:U; semantic_digest:H; layout_digest:H; membership_digest:H; export_profile:"svg_paths_v1"; members:[MemberDescriptor]; verification_version:"1.0.0"`. Exactly four sorted members, manifest does not hash itself. M1 attempts/fences/epochs/candidate and physical UID/FD/path stay solely in private staging record; full actual accepted C0 reference is validated against manifest + private record. |
| DiagramArtifactDescriptor | `manifest:DiagramArtifactManifest; reference:C0.ArtifactReference`. C0 reference/content/size binds exact manifest bytes; total member bytes separately bounded. Never expose internal proof/paths or assume supplied reference proves acceptance. |
| PreparedDiagram (M5) | `spec:DiagramSpec; layout:LayoutSpec; descriptor:DiagramArtifactDescriptor`. All scope/digests/IDs exact; bounded immutable data, no ORM/session/read handle, network/storage client or M1 control object. Layout provides direct vector glyph/route primitives; no SVG parser/PDF dependency. |
| DiagramViewRequest / response | Request `root_ids:[U],entity_kinds:[Kind],statuses:[Status],relationship_kinds:[Rel],include_contains:bool,depth:N,direction:outgoing\|incoming\|both,collapsed_group_ids:[Id],selected_entity_ids:[U]`; default empty filters mean all permitted by template, not a changed template. Response `base_generation_id:U,base_semantic_digest:H,request_digest:H,spec:DiagramSpec,layout:LayoutSpec`; derived, not accepted artifact/report input. Layout validated before reply; no pending geometry or persistent view session. |

**Artifact members are fixed:** manifest.json identifies spec.json, layout.json, members.json and scene.svg; no optional required member, host file/alias/latest, embedded font downloads or recursive manifest reference. `CandidateSeal.size_bytes/digests.content` describe manifest bytes; `digests.spec/layout` describe semantic/layout digests, not respective member-byte hash labels. Independently hash actual parsed records + exact bytes. Membership digest hashes the entire canonical catalog bytes. A requested format never substitutes another member/generation.

**Byte identity rules (fully selected):** let J be strict JSON over integer-only numbers, strings escaped only for quote/backslash/control U+0000–001F using lower-case \uXXXX, UTF-8 other scalar text, no whitespace/BOM/slash escaping; object keys sort by UTF-8 byte order (all defined C2 keys ASCII), no -0/floats. UUID lower-case; no Unicode normalization/casefold/percent decoding; decimals/floats in C2 are rejected, not rounded; frozen nested C0 values use their existing schema JSON representations. Set arrays sort by UTF-8 encoded stable IDs; meaningful direction/path/glyph/route order retained. Hash is lower-case SHA256(J([domain,version,payload])). Use domains `ss.diagram.id`, `ss.diagram.node`, `ss.diagram.edge`, `ss.diagram.group`, `ss.diagram.semantic`, `ss.diagram.members`, `ss.diagram.layout`, `ss.diagram.view`, `ss.diagram.profile`. diagram_id = `diagram://` + hash(scope + template + SemanticProfile fingerprint + normalized ViewDefinition request parameters **before** computed results). node ID `dn:` + hash(diagram_id + actual entity Ref); edge `de:` + hash(diagram_id + sorted real relationship Refs + visual endpoint IDs); group `dg:` + hash(diagram_id + group kind/rule + real container Ref/null + exact sorted membership Refs). Unequal collision is fatal integrity; no overwrite/random suffix.

Semantic digest hashes the complete DiagramSpec **excluding semantic_digest**, including membership_digest/provenance/coverage; excludes geometry/assets/generation/attempt/time/UI. Layout digest hashes complete LayoutSpec **excluding layout_digest**; integer coordinates obtained once by decimal ROUND_HALF_EVEN(raw_px ×1000), after finite/raw-bound validation, no subsequent compaction without translating all routes/ports/labels. Profile/font hash changes layout not semantic digest. Member hashes are raw SHA256(exact serialized bytes), manifest content likewise raw (not domain-hashed). Raw copied canonical text retained, unsafe display controls escaped per §7B.14. Derived views bind the same canonical scope + explicit request; no nondeterministic scores/time/iteration order.

**Compatibility matrix and exact ports:** only semantic/layout/artifact `1.0.0 / 1.0.0 / 1.0.0`, template/builder/rule v1, four template IDs, ELK/runtime/font/export profiles named above are accepted. Canonical schema v1 + frozen C0 v1 accepted; unknown schemas/profile combinations/fields rejected (422 at request, deterministic contract failure in handler; corrupt stored data 500), never best-effort downgrade. New schema/profile combinations require scoped change control; backward read support is not implicitly promised. Golden fixtures in §7B.17 are the compatibility acceptance examples, **not authority to change these fields during B1**. M5 `DiagramReadPort.read_exact(scope:VersionScope,reference:ArtifactReference)->PreparedDiagram`; member port `open_exact(scope,reference,member)->VerifiedMemberHandle` is in-process bounded/closable, never serialized. M6 API contracts §7B.12 and pure frontend interfaces §7B.13 use the same C2 records. All read ports revalidate acceptance/scope/descriptor and throw only the closed errors; renderer receives PreparedDiagram only, not lookup ability. C2 is complete **for owner approval**, not already frozen.

Remaining visual ID rules: port dp:hash(ss.diagram.port,[owner_visual_id,edge_id,source|target,ordinal]), label dl:hash(ss.diagram.label,[owner_visual_id,name|kind|edge|group,ordinal]), glyph gl:hash(ss.diagram.glyph,[actual_font_sha256,regular|bold,glyph_index,font_size_milli_px]); same tagged version1.0.0 J/hash rule. Port/label/glyph prefixes and diagram://,dn:,de:,dg: are the only Id forms, 64 hex suffix. Computed group/SCC/legend output is excluded from the initial request-ID seed to avoid a circular identity dependency; all computed result facts are included in semantic_digest.

| Producer/consumer combination | Accepted exact version/profile | Other case |
| --- | --- | --- |
| C0 work → M4 | C0 1.0.0, architecture schema1.0.0, exact registered four HandlerProfiles/configuration fingerprint | HandlerUnavailable before execution if unknown binding; malformed actual context permanent contract failure, never adapt/reopen C0 |
| M4 spec → layout/frontend/M5 | diagram1.0.0 + exact template/rules1.0.0 and semantic digest | Unknown/extra schema fields reject, no semantic reinterpretation |
| M4 layout → SVG/React/M5 | layout1.0.0 referencing exact understood spec digest; selected four profile IDs + pinned runtime/font toolchain | Geometry/profile/digest/ID mismatch reject; no host-font/automatic relayout fallback |
| M4 accepted packaging → M5 preparation/M6 delegated read | artifact1.0.0, exact four members + manifest and full matching committed C0 ref | Missing/corrupt/foreign member never fallback; typed exact unavailable/integrity |
| M4 derived view → reusable components/M6 host | view DTO exactly §7B.7/12 with same explicit base generation + spec digest; no artifact acceptance claim | Stale reply ignored, unauthorized/mismatched continuation rejected; never report input |
| Any new minor/major producer schema/profile | Not implicitly supported | Explicit compatibility/change-control review required before adding support; current fixtures remain regression cases |

### 7B.8 Canonical query, deterministic selection and bounded coverage

`DiagramSourceAdapter` calls actual public `get_architecture_query_service(session=<M4-owned read Session>)` provider, then `get_version/list_entities/list_relationships/list_evidence/list_unresolved/list_conflicts`; **no private repository, whole-document load_exact, guessed get_header or unbounded published-version discovery**. Exact public version DTO before/after checks project/repository/analysis/snapshot/version/digest/schema/sequence. Full analyzer/config provenance comes from real C0 WorkExecutionContext, not public-header fabrication. Configure owned read transaction read-only, statement_timeout and lock_timeout using remaining budget; cancel/close its owned DB connection on expiry. No borrowed publication Session is used for source reads. Public M1 `scope_for_record(record)` and `summary(scope,role)` are the actual summary seams; if no known scope/runtime, return nullable delivery_scope and unavailable-current-selection (or verified no-resource NOT_SCHEDULED), never downgrade accepted exact history or invoke inspect_requested/full load to fill missing fields.

Stream source collections at page size200 under §7B.15 counters; one entity pass for actual UUID/logical map/compact grouping, one relationship pass for true endpoint map and selected relations, one evidence pass retaining only selected actual references, one unresolved/conflict pass. Each public call checks deadline before/after and every100 records; copied DTO nested values validated/frozen, stable sorting by UTF-8 ID, duplicate mismatched identity fails. Verify public page totals/version/collection type, no fabricated absence after a bounded scan. At most two complete passes over any collection; no per-edge/full-version evidence scan, reusable global identity cache or private SQL. Source completeness is copied, not repaired.

Selection algorithm is **deterministic within the frozen §7B.6 semantic eligibility**: sort eligible roots by kind priority applications/frontend_application/service/worker/persistence/external then exact logical-ID bytes; canonical groups prefer actual contains parent, sorted logical ID for layout-only multi-parent choice, cycle annotations retained. Breadth-first expand real containment up to selected depth and node/group budget, sort ties by canonical logical ID; explicitly requested roots first, never rank by relevance/file path/framework/name. Include allowed actual direct edges whose selected/group/boundary mappings are valid; sort by kind then source/target logical-ID bytes then relationship logical ID. Bundle only same kind/status and visual source/target, retaining true per-member endpoints. Store necessary hidden membership/omitted references in members.json within its independent cap; no canonical record duplication. If a required root/ref/minimum cannot fit, fail capacity; otherwise requested view is explicitly bounded with accurate per-rule omissions. SCCs use sorted selected dependency slice, no global acyclic claim; §7B.6 direction/consumes/uncertainty rules remain unchanged.

Semantics/identities/digests are exactly §7B.7, not affected by page/input order, worker timing, physical generation or font. Intentional node/edge/group/label omission is disclosed; if totals cannot be known, null counts/source_partial with reason, never zero absence. Partial canonical source can produce valid partial/empty READY only if all represented mandatory refs validate and warnings survive report/UI. Failed generation has no successful coverage state.

Default products and views are canonical-only: no C1/C3/P4 initialization, no Neo4j/ES required and no graph/search-derived ownership/inference. Complex graph/search-assisted enrichment is deferred, not adapter work hidden in v1. Accepted subset focus/filter/collapse reuses geometry; expansion outside base retained index may page canonical data bound to the same version and produce a **derived unaccepted** spec/layout under the view budget. All view/header/pointer checks occur before response. API continuation contract is §7B.12; explicit historical generation remains readable while retained, active mode rejects replacement.

End-to-end cancellation at frozen public read seams: M4 dependencies create a request-owned read-only SQLAlchemy Engine/connection scope (NullPool, finite connect timeout), passed through existing public canonical/M1 coordinator/retirement factory constructors, never reuse/cancel a global engine or inspect private Sessions. A deadline watchdog tracks only that owned engine's live driver connections via public SQLAlchemy events and invokes the PostgreSQL driver's supported cancel API on expiry, then closes/disposes the owned scope; DB statements also have remaining statement/lock timeouts. M1's own bounded Session settings remain unchanged; the outer read watchdog prevents multiple individually bounded M1 summary queries exceeding the M4 operation budget. Only public scope_for_record/summary methods used, no discovery/retry/write on the read-only composition. Test blocked PostgreSQL call and verify pg_stat_activity no longer executing after expiry, plus no unrelated request cancellation. Unsupported safe driver cancellation or inability to construct this read-only public composition is a genuine contract/compatibility failure, not a thread timeout that leaves DB work running.

### 7B.9 Final ELK runtime/isolation architecture and B1 proof

**Selected design for owner approval:** Linux x86_64/arm64, Python 3.14 worker invoking a short-lived **Node 24.22.3 / elkjs 0.12.1** helper; exact npm lock/SRI and verified Node distribution hashes recorded during B1 acquisition. No npx, user module/script/ELK options, HTTP daemon, distributed queue or browser dependency. Node latest-v24 official security release is identified in §7B.3; this pin is a proposal, not an executed compatibility/security audit. Known exploitable dependency vulnerability at installation is a hard gate/change-control event, never silently choose a new version.

**Primary isolation:** non-setuid **bubblewrap 0.12.0**, unprivileged user + mount/PID/IPC/UTS/network namespaces, drop all capabilities, no-new-privileges, die-with-parent/new session; bind only an installation-owned minimal runtime root read-only (Node, its actual required loader/libraries, immutable entry/elk bundle), private bounded tmpfs scratch and proc for the child PID namespace. No home/repository/host /tmp/credentials/staging/sealed roots, Docker socket or whole-host /usr mount. Empty environment except fixed LANG=C.UTF-8/TZ=UTC/HOME=/nonexistent/NODE_OPTIONS="" and needed immutable runtime path; close all FDs except bounded stdin/stdout/stderr. Linux host kernel ≥6.1 with user namespaces enabled and cgroup v2 delegation is the selected deployment prerequisite, not a claim about this PC.

Launch under a dedicated worker-owned cgroup v2 child directory with memory.max/memory.swap.max=0, pids.max and cpu.max per §7B.15; entry moves into cgroup **before exec**. Launcher hardens with seccomp denying sockets/network, ptrace/process-vm, mount/setns/unshare after setup, privilege/device/keyring/BPF/perf syscalls; preserve only Node-required thread/file/memory operations. Seccomp profile is versioned installation data generated from the fixed deny policy, not runtime user input. Root contains no shell/interpreter except Node; input is JSON topology, never executable text. Node's possible V8 reservation means **do not use RLIMIT_AS as a substitute for resident cgroup memory enforcement**. CPU RLIMIT, wall watchdog, FD/file/core limits and bounded pipe readers are additional enforcement. Worker exposes no general privileged launcher; non-setuid sandbox cannot raise its permissions.

**Invocation/protocol fixed:** Python `ElkLayoutEngine.layout(spec_digest,profile,scene,budget,control)` executes immutable `layout_runtime/launch_elk.py` → bwrap → absolute Node entry `elk_layout.mjs`; no shell. One request/response per process; UTF-8 canonical JSON framed by 4-byte network-order length, no trailing frames. Request `{protocol_version:1,request_id:U,semantic_digest:H,profile:LayoutProfile,nodes:[{id,parent_id?,width,height,ports}],edges:[{id,source,target}],labels:[{id,owner_id,width,height}]}`, dimensions integer milli-px (helper scales to px); no extra fields. Response `{protocol_version:1,request_id,semantic_digest,profile_fingerprint:H,geometry:{nodes,groups,ports,labels,routes,bounds}}` or `{protocol_version:1,request_id,error:invalid_input|capacity|layout_failed}`. Parent checks binding, complete ID sets, nesting, endpoint/routes, finite raw geometry, payload and rounding; stderr never public. B1 materializes exact schema from these fields, not a new protocol design.

**Profiles/algorithm fixed:** all use ELK layered, DOWN, ORTHOGONAL, randomSeed=42 and sorted model/port/edge input. Overview/component use hierarchy INCLUDE_CHILDREN, node spacing 40 px/layer spacing 60 px/group padding 24 px; dependency groups selected SCCs and packs disconnected components; static-flow profile uses presentation categories without altering arrows. Node box width 240 px, base height 80 px, additional wrapped-label line 18 px up to 116 px; group header 24 px; font paths §7B.14. Layout disconnected components separately then deterministic shelf-pack sorted minimum visual ID at target 1600 px width, 80 px gutters; translate every route/port/label/group with its component. Never P4 compaction/stale routes. Scene aspect target ≤4:1 for fixture set is UX acceptance, not proof every graph can satisfy it.

**Predeclared contingency, no redesign:** a single `simple_layered_v1` attempt with hierarchy flattened for geometry only (semantic groups retained as annotated enclosing boxes, padding verified), same nodes/real edges/seed and POLYLINE routes, no fixed ports. It runs only for geometry/compound-layout failure under remaining original budget, records fallback/profile digest; do not retry an isolation violation, corrupt input, ownership loss or memory/OOM as a simpler layout. If both fail, generation fails, no READY/spec-only substitute. User namespaces/cgroup/seccomp unavailable: affected deployment disabled, provision a host satisfying these same requirements; **bare subprocess, Docker-socket access, less-isolated fallback or another engine is forbidden**. If required deployment cannot support them, security change control, not implementation-agent choice.

**Production compatibility/security experiment (original B1 certification matrix; deferred by owner amendment in §7B.16):** on intended worker OS, 10 cold +10 warm repeats per four templates, empty/partial/compound/multi-parent/cyclic/high-fanout/Unicode/disconnected and 500-node fixtures; repeat input permutations. Pass semantic digests equal and layout digests equal within the exact pinned profile/runtime across repeats; correct full geometry/route validation, no credential/env/FD/repository/store read or network/DNS/socket access in adversarial probe; malformed/deep/oversized input rejected without unbounded buffer. Inject cancel, EOF, worker death, child/grandchild CPU/memory/output saturation; parent kills entire cgroup/process group and reaps within 2s, no zombies/descriptors survive. Record actual cgroup stats/CPU/current & peak RSS/time, exact argv/schema/versions/hash and worker integration commands. A failing intended-deployment security proof blocks production capability enablement, not B2's pure semantic work. No READY based on mock experiment. Essential B1 compatibility evidence is separately recorded in §7B.20.

Helper topology record closure: node={id:Id,parent_id:Id|null,width:N>0,height:N>0,ports:[{id:Id,side:NORTH|SOUTH|EAST|WEST,order:N}]}; edge={id:Id,source:Id,target:Id}; label={id:Id,owner_id:Id,width:N,height:N}. Geometry fields mirror LayoutSpec nodes/groups/ports/labels/routes/bounds but label output supplies only boxes (glyph paths computed by trusted font preparation). Internal framing/protocol1 is not a new public C2 version. LayoutProfile fingerprint excludes its own fingerprint and hashes fixed options/node sizing/font/runtime asset hashes; unsupported variant/extra option rejected.

### 7B.10 Final local physical-sealing architecture and B1 proof

**Selected design for owner approval:** private local Linux ext4 or XFS filesystem; three OS roles: non-root `stacksense-worker` (existing worker identity mapped to this role), non-login non-root `stacksense-artifacts` (sealer owner), trusted operator `stacksense-artifact-gc` (may invoke exact GC entry as sealer). Fixed role names are deployment groups/accounts, numeric UIDs allocated administratively, not hard-coded in data. Read-only group `stacksense-artifact-read` includes API/worker reader roles. No network service, root Python worker, same-UID chmod seal or reuse of mutable ingestion store.

Root/admin installs immutable code/runtime under `/opt/stacksense/diagram-artifacts/`, owned root and not writable by worker/sealer; entry Python uses `-I`, sanitized env, fixed installed application dependencies (stdlib + existing SQLAlchemy/PostgreSQL driver, no worker-writable imports). `/var/lib/stacksense/diagrams/staging` worker-owned 0700 with exact ACL granting sealer read/traverse; `sealed` and all parents owned sealer, root dir 0750 group read, never worker-writable. Staging candidate dirs server-generated 0700, no shared world-writable parent. Worker invokes **only** `sudo -n -u stacksense-artifacts /opt/stacksense/diagram-artifacts/seal-generation`, fixed no argv, JSON descriptor via bounded stdin; root-owned sudoers exact command, env_reset/no SETENV, no wildcard shell/interpreter privilege. Launcher validates fixed executable/role/paths; sealer executable is not setuid Python. Deployment itself needs admin provisioning, never runtime root app.

Seal request is private `{protocol:1,attempt:C0.AttemptIdentity,generation_id:U,artifact_id:U,members:[{member,sha256,size_bytes}],manifest_sha256:H,manifest_size:N}`; file locations are derived solely from closed UUID/member enums under owned roots, never caller paths. Four members + manifest only. Before copying, sealer holds a sealer-owned per-generation flock (not removable by worker), reads the exact committed M4 STAGING row using its own read-only M4-only DB credentials from root-installed 0400 configuration, and validates full request/attempt/hash bindings. Layout never sees those credentials. DB read is outside M1 publication and bounded to1s. GC uses the same lock and rechecks DELETING reservation; tombstoned/accepted/foreign identities reject sealing, preventing stale worker recreation after GC. Sealer opens descriptor-relative/no-follow, rejects non-regular files/nlink≠1, source escape/mount/symlink/hardlink races, hashes predetermined bytes while copying into **fresh sealer-owned inodes** in a private 0700 directory. No writable sealed FD is inherited/returned. Fsync files, full independent second-pass hash/type/member-set verification, file mode 0440; atomic final placement using Linux renameat2 NOREPLACE, directory mode0550 and parent fsync. Existing final ID with equal complete bytes/proof is idempotent; unequal or incomplete generation is conflict, never overwritten/reopened. Response contains exact immutable descriptor/hash/size/proof version; physical paths/FD/permissions never C2 DTO.

**Predeclared filesystem contingency:** if renameat2 NOREPLACE is unsupported but directory-relative no-follow and durable fsync/exclusive mkdir are proven, create final generation directory with exclusive mkdir mode0700, copy complete fresh files, fsync/verify, write complete marker with exclusive create, then chmod0550/fsync. Public reads remain PostgreSQL-accepted-only, so incomplete physical directories cannot become visible/READY; an existing partial generation is never resumed and new M1 attempt gets new identity. Same proof suite must pass. If filesystem cannot enforce permissions/fresh-inode isolation/fsync/no-overwrite, capability disabled; provision ext4/XFS with selected identities. fs-verity/chattr/cloud alternatives are **not implicit fallbacks**; changing privilege model/store needs change control.

**Cleanup authority fixed:** normal worker seal entry has **no delete/update/accept/chmod verb**. Trusted operator alone may invoke root-owned `gc-generation` as sealer, taking a single exact generation UUID and validating the durable M4 deletion reservation with its own cleanup DB identity/public M1 retirement reader; no trusted worker-supplied proof or prefix. Separate operating role, code/config and credentials inaccessible to worker/layout. Accepted/current/historical/report-referenced generations are retained indefinitely in v1; no accepted-delete path exists. Pins block orphan deletion. GC follows §7B.11 retirement/locks/deletion reservation, checks again before exact unlink; unexpected file/link/member aborts, no recursive broad delete. Privileged sealer/admin/kernel/DB-authority compromise is outside ordinary-worker immutability guarantee; disk corruption is detected by exact reads. No claim C0 SQL atomicity prevents storage hardware loss.

**Production physical-sealing proof on intended OS/FS (100-race certification deferred from B1 by §7B.16):** record role/ACL/mount/kernel/parent permissions and installed code ownership; retain writable staging FDs and race path/hardlink/symlink/rename/copy for 100 attempts, then try write/truncate/reopen/chmod/unlink/replace through every worker path and seal entry. **Pass: zero mutation/deletion of independently hashed sealed bytes**, no writable sealed FD or route to GC/general sudo. Race same generation seal: at most one new complete artifact and exact idempotent replay, unequal bytes rejected. Kill each copy/member/manifest/fsync/placement/DB-stage boundary; incomplete bytes never produce a verification proof/accepted read. Real separate PG connections test acceptance/cleanup/pin races, forged deletion request, live/non-retired attempt, accepted history. Require corruption detection despite unchanged digest labels. A disproved production security assumption requires change control, not a same-UID production fallback. B3 deployment acceptance retains C0's actual immutability requirement; the B1 application-only experiment does not certify it or block B2.

### 7B.11 Database, staging, publication, exact reads and cleanup

**Additive schema fully selected:** three new SQLAlchemy models on established Base; PostgreSQL UUID/TIMESTAMPTZ/JSONB, digest CHAR(64) CHECK lower-case hex, nonnegative BIGINT sizes/counters, all identity fields NOT NULL. No M1/P3/P4/Mongo FK or table queries. Migration `backend/infra/migrations/versions/7d4a6c2e910b_create_diagram_artifacts.py`, revision `7d4a6c2e910b`; inspected current predecessor `26c95d71b603`. At implementation verify actual sole head remains that revision; if a new valid descendant exists, attach this **new** migration to actual head (bookkeeping, not redesign). Branch divergence/legacy schema conflict requires change control, never edit frozen migration.

| Table / keys | Complete stored fields and invariants |
| --- | --- |
| diagram_resource | `id UUID PK; project_id,architecture_version_id UUID; template VARCHAR(32) CHECK four templates; canonical_digest,input_fingerprint,binding_fingerprint CHAR(64); scope JSONB; binding JSONB; accepted_generation_id UUID NULL; created_at TIMESTAMPTZ`. UNIQUE(project_id,architecture_version_id,template,input_fingerprint,binding_fingerprint); composite scoped FK(pointer,id)→generation(id,resource_id), DEFERRABLE INITIALLY DEFERRED. UUID resource identity = UUIDv5(NAMESPACE_URL, "stacksense:diagram-resource:1:" + raw SHA256(J([scope.semantic_identity(),binding,input_fingerprint]))). Persistence identity is not canonical architecture logical identity. |
| diagram_generation | `id UUID PK; resource_id UUID FK RESTRICT; project_id,architecture_version_id UUID; template VARCHAR(32); work_id,attempt_id,candidate_id,artifact_id UUID; attempt_ordinal,fence BIGINT; dispatch_epoch UUID; attempt_snapshot JSONB; input_fingerprint,binding_fingerprint CHAR(64); state VARCHAR(24); manifest JSONB NULL; manifest_sha256,spec_digest,layout_digest,membership_digest CHAR(64) NULL; manifest_size,total_member_bytes BIGINT NULL; seal_proof JSONB NULL; accepted_reference,acceptance_context,retirement_snapshot JSONB NULL; deletion_nonce,deletion_actor UUID NULL; created_at,sealed_at,accepted_at,deleting_at,deleted_at TIMESTAMPTZ NULL except created_at`. Unique candidate_id/artifact_id/attempt_id and (id,resource_id); all scope/profile fields match resource snapshot. Seal proof is private fixed {verification_version,manifest_sha256,member_set_digest,physical_object_id,sealed_at}, no external IO in publication. |
| diagram_read_pin | `id UUID PK; generation_id UUID FK RESTRICT; actor_id UUID NULL; purpose VARCHAR(16) CHECK api\|report\|view; created_at,expires_at TIMESTAMPTZ; token_hash CHAR(64)`. Nonempty lifetime≤§7B.15; actor null only trusted internal report consumer. No workspace/session data. |

Generation state CHECK `STAGING|SEALED_VERIFIED|ACCEPTED|DELETING|DELETED`; immutable identity fields once inserted, seal fields immutable after SEALED_VERIFIED, ACCEPTED is terminal and cannot be updated/deleted. PostgreSQL trigger enforces transitions STAGING→SEALED_VERIFIED/DELETING, SEALED_VERIFIED→ACCEPTED/DELETING, DELETING→DELETED; state-specific NOT NULL/checks forbid accept without full proof/reference/acceptance_context. Physical deletion preserves tombstone, never removes accepted history. Resource pointer may reference only same scoped ACCEPTED generation (deferred constraint trigger). Index resource(project_id,architecture_version_id,template), generation(resource_id,accepted_at DESC,id DESC) WHERE ACCEPTED, generation(state,created_at,id), pin(generation_id,expires_at). No mutable content in metadata or general JSON updates after seal. Migration upgrade/rollback tested isolated PG; downgrade refuses if accepted artifacts/pins exist, never silently drops provenance. Runtime role has no DDL/trigger-disable ownership on new tables; deployment migration role owns them.

**Exact producer sequence:** receive actual WorkExecutionContext, revalidate all scope/profile/input/bundle/work/attempt/fence/epoch/candidate; lookup/insert one attempt staging row and generation/artifact UUIDv4, commit initial private STAGING. Public canonical read → deterministic spec/catalog → isolated layout/fonts → safe JSON/SVG → seal/full independent readback → commit SEALED_VERIFIED metadata on a separate producer connection → return PreparedOutput with exact candidate size/digests. Replay same attempt returns only same fully verified immutable descriptor, never new bytes under old identity; changed payload is conflict. Check C0 control/deadline every bounded phase; M1 owns stop/lifecycle/retry.

M1 alone opens outer UoW. Producer publication factory receives only GuardedEnlistedSession; locks resource then generation by UUID, checks full PreparedOutput/PublicationContext/attempt/profile/fingerprint/seal metadata, no DELETING/foreign or altered descriptor. Stages accepted reference/private acceptance_context/marker and pointer in same physical transaction; validate_ready returns exactly that reference using **local metadata only**. No storage hash/render/network/child work, commit/rollback/close/callback or M1-table access in enlisted port. Guard misuse poisons publication even caught; M1 final live-fence CAS+commit is sole public visibility. C0 public receipt fields used unchanged; failed commit leaves private candidate. All-four report barrier and overview-release/dispatch/recovery remain M1-only.

**Readability:** READY requires committed ACCEPTED reference plus valid immutable packaging; work state is a separate optional C0 WorkSummary. Availability `NOT_SCHEDULED|WAITING|BUILDING|READY|FAILED|CANCELLED|UNAVAILABLE` never replaces M1 lifecycle; retained ready bytes may coexist with work failure/building. Exact historical read checks project/version/template/actual reference and full parsed descriptor/hash/size/schema, not live original lease/current attempt. Acquire durable pin + read handle, rehash selected member into bounded verified spool **before** response headers; full read_exact verifies spec/layout/members/SVG and recomputes semantic/layout/catalog digests. Revalidate committed record and active pointer where request mode was active; pinned historical mode must not silently move. Cancellation closes handles/pin; no mutable alias. Corruption→typed integrity/unavailable, never substitute generation or alter canonical architecture.

**Cleanup exact algorithm:** trusted operator only, no new scheduler. First public AttemptRetirementReader observation, before producer locks; require retired attempt and no publication receipt. Then own PG transaction locks resource→generation→pins; reject any accepted/reference/pointer/report-eligible/pin, grace not elapsed or live writer; mark DELETING + delete reservation proof and commit. Separate gc entry takes the same protected per-generation sealer lock, rechecks reservation, independent public retirement and no acceptance/pins; physical stale-writer access cannot affect fresh sealed inodes, all old compute children must be reaped. Delete only exact unaccepted generation via protected helper; commit DELETED tombstone. Pin acquisition/publication rejects DELETING; accepted transition irreversible, races have one winner. Expired pin removed only after max stream lifetime and handle/process termination, not clock expiry alone. Sealed orphan with missing DB row requires **verified manifest + complete actual attempt identity** and public retirement; reconcile into STAGING orphan row under unique candidate before same deletion procedure. Missing/corrupt/unattributable proof means retain/quarantine, report operator action, not guess or delete prefix. Staging garbage without identity only operator quarantine after worker drain; no automated sweep. Disk pressure blocks new admission instead of deleting accepted history.

**Failure windows:** kill during copy/manifest → unaccepted incomplete; seal before final staging commit → restricted attributable orphan; staging before publication → private verified; ambiguous M1 commit → read committed receipt via public/M4 exact accepted state, no guessed republish; M1 retry uses fresh attempt/candidate, successful old bytes unchanged. Only existing OWNER/ADMIN failed-work `delivery/retry` with exact work UUID/idempotency is used; no diagram alias/completed successor/profile migration/cascade or additional queue.

M4 acceptance_context is a producer-local closed record {transaction_id,attempt_id,reference}; it is **not C0 PublicationReceipt**. M1 creates/owns that receipt after producer acceptance; M4 cannot mint its receipt_id or require a callback to copy it. Actual C0 receipts are consumed only when supplied through public retirement/barrier interfaces. This prevents an impossible producer-before-M1-receipt dependency.

The sealer read role has SELECT only on M4 resource/generation metadata; the GC role can execute only its scoped M4 cleanup and public M1 retirement adapter queries, no arbitrary worker-supplied SQL. Existing trusted publication coordinator/database authority is not expanded. Per-generation sealer lock + irreversible DB tombstone prevents a stale seal call recreating a GC-deleted candidate; no M1 transaction waits for this external lock. Root-installed deployment db-readonly.json contains only this least-privilege sealer connection, never worker/admin credentials; actual secret provisioned by operator, not committed.

Producer failure mapping is fixed: actual C0 ExecutionStopped (stop/ownership/deadline) propagates unchanged to M1; diagram_limit_exceeded and helper CPU/memory/wall exhaustion→RetryClass.CAPACITY; invalid canonical/schema/unsupported profile→PERMANENT; descriptor/hash/geometry/collision or physical-isolation violation→INTEGRITY; bounded DB/network/disk-temporary outage/admission busy→TRANSIENT, only under M1 pinned retry policy. Return public DeliveryExecutionFailure(DeliveryFailure(code=<safe code>,retry_class=<above>,source_capability="diagrams")); no custom retry counters/lifecycle or endlessly retrying deterministic failures. Unexpected error safe diagram_internal_error/PERMANENT until investigated, no raw text.

### 7B.12 Exact internal API, DTO, authorization, cursor and error contract

Prefix `V=/api/v1/architecture-versions/{version_id}`. All endpoints require explicit UUID project_id + existing trusted delegated-service authentication/context and project membership, validate canonical ownership **before** opening store/runtime. OWNER/ADMIN/DEVELOPER/VIEWER may read/build transient views; no persistent write or VIEWER-save scope. Existing M1 retry endpoint alone enforces OWNER/ADMIN and eligibility. Reauthorization on every request/page; no credentials/query DSL/path/actor override in bodies. Cross-project/unknown version returns public not-found consistent with frozen existing authorization handler, never record leaks.

**Closed DTOs:** `DiagramSummary={template,canonical_scope:CanonicalDiagramScope,delivery_scope:C0.VersionScope?,work:C0.WorkSummary?,availability:Availability,readability:unknown|verified|unavailable,verified_at:UTC?,reference:C0.ArtifactReference?,coverage:Coverage?,counts:{nodes:N,edges:N,groups:N}?,capabilities:{read:bool,view:bool,json:bool,svg:bool,completed_rebuild:false},reason:ErrorCode?,observed_at:UTC}`. This uses actual WorkSummary imported from frozen api_contracts, not a C2 work lifecycle. `DiagramCollection={canonical_scope,items:[exactly four summaries in frozen template order]}`. Pending summaries do not invent a full C0 scope. `GenerationSummary={generation_id:U,reference:C0.ArtifactReference,accepted_at:UTC,coverage:Coverage}`; `GenerationPage={scope:CanonicalDiagramScope,template,items:[GenerationSummary],next_cursor:T[4096]?}`. `MemberPage={generation_id:U,semantic_digest:H,group_id:Id?,items:[{record:Ref,reason:OmissionReason?}],total:N,next_cursor:T[4096]?}`. Descriptor/spec/layout/view records exactly §7B.7. `ErrorResponse={error:{code:ErrorCode,message:T[256],details:{dimension:T[64]?,actual:N?,limit:N?,request_id:U?}}}`; no raw input/exception/SQL/path/secret. All keys required with explicit nullable fields unless frozen nested C0 schema says otherwise; generated validation error sanitized into this wrapper by owned router, not global frozen handler changes.

| Method / path | Exact request → response |
| --- | --- |
| GET `V/diagrams` | project_id →200 DiagramCollection, including honest NOT_SCHEDULED/UNAVAILABLE per-template values. Uses public get_version, coordinator.scope_for_record/summary; runtime absent → nullable delivery scope, no full source load. |
| GET `V/diagrams/{template}` | project_id, optional generation_id:U →200 DiagramSummary. Without generation resolve M1 current accepted ref once, expose exact ID; no newest-timestamp resource selection. |
| GET `V/diagrams/{template}/generations` | project_id, limit1..200 default50, cursor? →200 GenerationPage, accepted history only. Scope/template ordered by accepted_at DESC,id DESC and captured high-water. |
| GET `V/diagrams/{template}/generations/{generation}/spec` | project_id →200 exact validated DiagramSpec, Content-Digest/ETag binds actual member bytes. |
| GET same `/layout` | project_id →200 exact validated LayoutSpec; spec/digest/IDs verified. |
| POST same `/view` | project_id + exact DiagramViewRequest →200 DiagramViewResponse. Reuse matching base geometry; if topology changes use same isolated layout under read budget, no persisted artifact/work/report-ready claim. Unknown selected root404, excluded unsupported policy422, no implicit pending layout. |
| GET same `/members` | project_id, group_id:Id? OR entity_id:U? (mutually exclusive), limit1..200 default50, cursor? →200 MemberPage from exact accepted members catalog. Neither selector means current selected/ref inventory, not arbitrary canonical DB search. |
| GET same `/export` | project_id, format=json or svg →200 `application/json` prepared {spec,layout,descriptor} or exact sealed `image/svg+xml`; no font lookup/re-render. Unknown format422, no fallback. Verified bounded bytes before headers; safe attachment name diagram-{template}-{generation}.json/svg, nosniff/private,no-store; Content-Length, SHA-256 Content-Digest, quoted ETag; abort closes pin/spool. No Range support (reject416 using same safe wrapper), no conditional304 bypass of acceptance/auth. |
| REUSE POST `V/delivery/retry` | Exact frozen RetryRequest + project_id, existing 202 RetryAccepted; failed work UUID/idempotency required. No new retry alias, generate/rebuild/scheduler/workspace route or profile override. |

**Cursors fully selected:** two-part base64url(J(payload)) + "." + base64url(HMAC-SHA256(secret,payload_bytes)); no padding, fixed domain `ss.diagram.cursor.1`, random ≥32-byte deployment secret, key_id allowlisted. Payload closed `{v:1,kid:T[64],operation:history|members,actor_id:U,project_id:U,version_id:U,template,resource_id:U,generation_id:U?,semantic_digest:H?,request_digest:H,high_water:{accepted_at:UTC,id:U}?,after:{accepted_at:UTC?,id:U},issued_at:N,expires_at:N,mode:active|exact}`. HMAC covers complete raw payload and domain; validate signature constant-time before trusting fields, bounded decode/no arbitrary SQL offset; expiry absolute10min, no renew chain. Bind page limit/selectors/filter/sort through request_digest; cap token4096 bytes. History initial selected resource/high-water retained, exact generation/member cursors retain same generation/spec. Active continuation rechecks pointer after backend/read mapping and rejects drift409, no silent restart. Explicit historical exact mode not invalid merely because current pointer changed; unavailable pinned bytes typed410 for continuation, 503 on direct exact read. Cursor possession never auth. No unbounded server session; short durable pins only during active read, expiry cannot authorize history deletion.

**Errors closed:** `diagram_authentication_required401; diagram_forbidden403; diagram_not_found404; diagram_not_ready409; diagram_scope_mismatch409; diagram_generation_changed409; diagram_cursor_invalid422; diagram_cursor_expired410; diagram_generation_unavailable410` (continuation only); `diagram_input_invalid422; diagram_contract_unsupported422; diagram_limit_exceeded422; diagram_range_unsupported416; diagram_runtime_unavailable503; diagram_store_unavailable503; diagram_source_unavailable503; diagram_timeout504; diagram_integrity_error500; diagram_internal_error500`. Message constant by code; details allowed only safe counters/request ID. Unsupported deployment/profile disabled capability503, malformed requested schema422, corrupt stored unsupported schema500. Uncaught exceptions safe500, never translate all failures to502. M6 relay must preserve all status/media/repeated query/abort semantics and binary limits later; no Express work here.

**Owned DI:** `get_diagram_source/get_diagram_repository/get_generated_artifact_store/get_diagram_reader/get_diagram_service/configured_diagram_registrations`; M4-owned staging/read Sessions, public canonical provider accepting owned read Session, clocks/budgets and real store/engine. Existing app composition appends exactly four explicit registrations alongside search, same API/worker root. Registration binds exact full ProducerProfile config hash (§7B.15), no memory production default. Disabled operational flag or failed capability probe leaves these bindings unavailable honestly; owner plan approval authorizes the described design/defaults, **does not assert runtime proof or enable production**. Capability probe checks package hashes/OS principals/bwrap/cgroup/FS permissions/assets and migration; it cannot replace B1/B3 security evidence.

History continuation uses mode=active and pins the initial accepted generation/resource plus high-water; member continuation uses mode=exact from its generation URL. Every issued continuation has a non-null generation_id/semantic_digest; empty/no-accepted history has no cursor. Members sort by (record_type,id UUID bytes), history by accepted_at/id; only UUID last keys enter cursors, not long logical IDs.

Read capability is independent of new-build capability: previously committed exact-generation spec/layout/member/export reads validate stored C2/profile/digests and need neither a running Node helper nor installed font lookup (prepared paths are self-contained). Disabling new M4/C0 dispatch or helper availability cannot silently downgrade a valid exact historical artifact to not-ready. If M1 current-scope selection is unavailable, default summary reports UNAVAILABLE/runtime reason rather than guessing a current resource; NOT_SCHEDULED only when no accepted M4 resource is verified. Exact-generation URLs remain readable, and history/current requests that cannot choose unambiguously return the documented conflict/unavailable rather than newest-timestamp fallback. No new source-of-truth pointer is introduced.

Summary's closed schema additionally requires `readability:unknown|verified|unavailable` and `verified_at:UTC|null`: ordinary metadata-only status sets unknown and the actual last seal/readback timestamp (null if none); it does not initiate a full four-artifact read or assert storage is healthy now. READY records committed, previously verified acceptance; verified readability may be claimed only after bounded exact bytes validation at the reported timestamp, unavailable only from an observed exact read failure. Returned data/downloads always undergo the mandatory fresh exact verification before exposure. This separates accepted lifecycle from current physical readability without a new public work state or database lifecycle.

### 7B.13 Exact M4/M6 frontend ownership and presentation interfaces

**M4 owns pure reusable presentation; M6 owns live transport/product.** No Express, App.jsx, navbar/global route, apiClient.js/graphService.js, existing P4 graph canvas/reducer/hooks/workers/protocol changes. No browser layout worker in M4 v1: accepted/derived LayoutSpec is authoritative geometry; browser relayout deferred, not a routine implementation option. Render prepared glyph/path geometry without host font shaping; accessible text/full original labels remain available. M6 supplies authenticated data and server callbacks, polling/retry/error/delivery UX, page/navigation and later persistent workspace design. Component-local busy/error/empty state only for its injected operations.

**Mandatory pure APIs:** `validateDiagramSpec(value)->Promise<DiagramSpec>`, `validateLayoutSpec(value,spec)->Promise<LayoutSpec>`, `validateDescriptor(value,spec,layout)->Promise<DiagramArtifactDescriptor>` reject closed-schema/version/scope/digest/ID/range violations. `toReactFlow(spec,layout,viewState)->{nodes,edges}` returns stable IDs/canonical refs, group parent positions converted from absolute LayoutSpec (never double translation), dedicated prepared route edges and glyph paths. No topology/semantics/digest rewrites, ELK, mutable input or network. `DiagramView` props exactly `{prepared:PreparedDiagram,requestView:(DiagramViewRequest,AbortSignal)=>Promise<DiagramViewResponse>,requestMembers:(MemberQuery,AbortSignal)=>Promise<MemberPage>,onSelect:(Ref|null)=>void,onExport:(json|svg)=>void,onError:(ErrorResponse)=>void}`; callbacks injected, no auth client imports. An embedding host not needing expansion may provide callbacks returning typed capability unavailable, not fake data; M4 tests exercise fully working bounded callbacks against its internal service fixtures.

`MemberQuery={generation_id:U,group_id:Id?,entity_id:U?,limit:N,cursor:T[4096]?}` matches §7B.12 mutual exclusion. `DiagramCanvas` gets `{spec,layout,viewState,onSelect,onFocus,onToggleGroup}`; exported cohesive node/edge/group renderers from this canvas accept validated geometry/ref/status callbacks only. `DiagramInspector` gets `{selection:Ref?,spec,members:MemberPage?,busy:bool,onClose,onFocus,onRequestMembers}`; actions before paged/collapsed evidence, no auto-loading 200 items. `useDiagramView` owns transient `{selected_ref,focus_visual_id,collapsed_groups,filters,camera,request_id,base_generation_id,base_digest}`, resets on explicit base change, preserves state for selection refresh, rejects stale request/generation/digest replies. Read-only node inspection never refits/reflows map.

Focus: wait for matching layout then center exact visual node, animate only when reduced-motion allows; first fit once at readable zoom, user camera survives inspection. Filter/collapse hides or uses bounded callback-derived scene; no fabricated edges/ownership. Expand derives matching LayoutSpec via server within budget, reports omissions, aborts stale request; keyboard Tab/Enter/Space/Escape and non-color status/ARIA applied. Structured alternative full-width/paginated, map inspector absent; text clip/wrap safely, full identifiers expandable. Optional low-risk manual positioning is transient only and cannot change accepted spec/layout/export/report; omit if risks core stability.

M6 consumer contract: props use exact C2 DTOs; adapters have no route assumption; callback service later calls **Express only**, preserving exact version/generation/cursors/errors and JSON/SVG media/hash/abort. M4 provides fixtures/OpenAPI and prepared export affordances, not live page, delivery polling/retry shell or download relay. No saved preference/workspace storage or write authorization enters M4.

JS digest validation uses built-in Web Crypto at async ingress once per spec/layout/descriptor change (never every render), deep-freezes validated nested records, and guards completion by request/base identity; pure toReactFlow only maps validated objects synchronously. Python parsers and helper raw JSON ingress reject duplicate wire keys; props are already parsed objects, so JS does not falsely claim it can recover duplicate keys lost by a host parser. No extra npm hashing dependency.

### 7B.14 Final export, font asset and M5 prepared-vector contract

**Selected formats/member set:** mandatory immutable JSON semantic/layout/membership + **stored SVG paths**, exact four members and manifest in §7B.7. User JSON export is {spec,layout,descriptor}; SVG is exact sealed scene.svg bytes, no request-time re-render. PNG, standalone diagram PDF, browser screenshot/rasterizer and SVG-parser dependencies excluded. M5 preparation obtains all-four exact BarrierReceipt ArtifactReferences, calls DiagramReadPort.read_exact for each and validates same scope/digest/schema. All four refs/profiles/coverage retained in report provenance; overview always embedded, other diagrams when useful. M5 receives PreparedDiagram vector boxes/routes/glyph commands, **does not need SVG parsing/font lookup/canonical queries in renderer**. Renderer-specific PDF layout/pagination/ReportSpec remains M5 responsibility, not an unresolved M4 contract.

**Exact assets/toolchain selected for approval:** unmodified **DejaVu Sans 2.37**, regular DejaVuSans.ttf and bold DejaVuSans-Bold.ttf from upstream dejavu-fonts-ttf-2.37 distribution; official license includes Bitstream Vera and Arev notices plus public-domain DejaVu changes. Preserve complete upstream license/attribution, do not rename modified font to protected names or sell font alone. [Upstream distribution/license](https://dejavu-fonts.github.io/License.html) is evidence of conditions, not proof assets installed. Proposed exact files: `backend/platform/diagrams/assets/fonts/DejaVuSans.ttf`, `DejaVuSans-Bold.ttf`, `LICENSE.dejavu.txt`, `font-manifest.json`. Last two are proposed notice-copy/manifest names, **not claimed upstream/existing filenames**. Manifest fields {profile:"dejavu_paths_2_37_v1",family:"DejaVu Sans",release:"2.37",files:[{name,sha256,size_bytes}],source_url,license_sha256,tool:"fonttools",tool_version:"4.68.0"}; actual hashes derive from verified upstream bytes in B1. Copy full legal text, include release/license/code notices in deployment/export metadata.

Add **fonttools==4.68.0** (MIT), pure-Python trusted bundled-TTF hmtx/cmap/glyf outline access only, no user font/upload/XML/URL; no Pillow/FreeType/HarfBuzz/native dependency. Python 3.14 compatibility must pass B1; official [release history](https://github.com/fonttools/fonttools/blob/main/NEWS.rst) identifies release, not executed compatibility proof. Use stdlib Decimal/int math and fontTools glyph outlines converted to closed M/L/Q/C/Z path commands in LayoutSpec; no opaque user SVG path strings. Node receives already measured label dimensions, never loads fonts. LayoutSpec font_notice contains full redistributable font notices; same notice appears SVG metadata and prepared JSON, M5 preserves notices in final PDF metadata/credits.

**Deterministic text profile:** name/body regular14px; kind/header bold12px; line height18px, node label max2 lines and 96 **display** code points, deterministic greedy wrap at spaces else character, U+2026 ellipsis measured from approved glyph or escaped ASCII fallback. Original canonical label remains untouched in spec/LabelGeometry.original_text/accessible inspector/SVG title; clipping records flag + label_limitation, never silent content loss. Supported simple left-to-right scalar ranges U+0020–007E, U+00A0–024F and U+0370–052F **only where cmap has a glyph**; kerning/ligatures/shaping disabled, sum font-unit advance widths at exact rational scale, rounded once to milli-px. ASCII controls/backslash, bidi controls, combining marks, RTL/CJK/emoji/out-of-profile/missing glyphs display explicit ASCII `[U+XXXX]` (six digits above FFFF), with escaped=true and visible legend reason `unsupported_text_escaped`. Full original Unicode retained and disclosed; no host fallback/network/download or falsely shaped script. This deliberate v1 accessibility limitation is part of owner approval, not implementation-agent choice. Escape marker ASCII must be in font; otherwise hard capability failure. Invalid UTF-8/surrogate fails contract.

Glyph ID hashes font asset hash/style + glyph index + fixed font size; integer paths in shared glyph table deduplicate. Place each glyph at prepared integer origin; SVG uses generated safe numeric path primitives and title/ARIA text, not external fonts or executable metadata. React canvas can draw same paths; M5 maps y-down milli-px to PDF coordinates with deterministic transform, Q→C path conversion is pure prepared geometry. Original label text available for report accessibility, not substituted as an unsupported rendered glyph.

**B1 pass/fail and contingency:** verify exact release/license/bytes/cmap/advances/outlines and asset-manifest hash; repeated Latin/Greek/Cyrillic/combining/BOM/CRLF/bidi/CJK/emoji/oversized fixtures, same metrics/path/digest, safe escaping/clipping, no missing characters silently. XML parser confirms no script/event/foreignObject/DTD/external-resource constructs; export/member limits. If a glyph is unsupported, already-specified visible escape path is the accepted contingency, not a new font. If fonts/toolchain cannot run on Python3.14 or license/source bytes fail verification, disable layout/export/READY and raise genuine dependency change control; no substitution of host font or unreviewed package. B1 records real asset SRI/license evidence; no additional design approval is needed for the exact above inventory after owner accepts this plan.

### 7B.15 Hard ceilings, configurable policy, measurement and fail-closed behavior

**Values below are fully selected design proposals for owner approval**, not measured capacity or existing production settings. Plan approval accepts these initial safety ceilings/defaults and the two deterministic selection profiles; it does not assert benchmark success, enable dispatch or change any frozen limit. Implement defaults exactly after approval; lower operational settings are allowed within [1,ceiling] (zero allowed only for optional feature disable), no auto-tuning/secret fallback. Raising a hard ceiling/schema/profile or runtime privilege requires change control. All timeouts take min(configured remaining time, actual C0 deadline), apply across source/mapping/child/export/seal/readback/final pointer validation, and actually cancel DB/child/stream, not only stop waiting.

| Dimension / enforcement owner | Default operating setting | Hard ceiling / failure |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Canonical per-version inventory | Frozen P3 counts: entities25,000; relationships50,000; evidence75,000; unresolved10,000; conflicts5,000 | Same counts; any foreign larger/schema input fails diagram_limit_exceeded, never raises P3 settings. |
| Total source rows / calls / page size / cumulative serialized input | 350,000 / 2,500 / 200 /128 MiB | 500,000 /5,000 /500 /256 MiB. Counts every page/pass incl evidence; at most2 passes/collection. Required minimum not proven→fail, no claimed absence. |
| Compact retained source/refs / canonical serialized retained bytes | 100,000 records /64 MiB | 200,000 /128 MiB; reject before new retention. These are object/serialized-data bounds, **not an OS cap on whole existing Celery process RSS**. |
| Visible canonical nodes / semantic edge objects / groups / depth | standard500:500 /2,000 /128 /4 | 1,000 /4,000 /256 /8; bounded deterministic selection with omissions; mandatory root cannot fit→fail. ~500 is UX target, not full repository maximum. |
| Bundled real relationships / group memberships / edge fanout | 25,000 /50,000 /100 per visual endpoint | 50,000 /100,000 /250; paginate sealed memberships, bundle equal-kind/status/direction only, retain true members; if catalog/ref integrity cannot fit, fail. |
| Per logical ID / canonical name / namespace component / evidence source key | 8,192 /4,096 /1,024 /8,192 UTF-8 bytes | Same hard values; no silent identity/name truncation. Display clipping separately disclosed in LayoutSpec. Namespace ≤64 components, annotation/legend≤10,000/64. |
| Helper input / stdout / stderr / scratch | 8 /16 /0.0625 /32 MiB | 16 /32 /0.25 /64 MiB; framed bounded readers and cgroup/tmpfs, flood→terminate capacity, no raw stderr output. |
| Helper memory / swap / processes / open FDs / CPU | 768 MiB /0 /64 /64 /20s | 1 GiB /0 /128 /128 /60s; actual cgroup memory.max+swap0+pids and RLIMITs, OOM/limit fails, never increase parser512MiB limit or kill entire M1 worker by assumption. CPU quota one core. |
| Helper wall (incl startup/fallback) / total build / view / exact read / individual DB statement / lock | 30 /180 /30 /15 /5 /1 seconds | 90 /300 /90 /60 /15 /3 seconds. One fallback shares original wall budget. Watchdog checkpoints≤100ms while child live; kill/reap≤2s additional cleanup bound. |
| Geometry / route sections / glyphs / glyph placements / path commands | abs(coordinate)≤10^9 milli-px /20,000 /512 /100,000 /300,000 | Same coordinate bound /50,000 /1,024 /200,000 /600,000; validate before materialization/export. One object box bounds finite, no arbitrarily huge canvas. |
| spec.json / layout.json / members.json / scene.svg / manifest / total member bytes | 8 /16 /32 /32 /0.125 /96 MiB | 16 /32 /48 /48 /0.25 /128 MiB. Per-member and aggregate both enforced; no partial seal/READY. |
| Focus roots / selected IDs / expanded groups / cumulative depth / API body | 1 /100 /32 /4 /64 KiB | 32 /200 /64 /8 /128 KiB; closed filters no generic query DSL. |
| Page size / cursor bytes / cursor absolute TTL | 50 /4,096 /600s | 200 /4,096 /600s; signed request/scope/actor/generation-bound keyset, every continuation auth. No indefinite renewal/session. |
| Read stream / durable pin lifetime / pins per generation / concurrent local reads | 60s /120s /32 /8 | 120s /300s /64 /16; max lifetime + handle termination proof precede expired-pin cleanup. Streaming budget distinct from preverified read; no early corrupt headers. |
| Concurrent local builds / per-project builds / new scratch free space | 1 /1 /2 GiB minimum | 2 /1 /512 MiB absolute minimum; if below max(configured free minimum, twice admitted candidate total budget), reject admission. Use OS slot locks, not new scheduler or queue. Existing M1 fleet/admission policies still govern distributed work. |
| Total staging+unaccepted sealed bytes / orphan grace / GC batch | 2 GiB /24h /20 | 8 GiB /minimum1h /100; missing/failed quota inspection disables admission/GC. Grace is necessary not sufficient; retirement, no refs/pins and writer termination mandatory. Accepted history indefinite, **no accepted-delete retention**. |

Configurations are strict closed `DiagramRuntimeConfig`: `enabled:bool=false,selection_profile:standard500|compact150,runtime/layout/font/export/schema versions` fixed above, `operational_limits` with **exact table dimension keys**, and `cursor_key_id/cursor_secret` (secret injected, never fingerprint/log). No arbitrary ELK/runtime/paths/options. Deployment roots fixed §7B.10; settings/env additions: `DIAGRAMS_ENABLED=false`, `DIAGRAMS_PROFILE_JSON` (the exact validated defaults/overrides), `DIAGRAMS_CURSOR_SECRET` (empty disables protected cursor APIs, never hard-coded); runtime/deployment lock records actual binary/package/font hashes. Missing unsupported/downgraded profile or out-of-ceiling config fails capability closed, no partially registered four-template production placeholder.

**Profile binding:** C0 ProducerProfile `producer_id="stacksense.diagrams"; producer_version="1.0.0"; generator_version="1.0.0"; output_schema_version="1.0.0"; configuration_fingerprint=SHA256(J(complete nonsecret config + actual runtime/asset hashes))`. HandlerProfile slot uses each actual diagram template. SemanticProfile fingerprint includes semantic version/template/default view + selection/retention/count policy only; runtime/font/cleanup/time settings affect C0 binding but not semantic identity unless semantic selection changes. Compact150 differs only visible nodes150/edges600/groups64/depth3, all other safety/security/schema same. No mutation of existing M1 bindings/work policy; profiles chosen **before production registration/discovery**; already pinned work never silently switched.

**B1/B3 measurement protocol:** named actual OS/FS/hardware/process versions; 10 cold+10 warm repeats for 20/40,150/300,500-variable-fanout fixtures and 25k/50k canonical input with bounded scene. Record raw per-phase time, counts/bytes/DB calls, current RSS before/after/gc vs lifetime peak (parent & helper separate), CPU/cgroup memory/exit and geometry/digests, 100 writer/security races. Standard500 passes if all mandatory source/default scene cases finish within selected budgets/caps, deterministic repeats and no network/write/FD leak, labels/routes valid, browser500 fixture responds to selection/pan within250ms p95 on named test machine and no position/refit loop. These are fixture acceptance gates, **not universal latency SLA**.

**Predeclared contingency submitted for owner approval:** on ordinary performance/geometry pressure, try already-defined simple ELK fallback within original budget; never truncate/raise limits. If standard500 cannot satisfy resource/UX gate after in-scope fixes, compact150 may be selected before any new registration only if it passes all correctness/security/default-budget tests and reports explicit bounded omissions; record measured loss and selected fingerprint. 500-node stress still measured/reported, not falsely passed. If neither profile can support mandatory four diagrams or security/contract guarantee fails, stop affected production path and request change control with measurements. No extra routine policy/design phase is required for these selected options; owner may reject/adjust values during **this** review. Existing global C0 production activation approval remains a deployment authorization, not silently overridden by M4 plan approval.

Exact operational-limit keys (N, units as table; no aliases/unknown keys): max_source_rows,max_source_calls,source_page_size,max_input_bytes,max_retained_records,max_retained_bytes,max_nodes,max_edges,max_groups,max_depth,max_relationship_members,max_group_members,max_fanout,max_logical_id_bytes,max_name_bytes,max_namespace_component_bytes,max_source_fact_key_bytes,max_helper_input_bytes,max_helper_output_bytes,max_helper_stderr_bytes,max_scratch_bytes,max_helper_memory_bytes,max_helper_processes,max_helper_fds,max_helper_cpu_seconds,helper_wall_seconds,build_seconds,view_seconds,read_seconds,statement_seconds,lock_seconds,max_route_sections,max_glyphs,max_glyph_placements,max_path_commands,max_spec_bytes,max_layout_bytes,max_members_bytes,max_svg_bytes,max_manifest_bytes,max_total_member_bytes,max_focus_roots,max_selected_ids,max_expanded_groups,max_api_body_bytes,page_size,max_cursor_bytes,cursor_ttl_seconds,stream_seconds,pin_seconds,max_pins_per_generation,max_local_reads,max_local_builds,max_project_builds,min_free_bytes,max_orphan_bytes,orphan_grace_seconds,gc_batch_size. Fixed swap=0, CPU quota=one core, geometry bound and schema/text caps cannot be relaxed. Per-project admission uses an M4-owned PostgreSQL advisory lock; local CPU slots use OS locks, with bounded wait≤lock budget then existing transient C0 failure classification—no new durable waiting state/scheduler. Hard limits reject config outside range; reducing a limit affects only new pinned bindings, never already scheduled work silently.
