### 7B.19 Security, tests, risks and measurable Definition of Done

Threat model: untrusted canonical labels/metadata, forged project/version/generation/descriptor/cursor, malformed graph/ELK input, runaway child, stale/malfunctioning ordinary artifact worker and retained descriptors, concurrent GC/pin/publication, crash and storage corruption. Trust root/admin/installed sealer/kernel and canonical/M1 database authority; do not promise integrity against those authorities being compromised. Ordinary worker cannot invoke general privileged command/GC or mutate accepted filesystem bytes; sealer/GC strict entry/role/lock/tombstone/security proof mandatory. Layout sees no credentials/network/source/store. No unsafe raw metadata/source excerpts/SQL/errors or font URLs. SVG uses trusted numeric glyph paths/escaped title/metadata only, no script/entity/foreignObject. Exact accepted/read pin/descriptor validation precedes any output; all caps/timeouts checked through final mapping/pointer verification.

Observability: observed template/project/version/generation/work/attempt/profile IDs, safe phase/error code/correlation, source row/page/call and selected/omitted/group/evidence counts, spec/layout/catalog/SVG/manifest sizes, per-phase timings, helper cgroup CPU/current & peak memory/termination, pins/cleanup/capability/fallback outcomes. Never raw secret/path/stderr/user source, fake completeness or estimated success. Logs do not decide work lifecycle; no new metrics SaaS/dependency.

**Module-wide tests and evaluation targets (executed B1 subset and actual results are recorded in §7B.20; later-batch targets are not claimed executed):**

**Unit/contract:** four complete golden DiagramSpecs from identical canonical facts with template-specific expected nodes/edges/groups/coverage; permutations and duplicate/ambiguous identity; exact-direction/message-consumption/persistence tests; isolated overview and hierarchical component rules; SCC scope/cycle labeling; honest empty dataflow; inferred/supported/conflicting/unknown handling; missing producer/fact key/artifact ID/ranges; JSON Unicode/CRLF/BOM metadata preservation without source access; deep nested-copy mutation/digest validation; canonical bytes/hash domains; invalid schema/profile/fence/ref/extra fields; no cross-generation or fake canonical group IDs; coverage arithmetic/required overflow; XML injection/oversized labels/symlinks/NaN; route/group geometry validators.

**Real integration:** public canonical paging over published PostgreSQL fixtures, separate staging and M1 publication connections, guarded enlistment/poison even when lifecycle violations are caught, actual filesystem readback/seal including open writer FD/stale worker/reopen/write/unlink attempts, accepted-reference-only visibility, committed historical reads, corruption despite unchanged metadata hash labels, foreign project/member/receipt, concurrent publication vs cleanup/pin race, uncommitted acceptance inaccessible, crash after seal/staging/M1 commit, retry/cancel/lease retirement, all-four barrier zero/one report scheduling with real M1. PostgreSQL advisory/row locks and independent sessions are required, not SQLite concurrency proof. Real RabbitMQ/Celery uses existing queue and registry/deadline/heartbeat/recovery/restart; worker capability missing runtime must not consume work/executions. Test overview transient hold/final failure sibling release and missing/corrupt ready reference prevents report dispatch; canonical model/search/good siblings survive failures. No report implementation needed: stub registered report consumer observes exact M1 barrier only, clearly labeled contract fixture.

Real ELK child must exercise compound/cross-group routes, fallback, timeout/CPU/address-space/input/output bounds, FD/env/network/repository denial, credential absence, cancellation/killed tree, restart, exact version/profile. Future P4/C3 adapter tests, only if separately authorized later, use real verified Neo4j/ES pinned generations and changed-pointer rejection; absence of those integrations in M4 v1 is proved by zero optional resource initialization for default builders. Do not make ES/Neo4j required just to inflate integration coverage. Each real-service test has explicit isolated setup/env gates and records skips as unavailable evidence, not pass. No arbitrary test-container names/restarts; fixtures validate ownership labels and target only task-created resources.

**Frontend/browser:** JS consumer guards over actual C2 JSON, validated stored layout fixtures and real server ELK; no browser layout worker in v1; stable IDs and group expansion; no geometry overlap/route dislocation; selection does not re-layout/refit/scroll; View on map exact-node focus; stale generation/revision/request replies ignored; template switch/cancel; evidence pagination/actions-before-long-list; keyboard/escape/accessibility/responsive text clipping; transient presentation reset; empty/partial/failed/forbidden/unavailable states; JSON/SVG downloads' enabled-state/metadata. Real browser (available browser tooling or explicitly approved test runner, not an unapproved new dependency) must visually inspect four representative diagrams and 500-visible-node scene, including very wide stars/long labels, compound routes and pan/zoom/collapse. jsdom does not prove actual layout/render memory or usability.



| Risk | Likelihood / impact | Mitigation and verification |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Reusing P4 as the diagram engine or editing frozen internals | High / high | Own C2/builders/renderer; public source/query ports only, diff/import audit, unchanged P4 regression. No enlarged P4 caps. |
| Neo4j/search becomes authority or mandatory | Medium / critical | Canonical-only four defaults; projection used only for explicit optional action with rehydration and final binding; outage/zero-init tests. |
| Version/digest/profile/generation drift | Medium / critical | Exact C0/reference, canonical header before/after, signed scope/generation cursors, no latest; foreign/multiple-version/pointer-switch tests. |
| Nondeterministic selection/geometry/digest | High / high | Sorted inputs/explicit grouping/hash domains/versions/pinned runtime/font, semantics separate layout; permutation/golden/runtime-repeat tests. No unproven cross-version pixel claim. |
| Invented dataflow/ownership/canonical abstraction | High / critical | Four rule sets, canonical direct refs and static labels, no response/reverse-consumes inference/transitive synthetic edges; sparse/conflict/asymmetric fixtures. |
| Evidence/conflicts disappear behind grouping | Medium / high | Every semantic/bundle member retains actual references/uncertainty and explicit omission counts; M3 detail optional public port, adversarial proof. |
| Large scan/retained membership/fanout exhausts resources | High / high | Separate source/retained/scene/route/byte caps, paged scans, collapsible/focused views, measured memory/current+peak and overflow tests; no silent first-N complete diagram. |
| ELK helper access/CPU/memory/output leak | Medium / critical | OS sandbox, no credentials/network/repository, bounded parent pipes/checkpoints/group kill; real denial/kill/restart tests and approved runtime gate. |
| Layout all-horizontal or routes detached after compaction | High / high UX | Compound/connected-component packing translates all geometry, action-driven relayout, view-specific profiles and visual 500-node/hub/long-label acceptance. |
| False seal, stale FD writer, cleanup/publication race | High / critical | Physically excluded sealed copy/new inodes, independent readback, committed staging, producer locks/deletion state+public retirement, retained accepted generations; actual multiprocess PG/FS tests. |
| M1 UoW misuse/report barrier duplication | Medium / critical | Borrowed guarded Session, no I/O/commit in publication, M1-owned barrier/retry only; guard-poison and four-template actual pipeline tests. |
| React selection/camera regressions | High / high | Separate M4 worker/reducer contracts, fit-once/explicit focus/stale guards, callbacks instead of network shell; frozen UI suite + real browser. |
| SVG/font/export or download disclosure | Medium / critical | Prepared escaped XML/no external resources, allowlisted members/scope, bounded preverified download/pins/private headers, licensed assets; injection/corruption/abort/project tests. |
| M4/M5/M6 contract mismatch or only mock integration | High / high | Freeze C2 fixtures/exact read and media contract before consumers, actual C0/store/ELK proof, no live gateway claim; M6 delivery integration separate. |
| Privilege-separated local store not deployable | Medium / high | Selected B1 host/FS/uid proof + specified exclusive-mkdir contingency; otherwise stop/change control, no insecure fallback or false READY. |

- **Corrections implemented:** `layout_runtime/elk_layout.mjs` now applies the declared group padding/options to nested containers and requests root-relative edge coordinates before disconnected-component packing. The previous default container-relative routes and default child padding failed actual compound validation; corrected nested/cross-group routes pass. This follows ELK's documented [coordinate-system semantics](https://eclipse.dev/elk/documentation/tooldevelopers/graphdatastructure/coordinatesystem.html). `layout.py` rejects success-shaped output from a failed child, preserves only closed typed error frames, checks cancellation/deadlines after process completion and geometry validation, classifies process-wait expiry and always runs pipe/cgroup finalizers. The acquisition-candidate helper hash is updated to actual bytes: `5cc60b7255df91dc2323401a2435eb34df9b25964e65469f129bd60a520a63e1`; it remains **not** a complete installed runtime lock.

The data-allocation bound is **not** a certified cgroup RSS bound or full sandbox certification. No production fallback/registry entry is added. Derived output is not accepted, stored, report-ready or a lifecycle write. `artifacts.py` classifies physically missing accepted generation/member bytes distinctly, preserving direct503 versus continuation410 behavior; no history deletion is introduced.
- Requests reject duplicate/unknown query keys, duplicate JSON keys, noninteger JSON, unknown fields, unsupported media/formats and bounded body overflow. Errors use only the closed sanitized wrapper/status set. Exports preverify and close their bounded handles/pins before headers, include actual Content-Length/SHA-256 Content-Digest/quoted ETag, attachment name/nosniff/private-no-store; Range is rejected416 and no conditional304 bypass is added. Existing frozen handlers/error middleware are unchanged.

## 7C. P5 M5 — Deterministic Architecture Report: Detailed Planning Record

### 7C.1 Status, authority and inspected dependencies

**2026-11-20 — M5 PLAN: FROZEN. OWNER DECISIONS: RESOLVED (D-M5-01–05). IMPLEMENTATION: AUTHORIZED TO EXECUTE B1 → B2 → B3 SEQUENTIALLY, SUBJECT TO ENGINEERING ACCEPTANCE GATES. IMPLEMENTATION NOT STARTED.** This owner instruction approves the entire detailed plan, C4 v1.0.0 design, narrow shared-port scope, content, dependency candidates, initial engineering caps and functional/deployment separation upfront. No separate approval between batches. Full execution plan: **§9, internal §§15.5.1–11**; this is the status handoff, not another specification. Actual implementation/conformance/measurements remain unverified. M1–M4/C0/C1/C2/C3 stay frozen except the precisely approved read-Protocol-only amendment; no lifecycle or M4 concrete-storage changes.

Inspected actual C0 contracts, `DeliveryCoordinator`, guarded `PublicationUnitOfWork`, registry/composition, retry/barrier/retirement ports; actual C2 `DiagramReadPort`, `PreparedDiagram`, `get_diagram_reader`, font assets and consumer/integration tests; public `ArchitectureQueryService` and `AnalysisLifecycleService` DTOs/paging/factories; ADR-0001, migrations/requirements/DI/API conventions, branch/worktree and recent Git history. Master audit targeted report §§17.1–7,19–39,43,46–56,61–71,76–86,89–90 and diagram/report handoff requirements; no claim of a fresh exhaustive master reread. No relevant contradiction was established, apart from the actual missing generic PDF-capable store interface below. This planning task did not execute renderers, install dependencies, benchmark, migrate, provision or rerun application tests.

### 7C.2 Proposed architecture and C4 invariants

`M1 report context + exact four-diagram barrier → public canonical/diagnostic capture and exact C2 reads → insert-once prepared snapshot → C4 validation → isolated ReportLab PDF → verified/sealed candidate and separately committed staging → M1-only fenced publication → authorized exact-generation report reads/history`.

- M1 alone owns eligibility, scheduling, work/attempt/fencing, retries and the outer publication transaction. M5 never polls prerequisites, creates jobs or regenerates M4 outputs. Existing retry resource_id is **M1 work_id**, as verified in `DeliveryRepository.retry_failed`, not report_id. Its completed-successor extension is search-only; no completed-report rebuild is proposed.
- C4 v1.0.0 approved design defines closed immutable `ReportSpec`, typed six-section content/provenance/coverage, prepared/rendered records, report manifest/descriptor, exact read port and M6 DTO/consumer fixtures. Reuse C0 scope/profile/reference/receipt and actual C2 records without copying their contracts.
- Six ordered sections: metadata; established inventory/boundaries; architecture diagrams (all four); all original diagnostics; coverage/limitations; provenance/notices. **Option 3 embedding is owner-approved:** all four exact-version READY diagrams (system_overview, component_architecture, dependency_map, data_flow) are embedded, not merely referenced, in that fixed order. Compact multi-diagram pages are preferred only when readable; use a full page or tiled continuation when required. Preserve exact identity/version/provenance/content and M4's prepared geometry; no new diagram generation/layout/semantic transformation. No AI/executive summary, new findings/severity, parsing, source excerpts, alternate formats or new framework. Actual severity (including critical), uncertainty and provenance are preserved; unsupported/unavailable metadata is explicit. Names/Git SHA are unavailable initially rather than fetched from MongoDB or invented from snapshot UUIDs.
- Read-only bounded REPEATABLE READ capture composes public version/canonical/diagnostic services; immutable captured inputs commit once per report resource, with concurrent insert/CAS protection. Failed attempts reuse that snapshot, including all four complete prepared diagram/glyph inputs, not newer diagnostics or diagrams. C0's barrier input fingerprint is unchanged; source/spec/render digests and stable resource UUID are separately specified in §15.5.3. Candidate IDs never enter PDF semantic bytes. Exact historic scope remains valid even if the analysis now exposes another published version.
- Renderer consumes prepared text/vector geometry and verified asset bytes only: no DB/API/network, SVG parser, ELK or implicit font lookup. Reuse licensed DejaVu2.37 assets; owner-approved ReportLab5.1.0/pypdf7.1.1 are initial pinned compatibility candidates, not installed/proven here. Fixed profile/fonts/dates/order, three cold byte-identical renders, full parse/security checks, all-page raster/visual review of every label/route/legend/caption (not PDF text extraction alone), explicit unsupported-script escapes and deterministic readable all-four compact packing/full-page fallback/tiling are B1/B2 gates.
- PostgreSQL owns report resource, immutable input snapshot containing all four prepared views and their receipts, generation history, accepted-reference metadata and bounded read pins. M1's guarded borrowed Session permits metadata-only acceptance/readiness; separate staging commit precedes publication. Guard violations poison acceptance even if caught. No unaccepted artifact is externally readable. Exact downloads verify full descriptors/digests and hold cleanup exclusion; accepted history is never orphan-GC eligible. Crash recovery, corruption, stale workers and cleanup use public C0 retirement, not inferred lease expiry.
- M5 owns internal FastAPI exact-version reports/list/resource/generation/download routes and safe errors. M6 owns Express transport, binary relay and UI. No Express/frontend change is in M5. Report failure never invalidates successful upstream outputs. No mutable latest/download alias, changed-generation fallback or report-specific retry endpoint.

The live graph pipeline initially exceeded its unchanged path deadline; isolated reruns passed that query. Repeated pipeline runs additionally reproduced a concurrent FastAPI `UnsupportedFieldAttributeWarning` failure: Python3.14's process-global warning filters race when FastAPI builds dependency schemas in simultaneous test threads. CI now enables `PYTHON_CONTEXT_AWARE_WARNINGS=1` and `PYTHON_THREAD_INHERIT_CONTEXT=1`, retaining warnings-as-errors inside worker threads while isolating filters per context. A direct thread-policy experiment confirms warning errors propagate; ten repeated runs of both graph API integration tests pass with both settings (20 tests, zero skips/failures). CI YAML parses successfully with the unchanged `pytest` command. An intermediate full run was deliberately interrupted (594 passed, three skipped) to restart with the final CI environment; it is not completion evidence.

### 7D.1 Status, inspection and authority

**2026-11-25 — COMPLETE PLAN / C5 v1.0.0: OWNER APPROVED / FROZEN. M6 IMPLEMENTATION: NOT STARTED; AWAITING EXPLICIT START INSTRUCTION.** The owner approved the complete three-batch plan and directed a status-only update with no implementation. Once explicitly started, execute B1 → B2 → B3 automatically after engineering gates; no discretionary owner reauthorization between batches. Failed tests require repair and rerun. Only a frozen-contract conflict, material security issue or demonstrated infeasibility triggers scoped change control. Planning-time proposal/approval-request wording below is historical design wording superseded by this owner decision; technical requirements and gates remain unchanged.

Authority: `master.txt` §§0.10–14, 9 (lifecycle independence), 14 (retrieval/provenance), 16 (diagrams), 17 (reports), 18.86/98/103–104 (public contracts/auth/version/DTO boundaries), and complete §19 (frontend/UX); ADR-0001; current frozen C0–C4 and actual route implementations. This is a targeted normative audit, not a claim of rereading every master section. Master §0 overrides historical organization examples. Existing accepted React/Vite/hash routing is retained under the established React implementation; the master’s conceptual Next.js direction does not authorize a framework migration. No newly introduced contract contradiction was established.

Inspected branch `feature_mannan`, history through merge `3d6de12`/M5 `a5dc411`, dirty files, Express `src/{routes,controllers,middleware,services,models,config}`, frontend `src/{pages,components,contracts,hooks,services,styles,workers}`, package scripts/Vite/Vitest, Python `api/v1/{delivery,knowledge,retrieval,diagrams,reports,architecture}.py`, producer contracts/configuration and consumer/real-boundary tests. Relevant findings:

- **Existing/accepted:** Express JWT middleware, project RBAC and delegated Python gateway; analysis/architecture routes, graph proxy, established dark design tokens and DashboardHeader; analysis SSE; canonical model and exact P4 Explorer pages; M4 DiagramView/DiagramCanvas/DiagramInspector, contract/digest validation, `useDiagramView`, pure `diagramAdapter`; M1–M5 Python APIs. M5 acceptance is reconciled in §7C.13, with real proof limitations preserved.

There is no existing frozen C5 artifact: baseline internal §17 is conceptual, not a runnable API specification. This §7D is the proposed C5 transport contract; actual producer routes below supersede outdated baseline examples only for M6 planning.
- **Reuse constraints:** `AnalysisGatewayService.request` already serializes arrays as repeated parameters and graph mode preserves HTTP status, but currently reads JSON without a byte cap and follows redirects in default mode. Its request `close` listener is unsuitable as a universal disconnect signal (a POST request can finish before its response). Extend it with an opt-in P5 mode; regression-lock default/graph/SSE behavior. `apiClient.apiRequest` parses non-JSON as text and turns fetch aborts into network errors; add binary support and preserve abort identity inside this client. Its 401 refresh is single-flight and 403 already preserves session.
- **M5 handoff:** C4 `ReportCollection`, `ReportSummary`, `ReportSlotStatus`, `ReportGenerationPage`, `ReportGenerationSummary`, exact `ArtifactReference`, `ReportCoverage`; report availability/readability are distinct. READY metadata is not physical download verification (`metadata_only` remains meaningful). Exact PDF headers include SHA-256 Content-Digest/ETag, byte length, safe attachment filename and no-store. No mutable-download alias or report-specific retry route exists after remediation. M1 retry consumes failed **work ID**, not report-resource UUID (§10.2 delivery contract).
- **No assumed version catalog:** existing canonical APIs expose exact version and analysis→current published version; no paged repository-wide Architecture Version catalog exists. M6 must not imply that listing analyses enumerates every historical version of each analysis.

### 7D.2 Product scope and ownership

One new repository-centered page presents Diagrams (default), Search, Knowledge and Reports, with a shared bounded evidence drawer and compact delivery strip. Existing repository, analysis, model and Explorer pages remain available through precise cross-links. React → existing Express API → FastAPI → owner stores; no browser Python/Neo4j/ES connection. Express owns authentication/delegation/transport only; M1 scheduling/retry/fencing; M2 lexical search; M3 controlled retrieval/context; M4 prepared exact-generation geometry/artifacts; M5 PDF/history. No new canonical facts, client-side confidence/severity calculation, raw source viewer, AI/chatbot, persistent workspace, queue, public lifecycle or storage authority.

**Scoped Express proposal:** add only C5 routes, validation/controller integration, gateway byte/error/binary behavior, configuration and tests below. Preserve JWT/project RBAC/Mongo ownership/ADR-0001. No producer source, frozen route, contract or database migration is planned. Deployment settings remain explicit; M6 does not enable disabled producers or approve production operating policies.

**Optional preparation bridge recommendation: DEFER.** Current ingestion has durable platform status/revision metadata, and analysis creation is a separate authorized/idempotent Python operation; there is no approved durable continuation contract. Automatic chaining needs a Mongo-owned continuation record, actor revalidation, a stable request key/revision binding and restart reconciliation. It is useful UX but not a prerequisite for reading P5 outputs. No bridge model/route/service/migration/test belongs to these three batches. Existing ingestion/start-analysis controls and exact analysis links remain. A future scoped plan can add the bridge; no browser effect/on-completion callback may masquerade as durable scheduling.

**Internal metadata recommendation: OMIT.** Use existing authorized project/repository/revision endpoints for live breadcrumb names; label M5 unavailable captured display names honestly. No Python→Express metadata API is required to display existing immutable reports. Do not retrofit names into C4 or call platform databases from Python.

### 7D.3 C5 route inventory and producer DTO authority

Browser prefix `/api/v1`; `V=/architecture-versions/:versionId`; every row requires one scalar `project_id` UUID query. Express forwards the same suffix/HTTP method to Python's existing `/api/v1` gateway base; no aggregate `/intelligence` backend resource. All reads use `authenticate` + fresh `projectAuthorization.requireAccess`; retry/rebuild use `requireManageAccess` (OWNER/ADMIN), and Python independently authorizes. URL UUID validation accepts all canonical UUID versions (architecture IDs include deterministic UUIDv5). IDs/cursors are opaque; encode path components exactly once.

| Method/path (C5 proposed Express surface) | Exact input besides project_id | Existing success DTO / owner |
|---|---|---|
| GET `V/delivery` | none | 200 `DeliverySummary` / M1 |
| POST `V/delivery/retry` | JSON `RetryRequest{resource_type,resource_id,idempotency_key}` | 202 `RetryAccepted` / M1; resource_id is eligible failed work ID |
| GET `V/search/status` | none | 200 `SearchStatus` / M2 |
| GET `V/search` | `q,mode,document_kinds[],entity_kinds[],statuses[],limit,cursor,timeout_seconds` | 200 `SearchPage` / M2; arrays are repeated bare keys |
| POST `V/search/rebuild` | JSON `{idempotency_key}` (`SearchRebuildRequest`) | 202 `SearchReprojectionAccepted` / M2→M1; no M6 successor lifecycle |
| POST `V/knowledge/query` | JSON `KnowledgeQueryRequest`; route-bound project/version may be omitted, conflicts rejected | 200 `KnowledgeQueryResponse` / M3 |
| POST `V/knowledge/context` | JSON `ContextRequest`; same route-scope rule | 200 `ContextBundle` / M3 |
| GET `V/knowledge/evidence` | one selector family `entity_id[]` OR `relationship_id[]` OR `evidence_id[]`, `limit,cursor` | 200 `EvidencePage` / M3; no offset |
| GET `V/diagrams` | none | 200 `DiagramCollection` / M4 (four ordered slots) |
| GET `V/diagrams/:template` | optional `generation_id` | 200 `DiagramSummary` / M4 |
| GET `V/diagrams/:template/generations` | `limit,cursor` | 200 `GenerationPage` / M4 |
| GET `V/diagrams/:template/generations/:generationId/spec` | none | 200 `DiagramSpec` / M4; exact bytes + digest headers |
| GET `V/diagrams/:template/generations/:generationId/layout` | none | 200 `LayoutSpec` / M4; exact bytes + digest headers |
| POST `V/diagrams/:template/generations/:generationId/view` | closed `DiagramViewRequest` JSON | 200 `DiagramViewResponse` / M4; transient view only |
| GET `V/diagrams/:template/generations/:generationId/members` | `group_id` OR `entity_id`, `limit,cursor`, as producer rules | 200 `MemberPage` / M4 |
| GET `V/diagrams/:template/generations/:generationId/export` | `format=json|svg` | 200 `PreparedDiagram` JSON or `image/svg+xml` / M4 |
| GET `V/reports` | `limit,cursor` | 200 `ReportCollection` / M5 |
| GET `V/reports/:reportId` | optional `generation_id` | 200 `ReportSummary` / M5 |
| GET `V/reports/:reportId/generations` | `limit,cursor` | 200 `ReportGenerationPage` / M5 |
| GET `V/reports/:reportId/generations/:generationId` | none | 200 `ReportGenerationSummary` / M5 |
| GET `V/reports/:reportId/generations/:generationId/download` | none; Range requests receive 416 | 200 `application/pdf` / M5, exact authenticated generation |

These 21 operations are the complete added inventory. Reuse existing project/repository/revision, analyses/current-model/exact-model and P4 graph APIs; no new graph routes, report POST generation/retry, diagram completed-rebuild, export spec-rewrite, C1 legacy alias, arbitrary upstream URL or Cypher passthrough. Configure P5 router before broad existing architecture registration and regression-test all old route matching.

**Schema source map and compatibility:** C0 VersionScope/ArtifactReference and M1 `delivery/api_contracts.py`; C1 `knowledge/contracts.py`, `model.py` and `delivery/reprojection.py`; C3 `knowledge/retrieval_contracts.py`; C2 `diagrams/{contracts,api_contracts}.py`; C4 `reports/{contracts,api_contracts}.py`. Existing `frontend/src/contracts/{analysis,architecture,graph,diagram}Contracts.js` remain their owners. C5 reuses complete producer payloads; it does not wrap them in a fabricated common Page or add a `c5_version` field to frozen responses. C5's own version identifies the route/validation/header/error mapping manifest recorded here and checked in M6 fixtures. Export source model schemas to M6 contract fixtures in B1; conformance tests compare runtime fields/defaults/enums/aliases against public producers. New validators are domain-specific consumers, not a new generic schema framework or new DTO authority. No new dependency is required.

Key consumer rules:

- C1 `SearchPage={scope,generation_id,index_uuid,items,total_hits,has_more,next_cursor,capabilities}`; hits retain canonical_reference/document_kind/evidence IDs, finite score and rank_tier. Initial search is lexical-only; semantic/hybrid visibly unavailable (422 capability error), never fallback. C1 q maximum500 and cursor2048; page default25/max100. Preserve exact first generation/index across pages; loss/expiry invalidates continuation, explicit restart only. Scores rank lexical results and do not establish architectural truth.
- C3 supports `lookup,relationships,dependencies,dependents,containment,paths,evidence`; `SubjectSelector` uses `selector_type`, `record_kind`, `record_id|logical_id|name`, optional namespace. Preserve ambiguous/incomplete/not_found resolution and offer explicit candidate selection. Request fields include expected repository/analysis/snapshot on KnowledgeQueryRequest, direction/containment_direction, relationship_kinds/document_kinds, bounded graph_depth≤5, limit≤100/cursor4096. ContextRequest uses subjects/q/operations/required_sources/optional_sources/target and include flags; budget overrides unavailable. No AI question interpretation. C3 result `facts,relationships,evidence,unresolved,conflicts,coverage,source_observations,paths,next_cursor` and ContextBundle rationales/omissions/usage/outcome/semantic_digest remain intact. Context has no paginated session. EvidencePage totals are nullable. Preserve current/stale/unknown, optional-source failures, retrieval success and source availability separately. No final C1 probe or private source call in M6.
- C2 templates are exactly `system_overview,component_architecture,dependency_map,data_flow`. Collection has `canonical_scope`, summaries have `delivery_scope`, availability/readability/work/reference/coverage/counts/capabilities/reason/observed_at. Do not require nonexistent `collection.scope`. Load a READY slot's exact accepted generation using **JSON export → PreparedDiagram**, then reuse `validatePreparedDiagram`/DiagramView. Spec/layout/history/members/view remain individually exposed. No public descriptor endpoint is invented. Export `json` capability is a wire alias (not `json_export`). Validate scope/template/generation plus C2 semantic/layout/manifest binding. Refuse mixed generation responses; retain previously displayed verified generation with a clear update action.
- C4 resources and generations are different: collection includes automatic slot status and resources; summaries bind report_id/profile/input/reference and may have metadata-only readability. History contains `reference,pdf_sha256,pdf_size_bytes,page_count,report_spec_digest,accepted_at`. Exact download uses both report ID and generation ID, compared to selected history metadata. Use existing M1 work retry when allowed; never send report_id as RetryRequest.resource_id. Do not use unused ReportRetryRequest/Response classes as evidence of an endpoint.
- C2 history/member pages default50/max200 and cursor4096; C4 collection/history pages default20/API max100 and cursor4096, subject to M5's configured max_history_page_size. C3 query/evidence default25/max100. Preserve each producer's actual defaults and effective restrictions rather than forcing the conceptual common Page defaults. UI may explicitly choose smaller pages.
- Schema/version mismatch fails visibly before rendering; producer hashes/fingerprints, including C3 semantic_digest, are treated as opaque exact values after shape/schema checks. C3 already validates its semantic digest on the server; M6 does not duplicate Python float/canonicalization/identity logic in JavaScript. Existing public C2 cross-language schema/digest verification remains mandatory through the reused validator. Do not approximate any producer digest with ordinary JSON.stringify. Producer-generated positive/negative fixtures verify these compatibility boundaries.

### 7D.4 Express validation, delegation, errors and bounded transport

`intelligenceRoutes` is a mergeParams router registered at V; explicit leaf paths/methods only. `intelligenceValidate` owns route/query/body allowlists, exact scalar/repeated shape, UUIDs, integer grammar, enum/range/byte caps and route/body scope agreement. No trimming/reordering of q or opaque cursor; no coercing nested objects to strings. Reject duplicate scalar keys; accept repeated bare filter keys only (reject brackets/nested/prototype keys); preserve array values with URLSearchParams.append. Python performs final semantic/profile/identity validation. M3 evidence selector exclusivity and producer request limitations are copied exactly. Use existing canonical enum vocabularies through generated contract fixtures, not M6 entity kinds.

Controller performs current membership/role lookup before forwarding and supplies only trusted actor/role/project plus a server-validated UUID correlation ID (generate when absent/invalid). Discard browser X-Service-Token/X-Actor-*/X-Project-Id and upstream hop-by-hop headers. Python validates project→version→generation/report/template ownership. No repository_id exists on these APIs; do not add a pretend server guard parameter: workspace verifies canonical version's repository/analysis/snapshot before issuing output calls and checks returned scope again. Version from another repository is valid only through its correct repository route; it cannot be merged into the current workspace. Cross-project access remains independently enforced by both services.

Extend the **existing** AnalysisGatewayService with `responseMode:'intelligence'` and a binary/artifact method; no parallel fetch gateway. P5 paths use redirect:error/manual and never follow redirects, even to same host. `AbortController` spans fetch, bounded body iteration and relay; headers-only timeouts are insufficient. Observe req.aborted and res.close when !writableFinished, remove listeners in finally; normal req.close after POST upload must not cancel a healthy response. Cancellation of an HTTP read does not cancel M1 durable work. Bound DNS/connect/fetch/body/spool/relay with one wall budget; cancel/destroy upstream and local streams on timeout/disconnect. Avoid synchronous large parsing on the event loop where measurements exceed limits; reduce panel concurrency before proposing another runtime/worker.

**Proposed engineering transport policy (part of upfront plan approval, not approved production capacity):**

| Dimension | Proposed default / hard ceiling | Enforcement and exceeded behavior |
|---|---|---|
| URL/header request context | query bytes≤32KiB; filter elements≤50/family; scalar cursor caps per producer | validator before fetch; 422; malformed request must not contact upstream |
| JSON request | 128KiB route-wide ceiling, plus exact producer field/body limits (C2 configured default64KiB, hard128KiB) | route validator under existing 1MiB parser; 413 on byte overflow, 422 fields; producer may impose smaller deployment policy |
| Metadata/search/context/report JSON | 4MiB maximum decoded body; C3 additionally obeys its configured producer cap (≤2MiB) | count actual stream bytes before JSON parse; 502 transport-invalid/oversized upstream; never trim records |
| Diagram bytes | spec16MiB/layout32MiB/PreparedDiagram64MiB/SVG48MiB | count actual bytes; digest verify artifact responses before success; JSON export envelope includes descriptor, so not limited to spec alone |
| PDF |64MiB maximum; expected exact selected generation length/digest | bounded disk spool + SHA-256 verification before client success; mismatch/short/extra bytes→502 |
| Upstream metadata/search read |30s total; preserve C1 timeout_seconds≤5 | gateway timer includes body; 504; no automatic retry loop |
| C3 query/context and C2 view |120s total transport ceiling | allows bounded producer deployment settings and recorded 25k canonical scan; does not reinstate a five-second M3 acceptance requirement or alter producer timeouts |
| Artifact/PDF |90s upstream spool,60s client relay; hard combined150s | abort + cleanup; 504 before headers, terminate stream after headers; browser reports interrupted download |
| Admission |2 active artifact relays per Express process;8MiB relay high-water budget plus bounded64MiB spool/request | overflow503 with safe transport_busy; prevents unbounded buffering; deployment concurrency is separately measured |
| UI |search/evidence/history page25/25/20; four diagram status slots; one heavy active artifact load | never fetch all pages; UI may request smaller producer-valid limits; no producer ceilings raised |

Emit new safe transport-only `{error:{code,message,details}}` codes `intelligence_upstream_invalid`(502), `intelligence_transport_limit`(413), `intelligence_transport_busy`(503), `intelligence_upstream_unavailable`(503), `intelligence_upstream_timeout`(504). Details only allow field/dimension/actual/limit/request_id; no upstream URL/path/body/token/stack. Existing authenticated 401/403 errors retain current platform behavior. Upstream **HTTP status is preserved** for recognized producer errors including 202/400/401/403/404/409/410/413/416/422/429/500/503/504. Preserve allowlisted public `error.code`, safe message/details and C3 `error.cause` (SAFE_CAUSES); do not use graph mode which drops cause. Unexpected upstream 4xx/5xx bodies are sanitized while retaining status; malformed successful responses/redirects use transport502. Never relay FastAPI validation `input`/ctx/internal exception content. Preserve producer distinctions: invalid report cursor400 versus search/C3/C2 cursor422; report unsupported contract409 versus C2 422; producer integrity500/503 as implemented. Unknown errors do not become a successful empty result. Local malformed inputs use422 except byte/Range cases above.

**Artifacts/downloads:** use the existing gateway delegated headers. Request identity content-encoding; reject compressed artifact bodies so Content-Length/digest apply to exact bytes. For PDF/SVG/diagram export stream to unique private temporary file, count/hash bytes and compare Content-Digest SHA-256 and safe ETag/length before committing response headers; descriptor/PDF metadata binding is also checked by React. Restrict filename to known attachment pattern or generate from template/version/generation; never use upstream arbitrary paths. Temp file is transient transport buffering, never artifact authority/cache. Use mkdtemp with private permissions, no browser path, bounded admission and stream.pipeline/backpressure. Always remove on success/error/disconnect. Only HTTP200 PDF/application/pdf or SVG/image/svg+xml succeeds; unexpected media/redirect/error document is rejected. JSON bodies/errors bounded separately. Relay only Content-Type, validated Content-Length/Content-Digest/ETag/Content-Disposition, private,no-store, nosniff and safe request ID; no Set-Cookie/Location/service headers. Reject Range locally416. Do not inline SVG HTML; SVG is download-only, C2 map uses validated vector primitives. Tests use real chunked HTTP, not just fetch mocks. On process crash scratch leftovers are removed only from this gateway's own spool prefix with explicit age/ownership checks; no accepted artifact cleanup.

### 7D.5 Route, page layout and user journeys

New canonical UI route: `#/projects/:projectId/repositories/:repositoryId/analyses/:analysisId/architecture/:versionId/intelligence`; equivalent pathname accepted. Parse before existing exact-model/legacy fallthrough and end-anchor it. Query inside hash allows `tab=diagrams|search|knowledge|reports`, `template`, selected canonical `entity_id`, and exact `report_id/generation_id` on Reports. Invalid combinations show a route error; no generic fallback to an unrelated page. No token/cursor/PIT/raw query payload stored in URL. UUID validation accepts v4/v5 and scope is validated again against `architectureService.getVersion`.

Repository detail adds an **Intelligence** entry beside existing technical tabs. It opens a bounded chooser of analyses/current published outputs using existing paged analysis listing and analysis→architecture lookup **only when selected**, then navigates to an exact URL before rendering outputs. No latest/version guess in the workspace. Exact supplied historical version is loaded directly and remains selected even when a newer model publishes. Without a version, show preparation/no-output state and existing ingestion/start-analysis/analysis links. A version chooser accepts an explicit version ID/link with full scope validation; choices discovered from analyses are labelled current-per-analysis rather than complete history. A paged all-versions catalog requires future public-contract change control and is not necessary here.

Page layout: reuse DashboardHeader, repository content width, breadcrumbs `Projects / Project / Repository / Analysis / Architecture vN / Intelligence`. Compact scope header shows repository/revision, version number/ID, captured/published time and links to analysis, canonical model and exact Explorer. Revision display resolves via existing revision metadata for snapshot_id where supported; otherwise show the immutable snapshot UUID with unavailable revision label (never fabricate commit SHA). Below: delivery strip with separate Search/four diagrams/Report slots, reasons and authorized failed-work retry. No invented aggregate percent. Primary tabs followed by one active panel; technical model/Explorer remain secondary links. Existing global dark tokens/typography/buttons/cards and restrained spacing apply.

| Panel/component | Presentation and interaction | Loading/empty/error behavior |
|---|---|---|
| DiagramPanel |Four template selector with independent availability; lazy DiagramView; generation badge/coverage; map and accessible structured list; inspector actions above collapsed/paged evidence; existing pan/zoom/focus/group collapse/export |Show each slot progressively; no all-four wait. Empty static data-flow explanation distinct from failed generation. Failed view retains previous verified view; update banner instead of camera reset. Search/graph failure does not block diagrams |
| SearchPanel |Explicit lexical form, document/entity/status filters, readable ranked cards, one page and Next/Previous cursor history; canonical subject/evidence actions |Disabled semantic/hybrid with capability reason; submission/cancel; empty results distinct from unavailable projection. STALE readable with label; UNKNOWN never CURRENT. 409/410 offers explicit restart |
| KnowledgePanel |Subject selector (UUID/logical/name), candidate choice, operation form for seven C3 operations; explicit deterministic “Build context” action and facts/relationships/paths/omissions tabs |Ambiguous/incomplete results require user choice; optional graph/search loss shown from source observations; no automatic name match or AI summary. No pagination for ContextBundle; bounded query/evidence cursors |
| EvidencePanel (shared drawer) |Source type/path/range/producer/strength/polarity/fact key, uncertainty/conflicts and reference provenance; one page, long IDs collapsed; actions always reachable |No arbitrary source fetching/excerpts; escape text. Focus trap/close/return target; no simultaneous duplicate evidence fetch per subject |
| ReportPanel |Automatic slot status, paged resources, exact resource detail, paged accepted generation history, size/pages/date/version/coverage/readability; explicit Download PDF |WAITING/BUILDING visible without blocking diagrams. READY metadata-only not corrupt/verified inference. Missing/mismatch download fails with visible message; previous accepted history remains accessible |

At desktop≥1024px, panel + bounded detail drawer; at tablet768–1023, drawer overlays with labelled modal and map keeps usable area; at narrow360–767, wrap controls and stack cards, horizontal scroll only within table/canvas. Buttons/headers never wrap by letter; min-width table columns and overflow-wrap for identifiers only. Tabs support arrow/Home/End keyboard navigation and aria-controls/selected/tabpanel; status announcements polite, failures role=alert. Heading hierarchy, labelled forms, visible focus, escape/drawer restoration, selected-node “View on map” exact focus, keyboard list equivalent and reduced-motion support required. Visual QA at360/768/1024/1440, text zoom200%; no global horizontal overflow or auto-fit on ordinary selection/status refresh.

### 7D.6 Frontend data/state contracts

Reuse apiClient's bearer/refresh logic; add `apiClient.download` inside that module and bounded response options for P5 JSON, while preserving current defaults. AbortError stays identifiable, no auth refresh/logout on403, at most one retry after401; aborted scope never retries. Binary reading uses Response.body with bounded byte accumulation (PDF≤64MiB), validates content-type/length/SHA-256/expected history digest **before** Blob creation; safe filename and object URL revoked after click/unmount. No window.open URL with bearer query, persistent PDF cache or direct storage URL. Do not use the current text parser for PDF. One click per active exact generation; cancellation does not create an error toast.

Services `deliveryService,knowledgeService,diagramService,reportService` own transport calls/URL construction. `knowledgeService` covers C1 + C3 to avoid a redundant second gateway. Domain-specific runtime contract validators check all rendered/navigational fields and public schemas/versions, nested identities, nullable/optional fields and safe finite numbers; preserve unrendered fields unchanged. C2 validation reuses existing complete schema/digest validators. Producer schemas and positive/negative fixtures are exported in B1 from public Python models (no server installation/runtime mutation) into new consumer fixtures; Express/React contract tests run against the same artifacts. Reject unsupported schema before rendering. No rewrites of C1/C2/C3/C4 fields or logical IDs.

`useRepositoryIntelligence` owns one validated canonical context and metadata load; `useIntelligenceDelivery` owns delivery/status observations; `useIntelligenceQuery` owns one panel request/page chain; `useIntelligenceDiagram` owns chosen template/exact PreparedDiagram and passes stable callbacks into existing useDiagramView; `useReportHistory` owns resource/history/download state. Page composes them. Each request is keyed by project/repo/analysis/snapshot/version + operation/template/resource/generation/query, with AbortController and monotonic sequence guarding both fetch and asynchronous validation. Reset paginated state on request/filter/scope change, retain signed original filters/limit per chain, never auto-restart a continuation. Store cursor back-stack for Previous only in mounted panel memory. C1 pin identity and C3 source observations remain bound per chain.

One workspace-owned poll timer, scheduled after previous response settles: propose3s while any relevant delivery slot is PENDING/QUEUED/RUNNING/RETRYING/WAITING_FOR_DIAGRAMS, backoff5/10/30s on transient availability failure; stop after three consecutive failures with manual Resume; stop terminal/non-scheduled/ineligible runtime-unavailable resources; pause hidden tab/offline and abort on unmount/scope change. A WAITING report with no diagram work/runtime capability is shown with reason, not polled forever. Terminal changes trigger one invalidation of diagram collection/search status/report collection; no endpoint-per-node polling or permanent context/search polling. A search completed-work rebuild is observed via search/status separately until resource settles, since delivery's original bundle may be terminal. Terminal failure with readable prior generation keeps read actions and stops polling. Manual Resume is explicit. Never retry mutations automatically; keep the same request idempotency key after ambiguous result until resolved.

Only active panel loads heavy data; preserve camera/selection per `(version,template,generation)` in mounted state with bounded most-recent-per-template retention; release previous large buffers when generation switches. Ordinary selection/polls/inspector width changes do not change geometry or auto-fit. C2 server layout remains authoritative; derive via public /view, consume returned unchanged geometry. **No new diagram layout worker/ELK instance**: existing M4 useDiagramView/adapter is sufficient. Existing P4 worker/Explorer behavior remains frozen. No persisted workspace/localStorage server records; existing auth token storage is unchanged.

### 7D.8 Three sequential batches and gates

**B1 — Transport and foundation.** Prerequisites: owner approves/freezes this entire plan/C5 manifest, including narrowly scoped Express/API-client/config extensions, verified spool policy and deferrals; accepted M1–M5 worktree/commit is available. Scope/files are all B1 rows in §7D.7; include every route, report JSON and binary relay foundation now. Order: export public contract fixtures → source parity → validator/authorization/explicit route registration → opt-in gateway bounded JSON/artifact streaming+config → service adapters/consumer validation → lazy route/header/scope/error-boundary shell. Do not populate B2 panel behavior or B3 report UX yet.

B1 tests:21-route/method matrix against real controlled HTTP and Mongo authorization, all roles/outsider/no-auth, browser header spoofing, repeated filters/duplicate scalar, UUIDv5/unsafe path/malformed encoding, route/body scope conflicts, 202/error/status/cause preservation, request/actual-response overflows, redirect denial, POST input-end vs real response disconnect, no secrets, backpressure/cancellation/timeout through body read, checksum/length rejection, spool admission/cleanup, pre-existing JSON/graph/SSE regression; frontend contract/service/shell/route/auth tests. Gate: C5 source parity and all focused + existing Express/frontend suites/build pass; exact version guards and authenticated binary foundation demonstrated. Producer DTO disagreement, wrong role, uncapped body, unsafe error, missing abort cleanup or old-workflow regression blocks B2. C5 is an implementation candidate until passing parity; upfront approval governs expected contract, no separate discretionary mid-module design approval.

### 7D.9 Verification, performance and Definition of Done

Existing commands (discovered, not executed during planning): `npm --prefix express test` (Vitest+mongodb-memory-server), `npm --prefix frontend test` (four legacy Node suites then Vitest), `npm --prefix frontend run build`; focused file arguments supported by actual Vitest scripts. New Python parity: `./venv/bin/python -m pytest tests/p5/transport -q`; applicable frozen consumer regression `./venv/bin/python -m pytest tests/p5/delivery/test_api.py tests/p5/knowledge/test_api.py tests/p5/knowledge/test_retrieval_api.py tests/p5/diagrams/test_api.py tests/p5/reports/test_api.py -q`. New exporter/check commands must be documented when implemented. Black/Ruff/Mypy apply to any added Python fixture-export/test code using existing repo configuration. There is no configured frontend/Express lint/typecheck script: do not claim one passed. No application tests/build/benchmark/install ran for this plan.

Security/test matrix spans: project outsider403; wrong version/project404/403 per owner; repository/analysis/snapshot mismatch rejected by workspace even when IDs are individually valid; unauthorized exact artifacts; forged IDs/filters/cursors; all upstream status distinctions; secrets/CRLF/path leakage and redirects; active/current/freshness/availability differences; source-score uncertainty; byte/digest/capacity corruption and client disconnect; stale frontend generation after route changes; polling overlap/terminal behavior; producer contract drift; accessibility and existing-flow preservation. Use real controlled HTTP streams for transport behavior and real Python/PostgreSQL/artifact adapters for product integration; mocks establish component behavior only. Keep M4 production certification/deferred tests visibly distinct.

Measurement policy: reuse M4's150/500-node interaction acceptance and real prepared C2 fixtures, not a new whole-repository graph rendering target. Record shell/navigation time, per-panel fetch/parse/validation/render time, JS long tasks, current/peak browser and Express memory, request counts, active requests, bytes/temp disk, time-to-cancel and time-to-first verified download. Proposed engineering targets: cached shell interactive≤1s on documented laptop; local transport overhead metadata p95≤100ms excluding Python/network/body parsing; cancel upstream and remove spool≤1s after local disconnect (≤2s at test scheduling boundary); max one status poll in flight; no geometry changes on selection; warmed150/500-node focus/pan/zoom response≤200ms p95 measured in browser; control/search form remains usable while active panel loads. These are proposed measurement gates, not achieved benchmarks or production SLA. Record hardware/runtime/sample sizes≥20 warmed interactions and five cold loads; measure64MiB relay with a slow client and admitted-concurrency limits. If target missed, profile and correct within M6, preserve producer safeguards; material infeasibility triggers scoped change control. Slow25k canonical C3 retrieval remains allowed under existing deployment policy and cancel UX; do not resurrect the removed five-second M3 gate.

M6 DoD: exact repository/revision/version visible throughout; all four READY diagrams individually usable/exportable through Express; C1 lexical and C3 deterministic inspection/cursors/coverage work; M1 status and authorized idempotent retry work without invented lifecycle; C4 paged resources/history/exact verified PDF works through authenticated boundary; safe abort/errors/403 behavior and no browser secrets/direct Python; keyboard/viewport/map-list alternatives pass; existing ingestion/analysis/model/Explorer regressions and build pass; real journey evidence and deployment limitations recorded here. No new migrations/producer dependency changes expected. If disabled runtime config prevents live product journey, report precise missing configuration/provisioning and finish tests/harness; do not enable unapproved production policies or claim E2E from mocks.

Await explicit start instruction, then B1→B2→B3 proceeds by the gates above; M7 detailed planning remains outside this task.
