###### 15.5.2 Data flow, content and preparation snapshot

`M1 claimed report WorkExecutionContext + four committed receipts → exact scope validation → capture/reuse immutable ReportInputSnapshot → validate ReportSpec + all four prepared diagrams/assets → isolated PDF render/validate → stage, independently read/hash and seal → commit SEALED_VERIFIED metadata → return C0 PreparedOutput → M1 enlisted publication/acceptance → authorized exact-generation retrieval`.

1. Validate WorkExecutionContext and barrier against report binding, bundle and input fingerprint before querying anything. M1 has already checked committed readiness; M5 additionally opens all four physical exact artifacts through DiagramReadPort. Capture exactly system_overview, component_architecture, dependency_map and data_flow in that fixed order, each once with full validated prepared content and receipt. Missing/corrupt prerequisites fail this report; never substitute current diagrams, invoke M4 generation or poll/wait inside the handler.
2. An M5-owned bounded read-only REPEATABLE READ transaction composes public analysis/canonical services and captures analysis terminal status, persisted coverage, all diagnostics, typed canonical aggregate counts and selected rows/references. Validate exact requested Architecture Version with get_version; do not use get_for_analysis/latest or reject a legitimate historical version merely because AnalysisResponse.published_version_id points elsewhere.
3. Get no names from MongoDB/legacy Python projects. Initial metadata uses actual project/repository/analysis/snapshot/version UUIDs; display names and revision SHA are explicitly unavailable unless supplied by an approved immutable public contract. Snapshot ID is not a fabricated Git SHA. No new Express request is in M5.
4. One complete prepared snapshot is insert-once per ReportResource, committed outside M1 publication, with checksum and captured_at fixed at first successful capture. Concurrent captures race through compare-and-set: only the committed winner is used; losing workers discard their snapshot and revalidate ownership. Retries reuse it, including diagnostics, all four prepared diagrams/assets and provenance, rather than silently collecting newer inputs. A crash before complete capture may retry capture; no visible PDF or semantic-replay guarantee exists before that first commit.
5. Canonical scans count all relevant source rows once in bounded pages. Retain at most the explicit report selections/evidence, not complete duplicated architecture. Selection is deterministic by canonical kind, UTF-8 namespace/logical_id then UUID; totals, selected/omitted counts and rules are printed. Select architecture-boundary/application/service/controller/endpoint/persistence/integration records, not every function/module. Do not derive new application ownership; use only actual canonical relationships.
6. All stored analysis diagnostics must be represented, including original info/warning/error/critical severity, IDs, category/code/stage/scope and available source references. Order critical,error,warning,info then stage/code/UUID; that is display order, not a new severity calculation. No separate finding detector/catalog. Collector-cap/drop indicators appear only when actually persisted; otherwise “not available,” never zero. Empty diagnostics explicitly do not mean “no vulnerabilities.”
7. Persist only allowlisted public DTO fields. Never copy free-form configuration/details/metadata blobs, source excerpts, credentials, host paths or storage keys. Preserve original safe diagnostic message/severity and actual provenance; unsafe/unexportable text is represented by source ID/code and an explicit withheld-text limitation, not invented wording or altered severity. Unknown enum/severity is a typed incompatibility, not silently mapped. Relative source paths/ranges remain actual values, never normalized into invented locations.

Report section order is fixed in template `architecture_analysis_v1`:

| Order / section | Requirement and exact content |
|---|---|
|1 Metadata/cover|Mandatory: report type/logical report ID, exact scope/version/analysis IDs, canonical digest, captured_at, template/C4/renderer/font versions, deterministic-mode label; names/SHA unavailable honestly. No executive summary.|
|2 Inventory and established boundaries|Mandatory: canonical counts by kind/status/relationship kind, chosen boundary inventory and existing relationships with direction, selected/omitted counts and source refs. Zero/unknown/conflicting are distinct.|
|3 Architecture diagrams|Mandatory: embed system_overview, component_architecture, dependency_map and data_flow in that fixed order, each from its exact accepted spec/layout/glyph assets, with title, legend, canonical scope, coverage/omissions and reference/profile/digests. Option 3 is owner-approved: compact multi-diagram pages where readable, full-page/tiled presentation otherwise. No diagram is provenance-only, omitted, re-generated or re-laid-out by M5.|
|4 Existing diagnostics|Mandatory, including empty-state disclaimer. Every retained authoritative diagnostic; original severity/provenance. Paginate rather than drop warnings; capacity failure prevents publication.|
|5 Coverage and limitations|Mandatory: exact canonical completeness + separate actual analysis completeness; do not force mismatched definitions into one percent. Unresolved/conflict counts, bounded selected reference catalog, unavailable languages/runtime/file details and diagram limitations explicitly identified. No “runtime verified” claim.|
|6 Provenance/appendix/notices|Mandatory: all four full exact diagram reference identities/receipt IDs/digests/profiles and coverage, source record refs, snapshot/digest of input capture, generator/renderer/font licenses, selection policy, omitted totals. TOC/page numbers derive from these sections.|
|Optional fields only|Nullable names/SHA and missing source ranges/producer/fact-key fields are labelled unavailable; sections are not silently removed. No optional network enrichment, new diagram choice or AI block.|

8–12 pages remains a readability target, not a hard document size. All four diagrams and long diagnostic/evidence content may require more pages; the readability target never overrides complete embedding. If a resource/page ceiling would omit mandatory warnings, fail closed rather than emit an apparently complete report. The appendix is bounded through explicit selected/omitted coverage, not hidden first-N truncation.

###### 15.5.3 C4 v1.0.0 approved contract design — schemas, identity and compatibility

All approved-to-implement records are closed, strict, deeply immutable Pydantic values with bounded tuples and no arbitrary JSON dictionaries. Reject duplicate keys, nonfinite/floating JSON numbers, invalid UTF-8/surrogates, unknown enum/version/profile, negative counts and unsafe paths at ingress and serialization. Serialization must revalidate unsafe model_construct/model_copy values. C4 is new; reuse C0 and C2 objects, not copied schemas.

| Approved contract type (`reports/contracts.py` unless stated) | Required fields / invariant |
|---|---|
|ReportSourceRef|source_kind=analysis_diagnostic/entity/relationship/evidence/unresolved/conflict; analysis/version IDs as applicable, actual UUID/logical_id; nullable source_record_type/artifact_id/source_fact_key/path/range/producer_id/version/strength/polarity copied only if present. Diagnostics without canonical evidence never get fabricated Evidence IDs or analyzer provenance.|
|ReportDiagnostic|source ref, original severity(info/warning/error/critical), code/category/scope/stage, safe message or withheld reason, created_at, retryability, optional actual artifact/path/line/column and existing evidence refs. No M5 risk/severity/confidence score.|
|ReportCoverage|typed canonical completeness fields; optional typed analysis completeness; counts selected/omitted by report section; canonical unresolved/conflict totals; original diagnostic-cap indication if exposed; explicit missing-fields/limitations codes.|
|ReportMetadata|C4/schema/template/type/report_id; C0 VersionScope and HandlerProfile; captured_at UTC; nullable display names/revision with availability; source_snapshot_digest; renderer/font/asset manifest versions/digests. No candidate/fence/lease or changing current time in semantic report data.|
|ReportSection|closed tagged union of MetadataSection, InventorySection, DiagramsSection, DiagnosticsSection, CoverageSection, ProvenanceSection, in exactly the order above. DiagramsSection contains exactly four ordered diagram inputs plus typed report-page placement records. Inventory/ref lists typed; headings are template-owned, not user markup.|
|ReportDiagramInput|C0 PublicationReceipt plus C2 scope/template/schema/profile/semantic/layout/member digests, coverage and provenance; exactly one of each template. Every entry includes its full validated PreparedDiagram and exact receipt; no provenance-only stand-ins. Store each prepared value once, reference it from section placements, and validate template/scope/schema/profile/semantic/layout/member digests.|
|ReportDiagramPlacement|PDF-presentation-only record: exact template/reference, one-based diagram-section page index, closed slot ID, positive rational scale numerator/denominator, integer milli-point translation and clip rectangle, optional tile row/column and caption. Reject overlaps/out-of-range rectangles; golden profiles fix half-even coordinate rounding and slot/gutter dimensions. Diagram-section indexes are resolved to final PDF page numbers during pagination; no feedback into canonical diagram identity or C2 layout.|
|ReportSpec|c4_version=1.0.0, report_type=architecture_analysis, metadata, ordered sections, four diagram inputs, coverage and report_spec_digest. All four embedded inputs must match their exact receipts and every dependency shares the exact scope/canonical digest.|
|ReportInputSnapshot (internal)|ReportSpec + immutable trusted font bytes/asset descriptors and all four diagram vector/glyph assets; snapshot digest/size/capture time; atomic insert-once per resource. Serializable prepared data only: no session/reader/path/API/callback credentials.|
|PreparedReport (renderer contract)|validated ReportSpec + verified font/glyph asset pack + pinned RendererProfile and finite limits. Renderer receives bytes/numbers/text only. Immutable page placements bind template/reference, page/slot, scale/translation, clip/tile rectangle and caption; this is PDF pagination, not a replacement C2 diagram layout.|
|RenderedReport|media_type=application/pdf, PDF bytes, sha256, size, page_count, report_spec_digest, renderer_profile_digest, validation_version. No lifecycle or readiness assertion.|
|ReportArtifactManifest/Descriptor|artifact_schema_version1.0.0, full C0 scope/binding/input_fingerprint and attempt/work/run/candidate/fence/epoch binding, resource/generation/artifact IDs, source/spec/render digests, the ordered four-diagram identity/digest set and report-page placement digest, PDF page count and exact two members: spec→report-spec.json(application/json), pdf→report.pdf(application/pdf), sizes/hashes. Descriptor pairs manifest with exact C0 ArtifactReference.|
|ReportSummary/ReportCollection/ReportGenerationPage (api_contracts)|safe exact scope/report ID, report type/profile, accepted reference?, M1 WorkSummary?, availability/readability, page/byte/digest/coverage metadata?, reason/observed_at; bounded items + signed next_cursor. No internal paths/lease tokens/fences.|
|ReportReadPort|read_exact(scope,reference)→prepared spec+validated descriptor metadata; open_exact(scope,reference,member=pdf)→shared bounded verified handle. Query code reauthorizes separately; a reference/token is not permission.|

Canonical C4 JSON algorithm: recursively encode UUIDs lowercase, timestamps UTC with six fractional digits+Z, enums as exact strings; object keys sorted by UTF-8 bytes, meaningful arrays preserve order, set-like refs sorted by (source_kind,UUID/logical_id) and duplicate-free. Encode integers in the exact ±(2^53−1) range, booleans/null, UTF-8 scalars without normalization; compact separators, no BOM/newline, short controls use lowercase \\u00xx, quote/backslash escaped. Publish golden cross-language schema/bytes/digests. Do not import M4's private canonicalizer as an unowned report contract.

- Keep C0 `work.input_fingerprint == barrier.input_fingerprint()` unchanged. `resource_id = UUID5(NAMESPACE_URL,"stacksense:report-resource:1:"+C0.fingerprint("report.resource",{scope.semantic_identity(),binding,input_fingerprint}))`; report_id is this logical resource UUID, not a database sequence or PDF hash.
- `source_snapshot_digest = SHA256(J(frozen captured DTO data + exact four receipts + asset hashes))`; `report_spec_digest = SHA256(J(["ss.report.spec","1.0.0",spec-without-report_spec_digest]))`. `render_fingerprint = SHA256(J(["ss.report.render","1.0.0",report_spec_digest,renderer profile,font/asset hashes,presentation configuration,deterministic four-diagram page-placement policy]))`. These are distinct from the frozen C0 fingerprint.
- New generation/artifact persistence UUIDs belong to each candidate/attempt; one candidate binding produces at most one generation. ReportSpec/PDF contain the stable report_id and captured_at, not attempt UUIDs/fence/dispatch timestamps or their own PDF hash. Manifest/API associate actual generation IDs. Thus the identical committed snapshot/assets/profile yields byte-identical PDF across replay without circular hash/identity dependencies.
- C0 candidate `digests.content` hashes the complete manifest; `digests.spec` is report_spec_digest; layout=null. Reference size is manifest length, **not PDF length**. PDF size/hash live in the member descriptor/API. AcceptedOutput.require_candidate remains unmodified.
- Compatibility matrix v1: C4/spec/artifact/template/report producer1.0.0 consumes exact C0/C2/source schemas1.0.0 and actual pinned M4 producer/profile versions in each receipt. M4 helper/profile changes are not a cue to recompute old diagrams: accept validated immutable values only; no current-profile substitution. Unknown major/minor schema/profile fails typed409; new generator/font/render settings change the report binding/fingerprint for newly authorized work, never reinterpret prior artifacts.
- No M1 completed-report rebuild API exists. M1's existing additive successor facility is **search-only**. M5 does not extend it or create changed-profile/report work. Schema/history can retain future independently authorized generations, but this module only processes work M1 already supplies and retries eligible failed work.

###### 15.5.4 Shared storage incompatibility and owner-approved change control (D-M5-01)

**Scope approval — SATISFIED by full-plan owner authorization, 2026-11-20:** implement only the narrow member-typed shared read-Protocol amendment in B1; no separate owner approval before B1. **Mandatory engineering gate before B2:** the implemented amendment must pass independent review and all required regressions proving unchanged M4 concrete storage behavior and C0/C2 contracts. Failed or skipped required checks block progression until corrected. Approval is not proof; independent review is not a recurring owner planning checkpoint. Approved minimal additive scope:

1. CREATE `backend/platform/delivery/artifacts.py` as a **small shared read Protocol only**, with `VerifiedArtifactHandle.read(size)/close()` and contravariant member type parameter on `GeneratedArtifactStore[MemberT].open_exact(scope,reference,member)`. It imports C0, not M4/M5. It is not a new storage framework, lifecycle owner or M1 coordinator implementation.
2. Amend only `backend/platform/diagrams/artifacts.py:GeneratedArtifactStore` to extend the shared Protocol specialized to its **unchanged** four-member Literal. Keep its existing authority property, open_exact signature, VerifiedMemberHandle structural compatibility and all behavior/C2 wire records unchanged. Do not move/refactor the concrete M4 store, sealer, manifest, directory policy or accepted authority. Under the already-approved B1 scope, the B2 engineering gate requires independent amendment review and passing regressions before B2 begins. Regression evidence must include strict typing of existing read adapters, unchanged C0/C2 schema/bytes/digests and four-member rejection behavior, focused C2 consumer/read/seal/export tests and real diagram storage/publication integration tests. No report support may depend on modifying M4 concrete behavior or manifests.
3. CREATE M5 `reports/artifacts.py` implementing the same shared Protocol for the closed Literal[spec,pdf], with ReportArtifactManifest and M5 accepted-read authority. This is a new producer-specific adapter, not a second incompatible generic store or reuse of diagram-private writer/sealer internals. Reuse only existing public bounded handle/port concepts and standard filesystem/PG patterns.
4. The amendment scope is approved; only evidence that it cannot meet frozen invariants triggers scoped change control. Otherwise correct failed checks within scope and rerun, without a new planning cycle. Never store PDF under “svg,” broaden M4's member allowlist implicitly, duplicate M4's private implementation or present the missing `delivery/artifacts.py` as already implemented.

The unchanged two-member report format contains all four prepared diagram inputs in report-spec.json and all four embeddings in report.pdf; no extra SVG/PDF members or mutable pointers into M4 storage. The snapshot/spec hash and immutable generation bind their exact ordered receipts, geometry/assets and placement policy, so retry/retrieval never chooses a newer diagram. Independent acceptance validation checks completeness of all four before sealing and metadata publication.

Report local store: private kind-specific staging/sealed/locks namespace, server-issued UUID directory names, no caller paths; independently copied regular files/new inodes, exclusive non-overwriting promotion, fsync file+directory, full readback/hash, no symlinks/hardlinks/special files. Stage only exact allowlisted spec/PDF/manifest members. Filesystem sealing and C0 acceptance remain distinct. Application-level immutability experiments are not evidence that a worker principal physically cannot modify accepted bytes.

Follow existing essential-vs-production separation rather than adding a new service: real PG/local sealing/publication/read/cleanup correctness is mandatory M5 acceptance; intended-host physical write exclusion/principal/OS deployment proofs remain an explicit production gate. **D-M5-05 is OWNER APPROVED for M5 functional closure; intended-deployment physical qualification is still separate, unproven and not authorized by functional acceptance.** If no physically qualified adapter exists, report production registration stays disabled; chmod/rename or a Literal[True] alone never proves a production seal. No administrator provisioning, S3 adapter, new queue or production infrastructure is implemented as part of this plan.

###### 15.5.5 Renderer, fonts, determinism and PDF validation

Owner-approved initial pinned compatibility candidates: **ReportLab5.1.0** and **pypdf7.1.1** (validation only; no SVG/PDF import/merge). Official PyPI metadata lists Python3.14 for both, not proof of this repository's execution. No extras (Cairo/shaping/bidi/browser), host fonts or new image service. Existing fonttools4.68.0 remains unchanged. Sources inspected2026-11-20: [ReportLab release](https://pypi.org/project/reportlab/5.1.0/), [pypdf release](https://pypi.org/project/pypdf/7.1.1/), [ReportLab font API](https://docs.reportlab.com/reportlab/userguide/ch3_fonts/), [graphics API](https://docs.reportlab.com/reportlab/userguide/ch2_graphics/). B1 must execute actual Python3.14 compatibility/security/license/dependency and byte-reproducibility checks, then record exact effective dependency/profile pins before B2. No new owner approval is required to retain validated candidates. Disproved candidates use scoped change control for compatible replacements, not unrelated redesign. No install/lock/proof is claimed now.

- `ReportRenderer.render(PreparedReport,deadline,ExecutionControl)->RenderedReport` is provider-free content→PDF. Section building/querying happens beforehand. A separate bounded child is supervised by M5's `render_runtime.py`: minimal environment, no inherited credentials/DB/API clients/unnecessary FDs, private scratch, finite RLIMIT/process/output limits, wall watchdog and process-group kill/reap≤2s. Parent maintains C0 checkpoints and uses min(remaining M1 deadline, M5 stage budget). No renderer thread can outlive a timed-out accepted operation.
- No untrusted HTML/XML/Markdown execution or remote fonts/images. Render paragraphs/tables from escaped plain text. Child receives preloaded verified font bytes and prepared vectors only; ReportLab library/standard-library imports are trusted code, not runtime project/source discovery. Reject/report any attempt to open a network/database/source input; test actual disabled network access and absence of inherited handles/credentials. If the selected deployment cannot enforce required execution restrictions, capability stays unavailable; do not substitute a claim of full OS certification.
- Use fixed A4 portrait,36pt margins, body9pt/12pt leading, headings14/11pt and tables≥8pt; fixed template palette. Repeat table headers, split rows/paragraphs explicitly, prevent orphan headings, paginate long identifiers/messages without character loss. Fixed heading-derived bookmarks/TOC, page numbers and source references. No live links to user-provided URLs.
- Reuse M4 DejaVu regular/bold bytes+license via the existing asset manifest (no duplicate font installation). Hash/length verify before render, register from BytesIO, pin subset/order settings. Latin/Greek/Cyrillic supported as measured; combining sequences preserved. Unsupported glyphs and scripts requiring unavailable shaping are visibly escaped as [U+XXXX] with limitations recorded; preserve original Unicode in internal spec. Never silently use tofu, reorder bidi into false text or download a fallback font. B1 proves the precise supported/escaped policy on CJK/emoji/RTL/control/BOM/CRLF/combining fixtures.
- Draw each of the four diagrams directly from its unchanged C2 boxes/routes/prepared glyph paths with a deterministic coordinate transform (milli_css_px÷1000, invert Y into PDF space); handle M/L/Q/C/Z, including exact quadratic→cubic conversion, glyph instances, groups, arrows and legends. No SVG parser/raster screenshot/ELK/new canonical queries. Reuse repeated glyphs as deterministic form objects where beneficial.
- Deterministic Option 3 page packing: retain the fixed four-template order; try compact fixed two-by-two slots, then two vertical slots on A4 portrait, using only consecutive diagrams. Accept a shared-page placement only when every prepared label, arrow, relationship label, legend and caption is complete and readable at the proposed minimum label size (initial engineering floor 6pt, subject to B1 readability validation; increase the floor within the approved readable-layout policy if required, never reduce readability), with nonoverlapping captions and fixed gutters. If it fails, give that diagram a full portrait or landscape page using the larger readable scale (portrait wins ties). If neither fits readably, tile the unchanged drawing at the readable scale in row-major order with overlap, tile IDs and a key map. Tiling continues the same diagram; it never replaces or drops any part. Slot order, dimensions, rounding and tie-breaks are pinned in RendererProfile and golden fixtures; no nondeterministic packing.
- Packing/tiling changes PDF placement only: uniform scaling, translation, page framing and explicit overlapping clip windows, never graph/node/route/label rearrangement, simplification, new layout, regeneration or semantic transformation. Do not replace M4's prepared glyph shapes with reinterpreted text. Preserve each diagram's title, legend, original content, exact version/reference and coverage. A sparse/empty diagram still receives its authoritative empty-state view. Excess aggregate tile/page budget fails clearly; never shrink-to-illegible, silently omit one of the four, trim relationships or exceed a limit to satisfy the 8–12-page target. Prove all-four compact/full-page/tiled/large-hub/long-label cases in B1/B2.
- Pin ReportLab invariant mode, compression policy, page sizes, locale/TZ, font order, glyph subset/object traversal and renderer/Python/zlib versions in RendererProfile. Use captured_at only for displayed generation time; PDF internal dates/IDs fixed deterministically. No wall clock, randomUUID, hostname or filesystem path enters bytes.
- Validate output **before sealing** with pinned pypdf in bounded child: strict parse, exact page count/media boxes, nonencrypted, embedded fonts as selected, metadata/ReportSpec binding, exactly four distinct template embeddings matching the accepted input identities/digests and validated page placements, no JavaScript/Launch/OpenAction/AA/embedded files/external URI/remote GoTo, no extra attachments, bounded decompressed streams/object traversal. Check PDF header/EOF/size/hash in addition; a magic prefix alone is insufficient. Reader rechecks accepted metadata, byte length/hash and pinned validation attestation; corrupted historical bytes fail, never re-render on download.
- B1 pass gate: import/install on real Python3.14; three cold-process renders of each identical committed input/assets/profile with identical spec and PDF SHA256; all-four compact/wide/full-page/tiled/empty/Unicode/long-warning fixtures, including mixed diagram sizes; verify all template captions and all placed nodes/routes/glyph instances against prepared input, no clipping outside intentional tile windows and complete tile coverage. Visually inspect every page at readable scale (including arrowheads/relationship labels/legends/captions); page count or PDF text extraction alone cannot establish vector-glyph completeness. Prove full-page fallback when compact slots fail readability. Then require PDF parser success and actual Poppler raster/text inspection. Different TZ/locale/insertion order cannot alter semantics/bytes. Any nondeterminism or unusable Unicode/pagination means fail and stop affected batch; do not silently downgrade to “semantic determinism only,” change fonts/versions or weaken guarantees. Document measurements and seek targeted change control if the selected approach is disproved.

###### 15.5.6 PostgreSQL, publication and recovery

Approved additive table design (no upstream/multi-database FK):

- `report_resource` / ReportResourceModel: stable UUID PK, project/version/type/profile/input fingerprints, immutable scope/binding/four-receipt barrier, captured input snapshot+hash/time (insert-once), nullable accepted_generation_id, created_at. Unique(project,version,report_type,binding_fingerprint,input_fingerprint); type=architecture_analysis. The one captured snapshot is bounded JSONB/byte payload, not raw source or a mutable workspace.
- `report_generation` / ReportGenerationModel: generation/artifact UUIDs, resource FK+scope composite key, candidate/work/run/attempt/fence/epoch binding JSON, spec/render/snapshot digests, manifest/spec snapshot including the exact four prepared diagram records and page-placement digest, PDF size/hash/page count, store seal/validation attestations, accepted C0 reference/receipt and timestamps; unique(candidate_id), unique(artifact_id), composite uniqueness(scope,resource,generation). No storage key or absolute path in public DTO.
- `report_read_pin` / ReportReadPinModel: bounded expiring pin UUID, generation FK, actor or trusted report-system purpose, expiry/created_at; indexed generation/expiry, count quota. No source/diagram persistence modifications.

ReportResource is output identity, **not another job**. Durable generation staging states only: STAGING→SEALED_VERIFIED→ACCEPTED; invalid preacceptance candidate→INVALID; safe retired unpublished candidate→DELETING→DELETED. ACCEPTED content/reference/snapshot immutable; DB constraints/triggers reject postacceptance changes, deletes or scope changes. No independent RUNNING/RETRYING/READY job lifecycle. Public availability derives from accepted readability and M1 WorkSummary: NOT_SCHEDULED/WAITING/BUILDING/READY/FAILED/CANCELLED/UNAVAILABLE; original work states remain verbatim in WorkSummary. A prior accepted generation remains readable regardless of later failure/registration outages.

1. Allocate candidate binding under M5 repository, reuse completed captured snapshot, render and validate. Commit M5 staging metadata and seal attestations on a dedicated connection before returning PreparedOutput.
2. `ReportPublicationPort.publish_prepared/validate_ready` receives only GuardedEnlistedSession+PublicationContext; checks every scope/profile/work/run/attempt/fence/epoch/candidate/generation/artifact/hash/size/member field and prepared binding, locked generation/resource rows and seal attestation. It changes only report acceptance metadata/pointer. **No file I/O or nested commit.** Return AcceptedOutput only; M1 validates/commits the receipt and lifecycle atomically.
3. Readers admit only a committed exact reference matching full descriptor+accepted metadata; pin before bounded read, hash/length verify, final acceptance/auth-scope recheck and release pin on success/error/disconnect. No latest aliases, artifact UUID as authorization or worker-supplied reference as commit proof.
4. Crash after stage/render/seal but before commit leaves unaccepted orphan; crash after sealed metadata may safely revalidate/reuse only the same still-owned attempt; retired attempts cannot mint replacement refs. Crash after M1 commit yields the same downloadable accepted artifact after restart even if dispatch/renderer is unavailable. Duplicate delivery does not create another completed generation; competing/stale fences lose acceptance.
5. Operator-only bounded orphan reconciliation uses public `build_attempt_retirement_reader`/`AttemptRetirementReader`, checks exact retired attempt with no publication and grace, then M5 row/advisory locks+tombstone and no resource/accepted/history/read pins. Publication and cleanup lock in the same order. Wait for active writers to stop; unlink only reserved exact UUID directory. Deletion interrupted after tombstone is resumable. Accepted historical generations are retained indefinitely in v1; no accepted purge/retention duration selected and no cleanup Beat added.
6. Add one named Alembic migration (filename/revision generated at B2, descendant of verified current head `7d4a6c2e910b`, recheck head before implementation). Upgrade fresh/existing populated PG and rollback; downgrade refuses accepted report history instead of deleting it. Model discovery/config/registry/router are narrowly inventoried composition changes, not blanket permission to touch frozen modules.

###### 15.5.7 API, authorization and M6 contract

Only **internal FastAPI** routes under `/api/v1/architecture-versions/{version_id}/reports`; every request requires explicit project_id + existing delegated service/actor/role/project context, published get_version and exact chain checks **before** resource/store access. OWNER/ADMIN/DEVELOPER/VIEWER may read; only OWNER/ADMIN may retry through M1. React calls Express only; M6 later implements the binary gateway, not this module.

| Approved route | Request / response / semantics |
|---|---|
|GET collection|project_id,limit1–100(default20),cursor≤4096. ReportCollection={scope,automatic:ReportSlotStatus,items:ReportSummary[],next_cursor?}; automatic exposes public M1 report WorkSummary/waiting_on and nullable reference separately from persisted resources. No resource yet can still mean WAITING_FOR_DIAGRAMS.|
|GET /{report_id}|ReportSummary={scope,report_id,report_type,profile,input_fingerprint,availability,readability,work?,reference?,pdf_size?,pdf_sha256?,page_count?,spec_digest?,coverage?,created_at,accepted_at?,reason?,observed_at}. Header sizes distinguish manifest from PDF; metadata does not imply fresh physical verification.|
|GET /{report_id}/generations|project_id,limit,cursor → {scope,report_id,items:ReportGenerationSummary[],next_cursor?}; exact accepted generations only, stable high-water+(accepted_at,generationUUID) keyset.|
|GET /{report_id}/generations/{generation_id}|Exact historical generation summary, complete accepted reference and source/renderer profile, no runtime capability dependency.|
|GET /{report_id}/generations/{generation_id}/download|PDF-only bounded preverified response; application/pdf, Content-Length(actualPDF), Content-Digest sha-256, quoted ETag, private,no-store,nosniff, attachment ASCII name report-architecture-v{sequence}-{generation}.pdf. Reject Range416; no public paths/presigned URLs or conditional304 auth bypass.|
|Retry — existing M1 endpoint, not a new report route|Existing `POST /architecture-versions/{version_id}/delivery/retry` with C0 RetryRequest(resource_type=report,resource_id=<M1 work_id>,idempotency_key)→202 RetryAccepted. Verified `DeliveryRepository.retry_failed` locks the work using request.resource_id: it is not M5 report_id. Only current FAILED work is retryable under M1 policy. No POST generate/report-rebuild/current-download endpoint. The old §17.2 report-specific retry suggestion is superseded, not an M1 contract amendment.|

Collection/history cursors are HMAC/domain/version/key-id signed stateless keysets bound to actor/project/exact version/report resource/profile/query/high-water/expiry; every page reauthorizes. Fixed absolute expiry≤600s, no secret default, no generic retrieval session. For an automatic-current selector, pin the resolved M1 bundle/work/reference on first page and reject selection drift409; exact resource history needs no active-generation substitution and stays at its immutable high-water snapshot. All returned generation identities remain exact. Forgery/malformed400, expired410, no silent restart.

Closed sanitized error envelope `{error:{code,message,details:{request_id,dimension?,actual?,limit?}}}`: auth401/403, unknown/cross-scope404, not-ready/reference drift/contract incompatibility409, expired continuation410, query/body422 (invalid cursor400), capacity413, Range416, unavailable renderer/store/DB503, bounded operation504, integrity failure503 (never “not found”), unexpected500. Reason codes are M5-prefixed; map worker failures separately to existing C0 RetryClass. No raw tracebacks/SQL/path/cursor secret/source or incoming error text. Disconnect closes handle/pin. Future M6 must relay statuses/media/headers/binary bytes exactly, never JSON-decode a PDF or expose Python service credentials.

C4 M6 fixtures: collection before scheduling/waiting/running/failed/READY/unavailable, multiple resources for one version, exact historic generation, role matrix, schema/profile incompatibility, missing/corrupt bytes, cursor expiry/drift, retry202 with M1 identity vs report identity, download byte/hash/header and disconnect. Prepared ReportSpec is an internal artifact, **not an alternate user export format**; v1 exposes PDF plus metadata only. No frontend/component changes in M5.

###### 15.5.8 Failure, observability and approved bounded engineering envelope

Map to existing C0 classes: transient DB/store/readiness outage → transient; changed foreign binding/corrupt member/digest/invalid publication → integrity; row/page/bytes/time/memory limits → capacity; unsupported schema/font/glyph policy, invalid mandatory report → permanent. Timeout's classification is capacity unless clearly an underlying transient unavailable service; never classify all exceptions retryable. M1 alone applies configured retry/max-execution/manual policy. Preparation/read failures retain old accepted generations; no work cancel method or new public event vocabulary.

Observe IDs (project/version/resource/work/attempt/generation/profile), safe phase/error/correlation, calls/rows/selected/omitted counts, spec/PDF bytes/pages, preparation/render/validate/seal/read durations, child current/peak RSS, cancellations/cleanup/pins. Never log report text/secret metadata/free-form stderr. Audit actor/operation for retry; no new metrics platform.

**Values below are owner-approved initial bounded engineering configuration for B1, not empirically validated production defaults.** B1 measures the stated workloads and establishes final safe effective settings before B2 without another discretionary owner approval. Tightening or adjustment within the listed hard ceilings requires measured justification and rerun acceptance tests; no unbounded fallback or mandatory-content loss. Raising hard ceilings, disproved safety assumptions or infeasible frozen contracts requires formal change control. Record exact effective profile/config fingerprints before registration; never mutate pinned work or accepted generations. They do not alter M1/C2/source limits. Effective deadline=min(context.deadline,remaining stage/operation budget); statement time, connect/checkout, iteration/mapping, validation and final reference check all count.

| Dimension | Approved initial engineering settings / hard safety ceiling | Exceeded behavior / B1 measurement |
|---|---|---|
|Public source page / cumulative rows / calls|100 /200,000 /2,500; hard500 /500,000 /5,000 (analysis diagnostics still≤100)|Stop typed capacity, no private SQL/N+1/cache workaround. Benchmark worst frozen canonical counts and measure repeated public pagination cost.|
|Selected inventory / appendix references / complete diagnostics|200 /2,000 /1,000; hard2,000 /10,000 /10,000|Only inventory/ref selection may omit with exact visible counts. Never silently truncate diagnostics; fail if full authoritative set cannot fit. Existing upstream diagnostic cap default1000 is not proof no source warnings were dropped.|
|Prepared spec / all-four aggregate embedded assets / PDF / cumulative staged bytes|16 /64 /32 /128MiB; hard64 /128 /64 /256MiB|Count all four geometries/glyph assets together (fonts may be deduplicated without changing identity); reject before allocation/write where possible and enforce during serialization/streaming, not solely after render. No per-diagram reset of the aggregate budget, dropped diagram or alternate image/format fallback.|
|Total PDF pages / aggregate diagram tiles / longest text field|128 /16 /16KiB; hard256 /64 /64KiB|The tile ceiling is the sum across all four diagrams, not a per-diagram allowance; retain complete content/readability or fail, no ellipsis hiding diagnostics. Render continuation lines/pages.|
|Prepare / render+PDF validation / total execution / exact read|120 /60 /240 /15s; hard240 /120 /300 /60s|Include four real M4 exact reads and public scans; M1 may impose a smaller remaining budget. Watchdog actually kills/reaps child≤2s. No threshold raise to pass.|
|Child memory / local concurrent renders / project concurrent renders|512MiB /1 /1; hard1GiB /2 /1|Process-specific measurement (not pytest lifetime RSS); real exhaustion/cancel proof and shared queue fairness. Admission timeout is classified, never a new scheduler.|
|Statement / lock+connect / output stream / read pin|5 /1 /30 /60s; hard15 /3 /120 /180s|Driver cancellation/finite checkout; pin expiry bounds active stream, no infinite renewal. Session/handle closes on all paths.|
|Read/history page / cursor TTL / cursor bytes|20 /600s /4096; hard100 /600 /4096|Reject invalid input, signed pinned continuation; no silent page shrink/switch.|
|Orphan grace / sweep size / accepted retention|B1-measured finite grace /20 /indefinite retain|B1 establishes a finite safe grace from measured writer shutdown/read-pin duration and clock margin before B2; keep cleanup disabled until supplied, without another owner policy vote. Existing public retirement+writer stop+no accepted/pinned references mandatory. No accepted history purge.|

B1 experiment matrix: small20-boundary/0-diagnostic, typical200/100, standard500-node-per-view where supported+1000 diagnostics, full frozen25k-entity/50k-relationship scan with up to75k evidence and10k unresolved/5k conflicts, and over-cap adversarial cases; every report case contains all four actual C2 templates, not four copies of an overview. Include asymmetric/dense/wide/empty views, compact-success, compulsory-full-page and compulsory-tiling fixtures. Measure maximum supported per-template C2 input sizes plus aggregate asset/PDF/page pressure separately from the full canonical scan; three fresh-process runs each. Record source calls/elapsed/current+after-GC+peak RSS separately, four exact M4 reads, preparation, four-view drawing, packing, PDF validation and total times; aggregate/per-view asset/PDF bytes/pages/tiles/font/object counts. Pass only complete four-template identity/content/provenance, readable labels/routes/legends with no unintended overlaps/clipping, times/pages/bytes/process RSS within each explicitly configured candidate limit (table above), byte-equal repeats, valid PDF and bounded child cancellation/reap≤2s with no leaked handles. Record all three raw measurements, maximum and environment; initial engineering approval is not production qualification, even after passing an experiment. Test each dimension just below/at/above its ceiling, actual exhaustion and timeout during the fourth diagram; any excess, missing view or skipped required proof fails the gate. If public-scan cost or rendering exceeds an initial operational setting, correct it or adjust within approved hard ceilings, document measurements and rerun acceptance. If hard bounds, safety or frozen public contracts make the plan infeasible, stop/escalate through scoped change control; no upstream internals, changed limits or unapproved cache. Diagram-read latency near its existing15s budget is a known M4 risk, not a reason to raise it in M5.

###### 15.5.10 Exactly three sequential batches and acceptance gates

**B1 — C4 contracts, preparation, validation and renderer/font compatibility**

- Prerequisites: full-plan authorization resolves D-M5-01–05 and authorizes B1's narrow shared-port amendment, C4/content direction, initial dependency candidates, engineering caps and functional closure. Existing C0/C2/public source contracts and isolated tooling are required inputs. No discretionary owner gate remains. M1–M4 stay frozen otherwise; production flags remain disabled.
- Files: B1 rows above; minimal real renderer compatibility uses the final PreparedReport/RenderedReport interface, not a throwaway alternative architecture. Implement the amendment only within the approved scope; pass its independent review and required regressions before B2 begins (engineering gate), not merely before a later consumer integration.
- Inputs/outputs: actual C0 context/barrier + public source DTOs + exact C2 fixtures/real read port → deeply immutable ReportInputSnapshot/ReportSpec/PreparedReport carrying all four complete diagrams, compact/full-page/tiling fixtures, schema/consumer fixtures and renderer/font/runtime measurement report.
- Acceptance: every required section/status/provenance covered; no raw-source/private upstream imports; original severity incl critical; exact four refs and historical version, all four embedded views with correct glyphs/routes and readable compact/full-page fallback; insert-once snapshot semantics designed and exercised with in-memory port and PG prototype where needed; three cold deterministic PDF samples parse/rasterize correctly; Unicode policy explicit; no network/query dependency, bounded kill/reap; source-scan and resource matrix measured.
- Tests: `pytest -q tests/p5/reports/test_contracts.py tests/p5/reports/test_preparation.py tests/p5/reports/test_validation.py tests/p5/reports/test_renderer_compatibility.py tests/p5/reports/test_c4_consumers.py`; opt-in `pytest -q -s tests/p5/benchmarks/test_report_capacity.py`; focused existing `tests/p5/diagrams/test_c2_consumers.py` plus amended-store regressions.
- DoD: C4 schemas/algorithms/fonts/package/profile compatibility and consumer fixtures reviewed, D-M5-01 independent review accepted and all required regressions passed with proof of unchanged M4 concrete storage and C0/C2 contracts; all measured limits reported. **B1 records C4 implementation/schema/consumer conformance to the already-frozen design, exact validated dependency/profile pins and measured safe effective settings before B2; passing B1 automatically releases B2 without another owner approval.** Feasibility/security failures block progression; no claimed full PDF/report/storage delivery from B1 alone.

**B2 — Complete PDF renderer, immutable persistence and history**

- Prerequisites: B1 acceptance passed, including independent shared-port review and all required regressions proving unchanged M4 concrete storage/C0/C2; C4 implementation/fixtures conform to the frozen design; exact validated dependency/profile pins and measured effective settings are recorded; real isolated PG/local storage available. **Proceed automatically; no further owner planning or batch authorization.**
- Files: B2 rows, complete pdf/render_runtime, report schema/migration/store/repository; no new M1 scheduling/public API.
- Inputs/outputs: committed prepared snapshots → bounded validated deterministic PDF/spec manifests, SEALED_VERIFIED candidates and independently readable accepted generations through actual M1 PublicationUnitOfWork test harness.
- Acceptance: all-four vectors with deterministic compact packing/full-page fallback/tiling, readable pagination/no clipped diagram labels, arrows, legends, captions or warning text; full PDF parse/security/metadata/font checks; exact two-member sealing; PG scope/identity/immutability constraints, separate staging/publication connections and guard poison; history/read pins, no accepted deletion, failure/resumable cleanup and first-snapshot races.
- Tests: `pytest -q tests/p5/reports/test_pdf.py tests/p5/reports/test_storage.py tests/p5/reports/test_publication.py tests/p5/reports/test_recovery.py` with explicit isolated PG env; render all pages via `pdftoppm` and inspect them, plus `pdfinfo`/`pdftotext` required labels/page counts. Exercise actual adapters, not SQLite or fake seal claims.
- DoD: migration tested fresh/existing/protected downgrade; duplicate attempts/hash corruption/restart/concurrent read/GC are correct; identical all-four prepared values/page-packing policy produce identical PDF bytes under pinned runtime; no READY/read without committed acceptance, no public report endpoints claimed yet. Production qualification stays explicit. Passing B2 automatically releases B3; failed acceptance blocks progression until corrected within scope, not a new planning cycle.

**B3 — M1/M4 integration, retrieval, retry and recovery**

- Prerequisites: B2 complete and C0/C2/C4 fixtures frozen; actual four M4 accepted references/store, PostgreSQL and existing RabbitMQ/Celery test infrastructure; explicit safe configuration of the isolated integration environment, not production activation or a new owner batch approval. Proceed automatically after B2 acceptance.
- Files: B3 inventory/composition/API/settings/tests; do not edit worker lifecycle/retry code or add Express/UI.
- Inputs/outputs: real M1 report context→M4 exact reads→prepared/validated PDF→sealed/staged→M1 guarded acceptance→authorized report metadata/history/download. Reuse existing M1 retry route and retirement port.
- Acceptance: zero report work before four valid same-version committed diagram refs, one automatic operation on concurrent/replayed fourth-ready signals; exact historical refs not aliases; report failure leaves all upstream outputs; worker kill/restart before/after seal and M1 commit; stale fences never accept; report retries preserve snapshot/fingerprint; all four PDF embeddings trace to the exact pinned receipts even after newer diagram publication; omit/duplicate/substitute/mutate any one input and acceptance fails. Physical prerequisite loss is typed and not polled/rebuilt; all read/retry roles, binary headers/digest/abort and cursor drift/expiry correct. No report-specific job or “generate” button/API introduced.
- Tests: `pytest -q tests/p5/reports tests/p5/integration/test_report_pipeline.py`; `pytest -q tests/p5/delivery tests/p5/diagrams tests/p5/knowledge/test_contracts.py tests/p5/knowledge/test_c1_consumers.py tests/p5/knowledge/test_c3_consumers.py`; full `pytest -q tests/p1 tests/p2 tests/p3 tests/p4 tests/p5` in declared environments, classify carried frozen failures rather than change them. Existing frontend `npm test`/`npm run build` are regressions only if shared composition affects them, not M5 frontend implementation.
- DoD: `black --check backend tests`, `ruff check backend tests`, `mypy backend`, `pip check`, sole Alembic head, `git diff --check`; real production-path integration and complete PDF visual review evidence; independent final code/C4 conformance review and evidence-backed completion report. Final implementation freeze/production acceptance is distinct from this already-authorized batch sequence; no recurring batch approval is required. Skipped deployment/Windows proofs remain named, not passed or silently removed.

##### 15.6 M6 — Platform Transport and Unified Frontend

**Historical phase-baseline sketch; detailed current proposal is §7D (2026-11-24).** §7D governs the exact21-route C5 inventory, three batches, file list and gates. In particular, the sketch's conditional internal metadata/preparation modules and new layout worker are not part of the proposed M6 scope; actual M4 components and existing platform reads are reused. This note preserves the original sketch without treating its unimplemented DTO/path suggestions as existing contracts.

**Purpose/scope:** minimal approved Express transport and repository-centered React workspace, search/knowledge/evidence, four diagram availability and report history/download. Separately approved durable preparation bridge may compose existing ingestion/analysis calls. Excludes graph/search business logic in Express, JWT redesign and duplicate dashboards.

**Dependencies/consumers:** C0–C5 as applicable; existing authentication/project RBAC/API client; Python output APIs. MongoDB has no P5 analysis/output authority; only optional platform preparation linkage is stored there. Browser state and user presentation settings do not become canonical architecture.

**Interfaces/API:** existing gateway with new bounded P5 route/controller methods and binary relay, domain React services/hooks/contract validators. Exact-version routes in Section 10 and external contracts in Section 17. **Lifecycle/background:** UI observes server state; requests cancel on scope change, terminal polling stops; optional preparation continuation is server durable, not browser lifetime. **Failure/security/metrics:** all status distinctions retained, 403 does not clear auth, no internal secret leaks, accessible keyboard/focus and safe downloads; UI request/poll/layout durations and safe correlation IDs.
